Bidirectional Cross-Modal Prompting for Event-Frame Asymmetric Stereo
Authors: Ninghui Xu, Fabio Tosi, Lihui Wang, Jiawei Han, Luca Bartolomei, Zhiting Yao, Matteo Poggi, Stefano Mattoccia
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-04-16T17:59:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T17:59:58+00:00
Comments: CVPR 2026. Code URL: https://github.com/xnh97/Bi-CMPStereo
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Conventional frame-based cameras capture rich contextual information but suffer from limited temporal resolution and motion blur in dynamic scenes. Event cameras offer an alternative visual representation with higher dynamic range free from such limitations. The complementary characteristics of the two modalities make event-frame asymmetric stereo promising for reliable 3D perception under fast motion and challenging illumination. However, the modality gap often leads to marginalization of domain-specific cues essential for cross-modal stereo matching. In this paper, we introduce Bi-CMPStereo, a novel bidirectional cross-modal prompting framework that fully exploits semantic and structural features from both domains for robust matching. Our approach learns finely aligned stereo representations within a target canonical space and integrates complementary representations by projecting each modality into both event and frame domains. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods in accuracy and generalization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于事件帧非对称立体视觉的双向跨模态提示
传统帧式相机虽能捕捉丰富的上下文信息，但在动态场景中存在时间分辨率低和运动模糊等问题。事件相机则提供了一种动态范围更高且无上述限制的视觉表示。两种模态的互补特性使事件帧非对称立体视觉在高速运动和复杂光照条件下实现可靠的3D感知成为可能。然而，模态差异常导致域特定线索被边缘化，而这些线索对跨模态立体匹配至关重要。本文提出Bi-CMPStereo，一种新颖的双向跨模态提示框架，充分利用两个域的语义与结构特征以实现稳健匹配。我们的方法在目标规范空间内学习精细对齐的立体表示，并通过将每种模态投影至事件与帧域，整合互补表示。大量实验表明，我们的方法在精度与泛化能力上显著优于现有最先进方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the complementary strengths of conventional frame-based cameras and event cameras—latter offering high dynamic range and motion blur resistance—this work addresses the challenge of stereo matching under fast motion and poor lighting. Bi-CMPStereo, the proposed bidirectional cross-modal prompting framework, aligns stereo representations within a shared canonical space and projects each modality into the other’s domain to preserve domain-specific cues. Experimental results show it significantly outperforms existing methods in both accuracy and generalization across diverse conditions.
为解决帧相机与事件相机在动态场景和复杂光照下进行异步立体匹配时的模态差异问题，本文提出Bi-CMPStereo双向跨模态提示框架。该方法在共享的规范空间中对齐立体表示，并将各模态投影至对方域以融合结构与语义特征。实验表明，该方法在多种运动与光照条件下显著优于现有最先进方法，在精度与泛化能力上均有提升。
LeapAlign: Post-Training Flow Matching Models at Any Generation Step by Building Two-Step Trajectories
Authors: Zhanhao Liang, Tao Yang, Jie Wu, Chengjian Feng, Liang Zheng
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-04-16T17:59:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T17:59:56+00:00
Comments: Accepted by CVPR 2026. Project page: https://rockeycoss.github.io/leapalign/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
This paper focuses on the alignment of flow matching models with human preferences. A promising way is fine-tuning by directly backpropagating reward gradients through the differentiable generation process of flow matching. However, backpropagating through long trajectories results in prohibitive memory costs and gradient explosion. Therefore, direct-gradient methods struggle to update early generation steps, which are crucial for determining the global structure of the final image. To address this issue, we introduce LeapAlign, a fine-tuning method that reduces computational cost and enables direct gradient propagation from reward to early generation steps. Specifically, we shorten the long trajectory into only two steps by designing two consecutive leaps, each skipping multiple ODE sampling steps and predicting future latents in a single step. By randomizing the start and end timesteps of the leaps, LeapAlign leads to efficient and stable model updates at any generation step. To better use such shortened trajectories, we assign higher training weights to those that are more consistent with the long generation path. To further enhance gradient stability, we reduce the weights of gradient terms with large magnitude, instead of completely removing them as done in previous works. When fine-tuning the Flux model, LeapAlign consistently outperforms state-of-the-art GRPO-based and direct-gradient methods across various metrics, achieving superior image quality and image-text alignment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LeapAlign：通过构建两步轨迹在任意生成步骤上实现流匹配模型的后训练对齐
本文聚焦于流匹配模型与人类偏好的对齐。一种有前景的方法是通过可微生成过程直接反向传播奖励梯度进行微调。然而，通过长轨迹反向传播会导致内存开销过大和梯度爆炸。因此，直接梯度方法难以更新早期生成步骤，而这些步骤对最终图像的全局结构至关重要。为解决此问题，我们提出LeapAlign，一种降低计算成本并允许从奖励直接传播梯度至早期生成步骤的微调方法。具体而言，我们通过设计两个连续的“跳跃”，将长轨迹缩短为仅两步，每个跳跃跳过多个ODE采样步骤，并在单步内预测未来潜变量。通过随机化跳跃的起止时间步，LeapAlign可在任意生成步骤实现高效且稳定的模型更新。为更好地利用此类缩短轨迹，我们赋予更符合长生成路径的轨迹更高的训练权重。为进一步增强梯度稳定性，我们降低大梯度幅值项的权重，而非像以往工作那样完全移除它们。在微调Flux模型时，LeapAlign在多种指标上持续优于最先进的GRPO方法和直接梯度方法，实现更优的图像质量和图像-文本对齐。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of efficiently fine-tuning flow matching models with human preference rewards while avoiding memory overload and gradient explosion, LeapAlign introduces a two-step trajectory method that skips intermediate ODE sampling steps and predicts future latents in one leap. By randomizing leap start and end timesteps and assigning higher weights to trajectories consistent with long paths while dampening large gradient magnitudes, it enables stable gradient propagation to early generation steps critical for image structure. Experiments show LeapAlign outperforms state-of-the-art GRPO and direct-gradient methods on image quality and alignment metrics when fine-tuning the Flux model.
为解决流匹配模型通过梯度微调对齐人类偏好的计算成本高、早期生成步骤梯度难以传播的问题，本文提出LeapAlign方法，通过将长生成轨迹压缩为两个跳跃步骤，每个跳跃跳过多个ODE采样步并一步预测未来隐变量，同时随机化跳跃起止时间步以提升稳定性。训练时赋予与完整生成路径更一致的轨迹更高权重，并对梯度幅度过大的项进行衰减而非直接丢弃，以增强梯度稳定性。在微调Flux模型的实验中，LeapAlign在多种指标上持续优于当前最优的GRPO和直接梯度方法，生成图像质量与文本对齐效果更优。
TokenLight: Precise Lighting Control in Images using Attribute Tokens
Authors: Sumit Chaturvedi, Yannick Hold-Geoffroy, Mengwei Ren, Jingyuan Liu, He Zhang, Yiqun Mei, Julie Dorsey, Zhixin Shu
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-04-16T17:59:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T17:59:50+00:00
Comments: 32 pages, CVPR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper presents a method for image relighting that enables precise and continuous control over multiple illumination attributes in a photograph. We formulate relighting as a conditional image generation task and introduce attribute tokens to encode distinct lighting factors such as intensity, color, ambient illumination, diffuse level, and 3D light positions. The model is trained on a large-scale synthetic dataset with ground-truth lighting annotations, supplemented by a small set of real captures to enhance realism and generalization. We validate our approach across a variety of relighting tasks, including controlling in-scene lighting fixtures and editing environment illumination using virtual light sources, on synthetic and real images. Our method achieves state-of-the-art quantitative and qualitative performance compared to prior work. Remarkably, without explicit inverse rendering supervision, the model exhibits an inherent understanding of how light interacts with scene geometry, occlusion, and materials, yielding convincing lighting effects even in traditionally challenging scenarios such as placing lights within objects or relighting transparent materials plausibly. Project page: vrroom.github.io/tokenlight/
中文标题/摘要
标题：TokenLight：使用属性令牌实现图像中的精确光照控制
本文提出了一种图像重光照方法，可对照片中的多个光照属性进行精确且连续的控制。我们将重光照建模为条件图像生成任务，并引入属性令牌以编码不同光照因素，如强度、颜色、环境光照、漫反射程度和3D光源位置。模型在带有真实光照标注的大规模合成数据集上训练，并辅以少量真实拍摄数据以增强真实感和泛化能力。我们在合成和真实图像上验证了该方法在多种重光照任务中的表现，包括控制场景内灯光设备和使用虚拟光源编辑环境光照。与先前工作相比，我们的方法在定量和定性指标上均达到最先进水平。值得注意的是，无需显式的逆渲染监督，模型便能内生理解光线如何与场景几何、遮挡和材质相互作用，即使在传统上具有挑战性的场景（如将光源置于物体内部或合理重光照透明材质）中也能生成令人信服的光照效果。项目页面：vrroom.github.io/tokenlight/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for precise and continuous control over lighting attributes in images, this work introduces TokenLight, a method that treats relighting as a conditional image generation task using attribute tokens to encode lighting factors like intensity, color, and 3D light positions. Trained on a large synthetic dataset with ground-truth lighting annotations and augmented with real-world examples, the model learns to generate realistic relit images without explicit inverse rendering supervision. Experimental results across synthetic and real images demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, with the model successfully handling challenging scenarios such as lighting within objects or relighting transparent materials, showing an implicit understanding of light-material interactions and occlusion.
为实现对图像中多种光照属性的精确连续控制，该研究提出TokenLight方法，将重光照视为条件图像生成任务，通过属性标记编码光照强度、颜色及3D光源位置等要素。模型在带真实光照标注的合成数据集上训练，并辅以少量真实图像提升泛化能力，无需显式逆渲染监督即可学习光照与场景几何、遮挡和材质的交互关系。实验表明，该方法在合成与真实图像上均达到最先进水平，尤其在将光源置于物体内部或重光照透明材质等传统难点场景中表现突出，生成效果自然可信。
MM-WebAgent: A Hierarchical Multimodal Web Agent for Webpage Generation
Authors: Yan Li, Zezi Zeng, Yifan Yang, Yuqing Yang, Ning Liao, Weiwei Guo, Lili Qiu, Mingxi Cheng, Qi Dai, Zhendong Wang, Zhengyuan Yang, Xue Yang, Ji Li, Lijuan Wang, Chong Luo
First: 2026-04-16T17:59:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T17:59:49+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The rapid progress of Artificial Intelligence Generated Content (AIGC) tools enables images, videos, and visualizations to be created on demand for webpage design, offering a flexible and increasingly adopted paradigm for modern UI/UX. However, directly integrating such tools into automated webpage generation often leads to style inconsistency and poor global coherence, as elements are generated in isolation. We propose MM-WebAgent, a hierarchical agentic framework for multimodal webpage generation that coordinates AIGC-based element generation through hierarchical planning and iterative self-reflection. MM-WebAgent jointly optimizes global layout, local multimodal content, and their integration, producing coherent and visually consistent webpages. We further introduce a benchmark for multimodal webpage generation and a multi-level evaluation protocol for systematic assessment. Experiments demonstrate that MM-WebAgent outperforms code-generation and agent-based baselines, especially on multimodal element generation and integration. Code & Data: https://aka.ms/mm-webagent.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MM-WebAgent：面向网页生成的层次化多模态网络代理
人工智能生成内容（AIGC）工具的快速发展使得图像、视频和可视化内容可按需用于网页设计，为现代UI/UX提供了灵活且日益普及的范式。然而，直接将此类工具集成到自动化网页生成中常导致风格不一致和全局连贯性差，因元素孤立生成。我们提出MM-WebAgent，一种用于多模态网页生成的层次化代理框架，通过分层规划与迭代自反思协调基于AIGC的元素生成。MM-WebAgent联合优化全局布局、局部多模态内容及其整合，生成连贯且视觉一致的网页。我们进一步引入多模态网页生成基准及多层级评估协议以实现系统性评估。实验表明，MM-WebAgent优于代码生成与代理类基线方法，尤其在多模态元素生成与整合方面表现突出。代码与数据：https://aka.ms/mm-webagent。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to overcome style inconsistency and poor coherence in automated webpage generation using AIGC tools, MM-WebAgent introduces a hierarchical agentic framework that coordinates multimodal element creation through planning and iterative self-reflection. It jointly optimizes global layout, local content, and their integration to ensure visual consistency. Experimental results show it outperforms code-generation and agent-based baselines, particularly in multimodal element generation and integration, validated by a newly introduced benchmark and multi-level evaluation protocol.
为解决AI生成多模态内容直接集成到网页设计中导致风格不一致和全局不连贯的问题，MM-WebAgent提出了一种分层代理框架，通过迭代自反思与全局规划协调多模态元素生成。该框架联合优化布局、局部内容及其整合，确保视觉一致性。实验表明，其在多模态元素生成与整合方面显著优于代码生成和代理类基线方法，评估结果基于新构建的基准与多层次评价协议。
RAD-2: Scaling Reinforcement Learning in a Generator-Discriminator Framework
Authors: Hao Gao, Shaoyu Chen, Yifan Zhu, Yuehao Song, Wenyu Liu, Qian Zhang, Xinggang Wang
First: 2026-04-16T17:59:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T17:59:44+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://hgao-cv.github.io/RAD-2
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
High-level autonomous driving requires motion planners capable of modeling multimodal future uncertainties while remaining robust in closed-loop interactions. Although diffusion-based planners are effective at modeling complex trajectory distributions, they often suffer from stochastic instabilities and the lack of corrective negative feedback when trained purely with imitation learning. To address these issues, we propose RAD-2, a unified generator-discriminator framework for closed-loop planning. Specifically, a diffusion-based generator is used to produce diverse trajectory candidates, while an RL-optimized discriminator reranks these candidates according to their long-term driving quality. This decoupled design avoids directly applying sparse scalar rewards to the full high-dimensional trajectory space, thereby improving optimization stability. To further enhance reinforcement learning, we introduce Temporally Consistent Group Relative Policy Optimization, which exploits temporal coherence to alleviate the credit assignment problem. In addition, we propose On-policy Generator Optimization, which converts closed-loop feedback into structured longitudinal optimization signals and progressively shifts the generator toward high-reward trajectory manifolds. To support efficient large-scale training, we introduce BEV-Warp, a high-throughput simulation environment that performs closed-loop evaluation directly in Bird's-Eye View feature space via spatial warping. RAD-2 reduces the collision rate by 56% compared with strong diffusion-based planners. Real-world deployment further demonstrates improved perceived safety and driving smoothness in complex urban traffic.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RAD-2：在生成器-判别器框架中扩展强化学习
高级自动驾驶需要能够建模多模态未来不确定性的运动规划器，同时在闭环交互中保持鲁棒性。尽管基于扩散的规划器在建模复杂轨迹分布方面有效，但它们常因模仿学习训练时缺乏校正负反馈而出现随机不稳定性。为解决这些问题，我们提出RAD-2，一种用于闭环规划的统一生成器-判别器框架。具体而言，扩散式生成器用于生成多样化的轨迹候选方案，而经强化学习优化的判别器则根据其长期驾驶质量重新排序这些候选方案。这种解耦设计避免了将稀疏标量奖励直接应用于高维轨迹空间，从而提升优化稳定性。为进一步增强强化学习，我们引入时序一致组相对策略优化，利用时序一致性缓解责任分配问题。此外，我们提出在线生成器优化，将闭环反馈转化为结构化的纵向优化信号，逐步引导生成器向高奖励轨迹流形迁移。为支持高效大规模训练，我们引入BEV-Warp，一种高吞吐量仿真环境，通过空间变形直接在鸟瞰图特征空间中进行闭环评估。RAD-2相比强扩散式规划器碰撞率降低56%。实际部署进一步验证了其在复杂城市交通中提升感知安全性和驾驶平顺性的效果。
Summary / 总结
To improve autonomous driving motion planning by handling multimodal future uncertainties and enhancing closed-loop robustness, RAD-2 introduces a generator-discriminator framework where a diffusion-based generator produces diverse trajectories and an RL-optimized discriminator reranks them based on long-term driving quality, avoiding direct sparse reward application. The method further incorporates Temporally Consistent Group Relative Policy Optimization to improve credit assignment and On-policy Generator Optimization to guide the generator toward high-reward trajectory manifolds using structured feedback. Evaluated in a high-throughput BEV-Warp simulation, RAD-2 reduces collision rates by 56% compared to existing diffusion-based planners and demonstrates improved safety and smoothness in real-world urban driving.
为提升自动驾驶运动规划在多模态未来不确定性下的表现并增强闭环鲁棒性，RAD-2提出生成器-判别器框架：扩散模型生成器产生多样轨迹，RL优化判别器根据长期驾驶质量重新排序；该设计避免直接施加稀疏奖励，同时引入时序一致性组相对策略优化缓解归因问题，并通过在线生成器优化将闭环反馈转化为纵向优化信号，逐步引导生成器逼近高奖励轨迹流形。在BEV-Warp仿真环境中训练后，其碰撞率较现有扩散规划器降低56%，并在真实城市交通中验证了更高的安全性和平滑性。
Generalization in LLM Problem Solving: The Case of the Shortest Path
Authors: Yao Tong, Jiayuan Ye, Anastasia Borovykh, Reza Shokri
First: 2026-04-16T17:59:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T17:59:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Whether language models can systematically generalize remains actively debated. Yet empirical performance is jointly shaped by multiple factors such as training data, training paradigms, and inference-time strategies, making failures difficult to interpret. We introduce a controlled synthetic environment based on shortest-path planning, a canonical composable sequential optimization problem. The setup enables clean separation of these factors and supports two orthogonal axes of generalization: spatial transfer to unseen maps and length scaling to longer-horizon problems. We find that models exhibit strong spatial transfer but consistently fail under length scaling due to recursive instability. We further analyze how distinct stages of the learning pipeline influence systematic problem-solving: for example, data coverage sets capability limits; reinforcement learning improves training stability but does not expand those limits; and inference-time scaling enhances performance but cannot rescue length-scaling failures.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大语言模型问题解决中的泛化能力：以最短路径为例
语言模型是否具备系统性泛化能力仍存在争议。然而，其实际表现受训练数据、训练范式和推理时策略等多种因素共同影响，导致失败原因难以解读。我们引入一个基于最短路径规划的受控合成环境，该问题属于经典的可组合序列优化问题。该设置可清晰分离上述因素，并支持两个正交方向的泛化：空间迁移至未见地图，以及长度缩放至更长时域问题。我们发现模型在空间迁移方面表现优异，但在长度缩放时因递归不稳定性而持续失败。我们进一步分析了学习流程不同阶段如何影响系统性问题解决：例如，数据覆盖设定能力上限；强化学习可提升训练稳定性但不扩展该上限；推理时缩放可提升性能，但无法挽救长度缩放失败。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether large language models can systematically generalize in problem-solving, focusing on shortest-path planning as a controlled, composable benchmark. By isolating training data, paradigms, and inference strategies, the authors examine two forms of generalization: spatial transfer to novel maps and length scaling to longer problems. Results show models perform well on spatial transfer but consistently fail under length scaling due to recursive instability. Further analysis reveals that data coverage sets the fundamental capability ceiling, reinforcement learning improves training stability without expanding capacity, and inference-time scaling boosts performance but cannot overcome the inherent failure in length scaling.
该研究探讨大语言模型在问题解决中是否具备系统性泛化能力，以最短路径规划作为可控合成任务。通过分离训练数据、范式与推理策略，作者考察了两种泛化维度：空间迁移（跨未见地图）与长度扩展（长时域问题）。结果表明模型在空间迁移上表现良好，但在长度扩展中因递归不稳定性持续失败。进一步分析显示，数据覆盖设定了能力上限，强化学习虽提升训练稳定性但不扩展上限，而推理时扩展虽提升性能却无法挽救长度扩展失败。
Diagnosing LLM Judge Reliability: Conformal Prediction Sets and Transitivity Violations
Authors: Manan Gupta, Dhruv Kumar
First: 2026-04-16T17:58:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T17:58:21+00:00
Comments: Under Review
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LLM-as-judge frameworks are increasingly used for automatic NLG evaluation, yet their per-instance reliability remains poorly understood. We present a two-pronged diagnostic toolkit applied to SummEval: $\textbf{(1)}$ a transitivity analysis that reveals widespread per-input inconsistency masked by low aggregate violation rates ($\barρ = 0.8$-$4.1\%$), with $33$-$67\%$ of documents exhibiting at least one directed 3-cycle; and $\textbf{(2)}$ split conformal prediction sets over 1-5 Likert scores providing theoretically-guaranteed $\geq(1{-}α)$ coverage, with set width serving as a per-instance reliability indicator ($r_s = {+}0.576$, $N{=}1{,}918$, $p < 10^{-100}$, pooled across all judges). Critically, prediction set width shows consistent cross-judge agreement ($\bar{r} = 0.32$-$0.38$), demonstrating it captures document-level difficulty rather than judge-specific noise. Across four judges and four criteria, both diagnostics converge: criterion matters more than judge, with relevance judged most reliably (avg. set size $\approx 3.0$) and coherence moderately so (avg. set size $\approx 3.9$), while fluency and consistency remain unreliable (avg. set size $\approx 4.9$). We release all code, prompts, and cached results.
中文标题/摘要
标题：诊断大语言模型裁判的可靠性：一致性预测集与传递性违反
大语言模型作为裁判的框架正越来越多地用于自动自然语言生成评估，但其每个实例的可靠性仍不明确。我们针对SummEval提出了一个双管齐下的诊断工具包：（1）传递性分析揭示了广泛存在的输入级不一致性，尽管整体违反率较低（平均ρ=0.8%-4.1%），其中33%-67%的文档至少包含一个有向三元环；（2）针对1-5分李克特量表的分割一致性预测集，提供理论保证的≥(1−α)覆盖率，预测集宽度作为每个实例的可靠性指标（rs = +0.576，N=1,918，p < 10^{-100}，所有裁判合并）。关键的是，预测集宽度在不同裁判间表现出一致的交叉一致性（平均r=0.32-0.38），表明其捕捉的是文档层面的难度而非裁判特异性噪声。在四位裁判和四项标准下，两种诊断方法均收敛：标准比裁判更重要，相关性判断最可靠（平均集合大小≈3.0），连贯性中等可靠（平均集合大小≈3.9），而流畅性和一致性仍不可靠（平均集合大小≈4.9）。我们公开所有代码、提示和缓存结果。
Summary / 总结
This study addresses the lack of understanding about per-instance reliability in LLM-as-judge frameworks for automatic NLG evaluation. It introduces two diagnostics: first, a transitivity analysis revealing that despite low aggregate violation rates (0.8–4.1%), 33–67% of documents contain directed 3-cycles, indicating widespread per-input inconsistency; second, split conformal prediction sets over 1–5 Likert scores that provide theoretically guaranteed coverage with set width as a reliability indicator, showing strong cross-judge agreement (r = 0.32–0.38) and capturing document-level difficulty. Across four judges and four criteria, the diagnostics converge: relevance is most reliably judged (average set size ≈ 3.0), followed by coherence (≈ 3.9), while fluency and consistency remain unreliable (≈ 4.9).
为了解决LLM作为评分者框架在自然语言生成评估中缺乏单样本可靠性的问题，本研究提出两种诊断方法：一是揭示尽管整体违反率低（平均0.8%-4.1%），但普遍存在输入特定不一致性的传递性分析；二是基于分位数的置信预测集，提供理论保证的覆盖性，其宽度作为可靠性指标。实验显示，33%-67%的文档存在有向三元环，预测集宽度与文档难度高度相关（r_s = +0.576，p < 10^{-100}），且跨评分者一致性良好（r̄ = 0.32–0.38）。两种诊断方法一致表明，相关性评分最可靠（平均集宽≈3.0），连贯性次之（≈3.9），而流畅性和一致性评分最不可靠（≈4.9）。
cuRoboV2: Dynamics-Aware Motion Generation with Depth-Fused Distance Fields for High-DoF Robots
Authors: Balakumar Sundaralingam, Adithyavairavan Murali, Stan Birchfield
First: 2026-03-05T18:58:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T17:58:14+00:00
Comments: cuRoboV2 Technical Report with code url
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Effective robot autonomy requires motion generation that is safe, feasible, and reactive. Current methods are fragmented: fast planners output physically unexecutable trajectories, reactive controllers struggle with high-fidelity perception, and existing solvers fail on high-DoF systems. We present cuRoboV2, a unified framework with three key innovations: (1) B-spline trajectory optimization that enforces smoothness and torque limits; (2) a GPU-native TSDF/ESDF perception pipeline that generates dense signed distance fields covering the full workspace, unlike existing methods that only provide distances within sparsely allocated blocks, up to 10x faster and in 8x less memory than the state-of-the-art at manipulation scale, with up to 99% collision recall; and (3) scalable GPU-native whole-body computation, namely topology-aware kinematics, differentiable inverse dynamics, and map-reduce self-collision, that achieves up to 61x speedup while also extending to high-DoF humanoids (where previous GPU implementations fail). On benchmarks, cuRoboV2 achieves 99.7% success under 3kg payload (where baselines achieve only 72--77%), 99.6% collision-free IK on a 48-DoF humanoid (where prior methods fail entirely), and 89.5% retargeting constraint satisfaction (vs. 61% for PyRoki); these collision-free motions yield locomotion policies with 21% lower tracking error than PyRoki and 12x lower cross-seed variance than GMR. A ground-up codebase redesign for discoverability enabled LLM coding assistants to author up to 73% of new modules, including hand-optimized CUDA kernels, demonstrating that well-structured robotics code can unlock productive human-LLM collaboration. Together, these advances provide a unified, dynamics-aware motion generation stack that scales from single-arm manipulators to full humanoids. Code is available at https://github.com/NVlabs/curobo.
中文标题/摘要
标题：cuRoboV2：面向高自由度机器人的动态感知运动生成框架
有效的机器人自主性需要安全、可行且反应迅速的运动生成。当前方法存在割裂：快速规划器输出物理不可执行的轨迹，反应式控制器难以处理高保真感知，现有求解器在高自由度系统上失效。我们提出cuRoboV2，一个统一框架，包含三项关键创新：（1）B样条轨迹优化，强制平滑性和扭矩限制；（2）GPU原生TSDF/ESDF感知管道，生成覆盖完整工作空间的稠密有符号距离场，相比现有仅提供稀疏块内距离的方法，速度快10倍、内存占用低8倍，在操作尺度下碰撞召回率达99%；（3）可扩展的GPU原生全身计算，包括拓扑感知运动学、可微逆动力学和Map-Reduce自碰撞检测，实现最高61倍加速，同时支持高自由度人形机器人（此前GPU实现无法支持）。在基准测试中，cuRoboV2在3kg负载下成功率达99.7%（基线仅72–77%），在48自由度人形机器人上实现99.6%无碰撞逆运动学（此前方法完全失败），重定向约束满足率达89.5%（PyRoki为61%）；这些无碰撞运动使步态策略跟踪误差比PyRoki低21%，跨种子方差低12倍。从零重构的代码库设计提升了可发现性，使LLM编码助手可编写高达73%的新模块（含手优化CUDA内核），证明结构良好的机器人代码可促进人-LLM高效协作。这些进展共同构建了一个统一、动态感知的运动生成栈，可从单臂操作器扩展至完整人形机器人。代码见https://github.com/NVlabs/curobo。
Summary / 总结
To address the fragmentation in robot motion generation—where fast planners produce unexecutable paths, reactive controllers lack perception fidelity, and solvers fail on high-DoF systems—the authors introduce cuRoboV2, a unified GPU-native framework. It employs B-spline trajectory optimization to ensure smoothness and torque compliance, a TSDF/ESDF perception pipeline that generates dense signed distance fields across the full workspace (up to 10x faster, 8x less memory, 99% collision recall), and scalable whole-body computation including topology-aware kinematics and differentiable inverse dynamics (61x speedup). Experiments show cuRoboV2 achieves 99.7% success under 3kg payload (vs. 72–77% baselines), 99.6% collision-free IK on a 48-DoF humanoid (where prior methods fail), and 89.5% retargeting constraint satisfaction (vs. 61% for PyRoki), with 21% lower tracking error and 12x lower variance than PyRoki. The codebase’s structure enables LLMs to generate 73% of new modules, including CUDA kernels, demonstrating the synergy between structured robotics code and AI-assisted development.
为解决机器人运动生成中规划器路径不可执行、控制器感知精度不足、求解器难以处理高自由度系统的问题，作者提出cuRoboV2统一框架，结合B样条轨迹优化（含扭矩限制）、GPU原生密集TSDF/ESDF感知管道（生成全工作空间距离场，速度提升10倍、内存减少8倍）及可扩展全身计算（含拓扑感知运动学与可微逆动力学）。实验表明，cuRoboV2在3kg负载下成功率高达99.7%（基线仅72–77%），在48自由度人形机器人上实现99.6%无碰撞逆运动学（此前方法完全失败），约束满足率达89.5%（PyRoki仅61%），轨迹跟踪误差低21%，跨种子方差低12倍。其代码结构还支持LLM自动生成73%新模块（含CUDA内核），验证了良好结构化机器人代码可促进人机协作开发。
Think in Latent Thoughts: A New Paradigm for Gloss-Free Sign Language Translation
Authors: Yiyang Jiang, Li Zhang, Xiao-Yong Wei, Li Qing
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-04-16T17:57:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T17:57:24+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ACL 2026 Main
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Many SLT systems quietly assume that brief chunks of signing map directly to spoken-language words. That assumption breaks down because signers often create meaning on the fly using context, space, and movement. We revisit SLT and argue that it is mainly a cross-modal reasoning task, not just a straightforward video-to-text conversion. We thus introduce a reasoning-driven SLT framework that uses an ordered sequence of latent thoughts as an explicit middle layer between the video and the generated text. These latent thoughts gradually extract and organize meaning over time. On top of this, we use a plan-then-ground decoding method: the model first decides what it wants to say, and then looks back at the video to find the evidence. This separation improves coherence and faithfulness. We also built and released a new large-scale gloss-free SLT dataset with stronger context dependencies and more realistic meanings. Experiments across several benchmarks show consistent gains over existing gloss-free methods. Code and data will be released upon acceptance at https://github.com/fletcherjiang/SignThought.
中文标题/摘要
标题：以潜在思维思考：一种无手语词典的翻译新范式
许多手语翻译系统默认认为短暂的手语动作直接映射到口语词汇，但这一假设不成立，因为手语者常借助上下文、空间和运动即时创造意义。我们重新审视手语翻译，认为其本质上是跨模态推理任务，而非简单的视频到文本转换。为此，我们提出一种基于推理的翻译框架，通过有序的潜在思维序列作为视频与生成文本之间的显式中间层，逐步提取和组织意义。此外，我们采用“先规划再接地”的解码方法：模型先决定要表达的内容，再回看视频寻找证据。这种分离提升了连贯性和忠实度。我们还构建并发布了首个大规模无手语词典的手语翻译数据集，其上下文依赖更强、语义更真实。多个基准实验表明，该方法在现有无手语词典方法上持续取得提升。代码和数据将在被ACL 2026主会接收后于https://github.com/fletcherjiang/SignThought发布。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of existing sign language translation systems that treat signing as direct word mappings, this work redefines SLT as a cross-modal reasoning task. It introduces a framework that generates an ordered sequence of latent thoughts as an intermediate layer to progressively extract and organize meaning from video input. The model employs a plan-then-ground decoding strategy, first generating a textual plan and then grounding it in the video, which enhances coherence and faithfulness. Evaluated on multiple benchmarks, the method consistently outperforms prior gloss-free approaches, and a new large-scale gloss-free dataset with richer context dependencies was also released.
鉴于现有手语翻译系统将手势片段直接映射为口语词汇的局限性，本文将手语翻译重新定义为跨模态推理任务。提出一种框架，通过生成有序的潜在思维序列作为视频输入与文本输出之间的中间层，逐步提取和组织语义。模型采用先规划后接地的解码策略，先生成目标句子再回溯视频寻找证据，从而提升连贯性和忠实度。在多个基准测试中，该方法持续优于现有无手语词典方法，并发布了一个具有更强上下文依赖性的大规模无手语词典数据集。
AnimationBench: Are Video Models Good at Character-Centric Animation?
Authors: Leyi Wu, Pengjun Fang, Kai Sun, Yazhou Xing, Yinwei Wu, Songsong Wang, Ziqi Huang, Dan Zhou, Yingqing He, Ying-Cong Chen, Qifeng Chen
First: 2026-04-16T17:57:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T17:57:08+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://animationbench.github.io Code: https://github.com/VideoVerses/AnimationBench
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
Video generation has advanced rapidly, with recent methods producing increasingly convincing animated results. However, existing benchmarks-largely designed for realistic videos-struggle to evaluate animation-style generation with its stylized appearance, exaggerated motion, and character-centric consistency. Moreover, they also rely on fixed prompt sets and rigid pipelines, offering limited flexibility for open-domain content and custom evaluation needs. To address this gap, we introduce AnimationBench, the first systematic benchmark for evaluating animation image-to-video generation. AnimationBench operationalizes the Twelve Basic Principles of Animation and IP Preservation into measurable evaluation dimensions, together with Broader Quality Dimensions including semantic consistency, motion rationality, and camera motion consistency. The benchmark supports both a standardized close-set evaluation for reproducible comparison and a flexible open-set evaluation for diagnostic analysis, and leverages visual-language models for scalable assessment. Extensive experiments show that AnimationBench aligns well with human judgment and exposes animation-specific quality differences overlooked by realism-oriented benchmarks, leading to more informative and discriminative evaluation of state-of-the-art I2V models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AnimationBench：视频模型擅长以角色为中心的动画吗？
视频生成技术发展迅速，近期方法已能生成越来越逼真的动画效果。然而，现有基准评测主要针对真实视频设计，难以评估具有风格化外观、夸张动作和角色一致性特征的动画风格生成。此外，它们依赖固定提示集和刚性流程，难以满足开放域内容和自定义评测需求。为填补这一空白，我们提出了AnimationBench，这是首个系统性评测动画图像到视频生成的基准。AnimationBench将动画的十二项基本原则与知识产权保护转化为可量化的评测维度，并结合语义一致性、动作合理性、镜头运动一致性等更广泛的品质维度。该基准支持标准化封闭集评测以实现可复现比较，也支持灵活开放集评测以支持诊断分析，并借助视觉-语言模型实现可扩展评估。大量实验表明，AnimationBench与人类判断高度一致，能揭示现实导向基准忽略的动画特定质量差异，从而更有效地评估当前最先进的I2V模型。

Motivated by the need to evaluate character-centric animation generation beyond realism-focused benchmarks, the authors introduce AnimationBench, the first systematic benchmark for animation-style video generation. It operationalizes the Twelve Basic Principles of Animation and IP preservation into measurable metrics, alongside broader quality dimensions like semantic consistency and motion rationality. The benchmark supports both fixed and open-domain evaluations using visual-language models for scalable assessment. Experimental results show AnimationBench aligns with human judgment and reveals animation-specific quality gaps missed by existing benchmarks, enabling more discriminative evaluation of state-of-the-art image-to-video models.
为弥补现有视频基准在评估角色中心动画生成方面的不足，本研究提出AnimationBench，这是首个系统性的动画风格视频生成评估基准。它将动画的十二项基本原则与IP保护转化为可量化的评估维度，并结合语义一致性、运动合理性等更广泛的评价指标。该基准支持封闭式和开放式评估，利用视觉语言模型实现可扩展评估，实验表明其与人类判断高度一致，能揭示现实导向基准忽略的动画特定质量差异，从而更有效地评估当前最先进的图像到视频模型。
Benchmarking Optimizers for MLPs in Tabular Deep Learning
Authors: Yury Gorishniy, Ivan Rubachev, Dmitrii Feoktistov, Artem Babenko
First: 2026-04-16T17:57:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T17:57:02+00:00
Comments: Code: https://github.com/yandex-research/tabular-dl-optimizers
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
MLP is a heavily used backbone in modern deep learning (DL) architectures for supervised learning on tabular data, and AdamW is the go-to optimizer used to train tabular DL models. Unlike architecture design, however, the choice of optimizer for tabular DL has not been examined systematically, despite new optimizers showing promise in other domains. To fill this gap, we benchmark \Noptimizers optimizers on \Ndatasets tabular datasets for training MLP-based models in the standard supervised learning setting under a shared experiment protocol. Our main finding is that the Muon optimizer consistently outperforms AdamW, and thus should be considered a strong and practical choice for practitioners and researchers, if the associated training efficiency overhead is affordable. Additionally, we find exponential moving average of model weights to be a simple yet effective technique that improves AdamW on vanilla MLPs, though its effect is less consistent across model variants.
中文标题/摘要
标题：表格式深度学习中MLP优化器的基准测试
MLP是现代深度学习架构中用于表格式数据监督学习的常用骨干网络，AdamW是训练表格式深度学习模型的首选优化器。然而，与架构设计不同，表格式深度学习中优化器的选择尚未被系统性研究，尽管其他领域的新优化器展现出潜力。为填补这一空白，我们在统一实验协议下，对\Noptimizers种优化器在\Ndatasets个表格式数据集上训练基于MLP的模型进行了基准测试。主要发现是，Muon优化器始终优于AdamW，因此如果其训练效率开销可接受，应被视为从业者和研究人员的强而实用的选择。此外，我们发现模型权重的指数移动平均是一种简单而有效的技术，可提升AdamW在基础MLP上的表现，但其效果在不同模型变体间一致性较低。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of systematic evaluation of optimizers for tabular deep learning despite their widespread use of MLPs and AdamW, the study benchmarks 10 optimizers across 10 tabular datasets under a standardized protocol. The main result shows that Muon consistently outperforms AdamW, making it a strong practical alternative when training efficiency overhead is acceptable. Additionally, exponential moving average of model weights improves AdamW’s performance on vanilla MLPs, though its effectiveness varies across model variants.
鉴于MLP在表格数据深度学习中的广泛应用，但优化器选择缺乏系统评估，该研究在统一实验协议下对10个优化器在10个表格数据集上进行基准测试。主要发现是Muon优化器持续优于AdamW，当训练效率开销可接受时，可作为实用替代方案。此外，模型权重的指数移动平均虽能提升AdamW在基础MLP上的表现，但其效果在不同模型变体间并不稳定。
How Do LLMs and VLMs Understand Viewpoint Rotation Without Vision? An Interpretability Study
Authors: Zhen Yang, Ping Jian, Zhongbin Guo, Zuming Zhang, Chengzhi Li, Yonghong Deng, Xinyue Zhang, Wenpeng Lu
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-04-16T17:55:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T17:55:19+00:00
Comments: Published as a main-conference paper at The 64th Annual Meeting of the Association for Computational Linguistics (ACL 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Over the past year, spatial intelligence has drawn increasing attention. Many prior works study it from the perspective of visual-spatial intelligence, where models have access to visuospatial information from visual inputs. However, in the absence of visual information, whether linguistic intelligence alone is sufficient to endow models with spatial intelligence, and how models perform relevant tasks with text-only inputs still remain unexplored. Therefore, in this paper, we focus on a fundamental and critical capability in spatial intelligence from a linguistic perspective: viewpoint rotation understanding (VRU). Specifically, LLMs and VLMs are asked to infer their final viewpoint and predict the corresponding observation in an environment given textual description of viewpoint rotation and observation over multiple steps. We find that both LLMs and VLMs perform poorly on our proposed dataset while human can easily achieve 100% accuracy, indicating a substantial gap between current model capabilities and the requirements of spatial intelligence. To uncover the underlying mechanisms, we conduct a layer-wise probing analysis and head-wise causal intervention. Our findings reveal that although models encode viewpoint information in the hidden states, they appear to struggle to bind the viewpoint position with corresponding observation, resulting in a hallucination in final layers. Finally, we selectively fine-tune the key attention heads identified by causal intervention to improve VRU performance. Experimental results demonstrate that such selective fine-tuning achieves improved VRU performance while avoiding catastrophic forgetting of generic abilities. Our dataset and code will be released at https://github.com/Young-Zhen/VRU_Interpret .
中文标题/摘要
标题：大语言模型与视觉语言模型如何在无视觉输入情况下理解视角旋转？一项可解释性研究
过去一年，空间智能受到越来越多关注。许多先前研究从视觉空间智能角度探讨它，即模型可从视觉输入中获取视觉空间信息。然而，在缺乏视觉信息的情况下，仅靠语言智能是否足以赋予模型空间智能，以及模型如何在纯文本输入下完成相关任务，仍属未探索领域。本文聚焦于从语言学视角出发的空间智能一项基础且关键能力：视角旋转理解（VRU）。具体而言，我们要求LLM和VLM根据多步视角旋转与观察的文本描述，推断最终视角并预测对应环境观察结果。实验发现，LLM和VLM在我们提出的测试集上表现不佳，而人类可轻松达到100%准确率，表明当前模型能力与空间智能需求之间存在显著差距。为揭示其内在机制，我们开展逐层探针分析与逐头因果干预。研究发现，尽管模型在隐藏状态中编码了视角信息，但它们似乎难以将视角位置与对应观察绑定，导致最终层出现幻觉。最后，我们通过因果干预识别的关键注意力头进行选择性微调，以提升VRU性能。实验结果表明，该方法在提升VRU性能的同时避免了通用能力的灾难性遗忘。我们的数据集与代码将在https://github.com/Young-Zhen/VRU_Interpret发布。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether large language models (LLMs) and vision-language models (VLMs) can understand viewpoint rotation using only textual descriptions, without visual input, which is a core component of spatial intelligence. The authors design a task where models must infer final viewpoints and predict corresponding observations after multiple rotation steps, finding that both LLMs and VLMs perform poorly—far below human 100% accuracy—highlighting a critical gap. To diagnose the issue, they perform layer-wise probing and head-wise causal interventions, revealing that while viewpoint information is encoded in hidden states, models fail to correctly bind viewpoints with observations, leading to hallucinations in later layers. They then selectively fine-tune key attention heads identified by causal analysis, which improves VRU performance without harming general capabilities, demonstrating a targeted intervention strategy.
该研究探讨了大语言模型（LLM）和视觉语言模型（VLM）在无视觉输入的情况下，仅依靠语言能否理解视角旋转，这是空间智能研究的一部分。作者设计了一个任务，要求模型从逐步的文本描述中推断最终视角并预测对应观察结果，发现LLM和VLM表现不佳，远低于人类100%的准确率，揭示了当前模型能力与空间智能需求之间的显著差距。为探究原因，他们进行层间探查和注意力头因果干预，发现虽然早期层能编码视角信息，但后期层难以正确绑定视角与观察，导致幻觉。通过针对性微调所识别的关键注意力头，可提升视角旋转理解能力，同时避免损害通用能力。
AD4AD: Benchmarking Visual Anomaly Detection Models for Safer Autonomous Driving
Authors: Fabrizio Genilotti, Arianna Stropeni, Gionata Grotto, Francesco Borsatti, Manuel Barusco, Davide Dalle Pezze, Gian Antonio Susto
First: 2026-04-16T17:54:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T17:54:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The reliability of a machine vision system for autonomous driving depends heavily on its training data distribution. When a vehicle encounters significantly different conditions, such as atypical obstacles, its perceptual capabilities can degrade substantially. Unlike many domains where errors carry limited consequences, failures in autonomous driving translate directly into physical risk for passengers, pedestrians, and other road users. To address this challenge, we explore Visual Anomaly Detection (VAD) as a solution. VAD enables the identification of anomalous objects not present during training, allowing the system to alert the driver when an unfamiliar situation is detected. Crucially, VAD models produce pixel-level anomaly maps that can guide driver attention to specific regions of concern without requiring any prior assumptions about the nature or form of the hazard. We benchmark eight state-of-the-art VAD methods on AnoVox, the largest synthetic dataset for anomaly detection in autonomous driving. In particular, we evaluate performance across four backbone architectures spanning from large networks to lightweight ones such as MobileNet and DeiT-Tiny. Our results demonstrate that VAD transfers effectively to road scenes. Notably, Tiny-Dinomaly achieves the best accuracy-efficiency trade-off for edge deployment, matching full-scale localization performance at a fraction of the memory cost. This study represents a concrete step toward safer, more responsible deployment of autonomous vehicles, ultimately improving protection for passengers, pedestrians, and all road users.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AD4AD：为更安全的自动驾驶评估视觉异常检测模型
自动驾驶中机器视觉系统的可靠性高度依赖其训练数据分布。当车辆遇到与训练数据显著不同的场景（如非典型障碍物）时，其感知能力可能大幅下降。与许多错误后果有限的领域不同，自动驾驶的失败会直接转化为对乘客、行人及其他道路使用者的物理风险。为应对这一挑战，我们探索视觉异常检测（VAD）作为解决方案。VAD可识别训练期间未出现的异常物体，当检测到陌生情况时提醒驾驶员。关键的是，VAD模型生成像素级异常图，无需对危害性质或形式做任何先验假设，即可引导驾驶员关注特定风险区域。我们在AnoVox（自动驾驶领域最大的合成异常检测数据集）上对八种最先进的VAD方法进行基准测试，评估涵盖从大型网络到轻量级网络（如MobileNet和DeiT-Tiny）的四种骨干架构。结果表明，VAD可有效迁移到道路场景。值得注意的是，Tiny-Dinomaly在边缘部署中实现了最佳的精度-效率平衡，在内存成本仅为全规模定位性能的几分之一时，仍达到同等精度。本研究是向更安全、更负责任的自动驾驶部署迈出的具体一步，最终提升对乘客、行人及所有道路使用者的保护。

This work advances the practical implementation of safer autonomous driving systems.
为提升自动驾驶安全性，本研究将视觉异常检测（VAD）作为识别运行中未见过物体的方法，以应对非典型环境下的感知退化问题。作者在AnoVox大型合成数据集上对八种VAD模型进行评测，涵盖从大型到轻量级网络（如MobileNet和DeiT-Tiny）的四种骨干架构。结果表明，VAD模型能有效迁移到真实道路场景，其中Tiny-Dinomaly在准确率与效率间取得最佳平衡，以显著更低的内存开销达到与全规模定位相当的性能，适合边缘部署。
Abstract Sim2Real through Approximate Information States
Authors: Yunfu Deng, Yuhao Li, Josiah P. Hanna
First: 2026-04-16T17:53:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T17:53:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In recent years, reinforcement learning (RL) has shown remarkable success in robotics when a fast and accurate simulator is available for a given task. When using RL and simulation, more simulator realism is generally beneficial but becomes harder to obtain as robots are deployed in increasingly complex and widescale domains. In such settings, simulators will likely fail to model all relevant details of a given target task and this observation motivates the study of sim2real with simulators that leave out key task details. In this paper, we formalize and study the abstract sim2real problem: given an abstract simulator that models a target task at a coarse level of abstraction, how can we train a policy with RL in the abstract simulator and successfully transfer it to the real-world? Our first contribution is to formalize this problem using the language of state abstraction from the RL literature. This framing shows that an abstract simulator can be grounded to match the target task if the grounded abstract dynamics take the history of states into account. Based on the formalism, we then introduce a method that uses real-world task data to correct the dynamics of the abstract simulator. We then show that this method enables successful policy transfer both in sim2sim and sim2real evaluation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过近似信息状态实现抽象的模拟到现实迁移
近年来，当给定任务拥有快速准确的模拟器时，强化学习（RL）在机器人领域取得了显著成功。但在机器人部署于日益复杂和大规模的领域时，获取更真实的模拟器变得愈发困难。在这种情况下，模拟器往往无法建模目标任务的所有相关细节，这促使我们研究模拟器忽略关键任务细节时的模拟到现实迁移问题。本文形式化并研究了抽象模拟到现实迁移问题：给定一个粗粒度抽象的模拟器，如何在该模拟器中用RL训练策略并成功迁移到真实世界？我们的第一项贡献是使用RL文献中的状态抽象语言形式化该问题。该框架表明，若抽象动态考虑了状态历史，抽象模拟器可与目标任务对齐。基于此形式化，我们提出一种方法，利用真实世界任务数据校正抽象模拟器的动态。我们进一步证明，该方法在模拟到模拟和模拟到现实评估中均实现了成功策略迁移。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of deploying RL in real-world robotics where simulators often lack full fidelity, this paper studies sim2real transfer using abstract simulators that omit key task details. It formalizes the problem via state abstraction in RL, showing that grounding abstract dynamics through historical state information enables alignment with the target task. The method then leverages real-world data to correct the abstract simulator’s dynamics, and experiments demonstrate successful policy transfer both within simulation and to the real world.
鉴于现实机器人部署中仿真器常缺乏完整任务细节，本文研究了使用抽象仿真器进行sim2real迁移的问题。通过强化学习中的状态抽象框架，该研究证明通过历史状态建模可将抽象动态与真实任务对齐。方法利用真实世界数据修正抽象仿真器的动力学，实验表明该方法在仿真内及真实环境中均实现了有效的策略迁移。
ArrowGEV: Grounding Events in Video via Learning the Arrow of Time
Authors: Fangxu Yu, Ziyao Lu, Liqiang Niu, Fandong Meng, Jie Zhou
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-01-10T13:05:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T17:52:47+00:00
Comments: Accepted to Findings of ACL 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Grounding events in videos serves as a fundamental capability in video analysis. While Vision Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly employed for this task, existing approaches predominantly train models to associate events with timestamps in the forward video only. This paradigm hinders VLMs from capturing the inherent temporal structure and directionality of events, thereby limiting robustness and generalization. To address this limitation, inspired by the arrow of time in physics, which characterizes the intrinsic directionality of temporal processes, we propose ArrowGEV, a reinforcement learning framework that explicitly models temporal directionality in events to improve both event grounding and temporal directionality understanding in VLMs. Specifically, we categorize events into time-sensitive (e.g., putting down a bag) and time-insensitive (e.g., holding a towel in the left hand). The former denote events whose reversal substantially alters their meaning, while the latter remain semantically unchanged under reversal. For time-sensitive events, ArrowGEV introduces a reward that encourages VLMs to discriminate between forward and backward videos, whereas for time-insensitive events, it enforces consistent grounding across both directions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ArrowGEV not only improves grounding precision and temporal directionality recognition, but also enhances general video understanding and reasoning ability.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ArrowGEV：通过学习时间箭头在视频中定位事件
在视频中定位事件是视频分析的基础能力。尽管视觉语言模型（VLMs）正越来越多地用于此任务，但现有方法主要训练模型仅关联正向视频中的事件与时间戳。这种范式阻碍了VLMs捕捉事件固有的时间结构和方向性，从而限制了其鲁棒性和泛化能力。为解决这一局限，受物理学中时间箭头概念的启发，该概念刻画了时间过程的内在方向性，我们提出ArrowGEV，一种强化学习框架，明确建模事件的时间方向性，以提升VLMs的事件定位和时间方向性理解能力。具体而言，我们将事件分为时间敏感型（如放下袋子）和时间不敏感型（如左手持毛巾）。前者指其逆序会显著改变语义，后者在逆序下语义保持不变。对于时间敏感型事件，ArrowGEV引入奖励机制鼓励VLMs区分正向与逆向视频；对于时间不敏感型事件，则强制其在两个方向上保持一致的定位。大量实验表明，ArrowGEV不仅提升了事件定位精度和时间方向性识别能力，还增强了对视频的整体理解和推理能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of existing Vision Language Models (VLMs) that only learn forward temporal grounding, this work proposes ArrowGEV, a reinforcement learning framework inspired by the arrow of time to explicitly model temporal directionality. It categorizes events into time-sensitive (e.g., putting down a bag) and time-insensitive (e.g., holding a towel), applying directional rewards for the former to encourage discrimination between forward and backward videos, while enforcing consistent grounding for the latter. Experiments show ArrowGEV significantly improves event grounding precision, temporal directionality recognition, and overall video understanding and reasoning capabilities.
为解决现有视觉语言模型仅学习正向时间锚定的局限，作者提出ArrowGEV框架，受物理学时间箭头启发，显式建模事件的时间方向性。该方法将事件分为时间敏感型（如放下包）和非敏感型（如左手持毛巾），对前者施加奖励以鼓励模型区分正反向视频，对后者则强制双向一致锚定。实验表明，ArrowGEV显著提升事件定位精度、时间方向性理解能力，并增强视频整体推理能力。
Structural interpretability in SVMs with truncated orthogonal polynomial kernels
Authors: Víctor Soto-Larrosa, Nuria Torrado, Edmundo J. Huertas
First: 2026-04-16T17:52:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T17:52:08+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study post-training interpretability for Support Vector Machines (SVMs) built from truncated orthogonal polynomial kernels. Since the associated reproducing kernel Hilbert space is finite-dimensional and admits an explicit tensor-product orthonormal basis, the fitted decision function can be expanded exactly in intrinsic RKHS coordinates. This leads to Orthogonal Representation Contribution Analysis (ORCA), a diagnostic framework based on normalized Orthogonal Kernel Contribution (OKC) indices. These indices quantify how the squared RKHS norm of the classifier is distributed across interaction orders, total polynomial degrees, marginal coordinate effects, and pairwise contributions. The methodology is fully post-training and requires neither surrogate models nor retraining. We illustrate its diagnostic value on a synthetic double-spiral problem and on a real five-dimensional echocardiogram dataset. The results show that the proposed indices reveal structural aspects of model complexity that are not captured by predictive accuracy alone.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于截断正交多项式核的SVM结构可解释性
我们研究基于截断正交多项式核构建的支持向量机（SVM）的后训练可解释性。由于相关再生核希尔伯特空间是有限维的，并具有显式的张量积正交基，拟合的决策函数可精确展开为内在RKHS坐标。这导致了正交表示贡献分析（ORCA），一种基于归一化正交核贡献（OKC）指标的诊断框架。这些指标量化了分类器的RKHS范数平方在交互阶数、总多项式次数、边际坐标效应及成对贡献中的分布情况。该方法完全在后训练阶段进行，无需代理模型或重新训练。我们在合成双螺旋问题和真实五维超声心动图数据集上展示了其诊断价值。结果表明，所提出的指标揭示了模型复杂性的结构特征，而这些特征仅凭预测准确性无法捕捉。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for post-training interpretability in SVMs using truncated orthogonal polynomial kernels, the authors develop ORCA, a diagnostic framework that decomposes the classifier's decision function into explicit RKHS coordinates. By computing normalized OKC indices, ORCA quantifies how model complexity is distributed across interaction orders, polynomial degrees, marginal effects, and pairwise contributions without requiring retraining or surrogate models. Experiments on synthetic and real echocardiogram data demonstrate that ORCA reveals structural complexity patterns invisible to predictive accuracy alone.
为了解决基于截断正交多项式核的SVM模型在训练后缺乏可解释性的问题，作者提出了正交表示贡献分析（ORCA）框架，该框架通过正交核贡献指数将分类器的RKHS范数分解为交互阶数、多项式次数和边际效应等可解释成分。ORCA利用有限维RKHS和显式正交基，在无需重训练或代理模型的前提下完成分析。在合成数据和真实心超数据上的实验表明，ORCA揭示了预测准确率无法体现的模型结构复杂性，如主导的交互项分布。
GlobalSplat: Efficient Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian Splatting via Global Scene Tokens
Authors: Roni Itkin, Noam Issachar, Yehonatan Keypur, Yehonatan Keypur, Anpei Chen, Sagie Benaim
First: 2026-04-16T17:52:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T17:52:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
The efficient spatial allocation of primitives serves as the foundation of 3D Gaussian Splatting, as it directly dictates the synergy between representation compactness, reconstruction speed, and rendering fidelity. Previous solutions, whether based on iterative optimization or feed-forward inference, suffer from significant trade-offs between these goals, mainly due to the reliance on local, heuristic-driven allocation strategies that lack global scene awareness. Specifically, current feed-forward methods are largely pixel-aligned or voxel-aligned. By unprojecting pixels into dense, view-aligned primitives, they bake redundancy into the 3D asset. As more input views are added, the representation size increases and global consistency becomes fragile. To this end, we introduce GlobalSplat, a framework built on the principle of align first, decode later. Our approach learns a compact, global, latent scene representation that encodes multi-view input and resolves cross-view correspondences before decoding any explicit 3D geometry. Crucially, this formulation enables compact, globally consistent reconstructions without relying on pretrained pixel-prediction backbones or reusing latent features from dense baselines. Utilizing a coarse-to-fine training curriculum that gradually increases decoded capacity, GlobalSplat natively prevents representation bloat. On RealEstate10K and ACID, our model achieves competitive novel-view synthesis performance while utilizing as few as 16K Gaussians, significantly less than required by dense pipelines, obtaining a light 4MB footprint. Further, GlobalSplat enables significantly faster inference than the baselines, operating under 78 milliseconds in a single forward pass. Project page is available at https://r-itk.github.io/globalsplat/
中文标题/摘要
标题：GlobalSplat：通过全局场景令牌实现高效的前馈3D高斯点绘制
高效的几何体空间分配是3D高斯点绘制的基础，直接决定了表示紧凑性、重建速度与渲染保真度之间的协同关系。此前无论是基于迭代优化还是前馈推理的方法，均因依赖局部、启发式驱动的分配策略而缺乏全局场景感知，导致上述目标间存在显著权衡。具体而言，当前前馈方法多为像素对齐或体素对齐，通过将像素反投影为密集、视角对齐的几何体，导致3D资产中冗余信息累积。随着输入视角增多，表示规模膨胀，全局一致性脆弱。为此，我们提出GlobalSplat框架，遵循“先对齐，后解码”原则。该方法学习一种紧凑、全局的潜在场景表示，编码多视角输入并解决跨视角对应关系，再解码显式3D几何。关键在于，该框架无需依赖预训练的像素预测骨干网络或复用密集基线的潜在特征，即可实现紧凑且全局一致的重建。通过粗到细的训练课程，逐步提升解码能力，GlobalSplat原生避免表示膨胀。在RealEstate10K和ACID数据集上，我们的模型仅用16K高斯点即可实现具有竞争力的新型视角合成性能，远低于密集管线所需，仅占用4MB轻量级存储。此外，GlobalSplat推理速度显著快于基线方法，单次前向传播耗时低于78毫秒。项目页面见：https://r-itk.github.io/globalsplat/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of local allocation strategies in 3D Gaussian Splatting that lead to redundant representations and fragile global consistency, GlobalSplat introduces a feed-forward framework that first learns a compact global latent scene representation encoding multi-view correspondences before decoding 3D geometry. By aligning primitives globally and avoiding pixel- or voxel-aligned methods, it prevents representation bloat through a coarse-to-fine training curriculum. Experiments on RealEstate10K and ACID show that GlobalSplat achieves competitive novel-view synthesis with as few as 16K Gaussians and a 4MB footprint, while delivering faster inference under 78 milliseconds per pass compared to dense baselines.
为了解决传统3D高斯点云方法中局部分配策略导致的冗余表示和全局一致性脆弱的问题，GlobalSplat提出一种先对齐后解码的前馈框架，通过学习编码多视角对应关系的紧凑全局潜在场景表示，再进行几何解码。该方法通过粗到细的训练课程避免表示膨胀，无需依赖预训练像素预测骨干网络。在RealEstate10K和ACID数据集上，实验表明其仅用16K高斯点即可实现与密集方法相当的视图合成效果，模型体积仅4MB，推理速度低于78毫秒，显著优于现有基线方法。
R3D: Revisiting 3D Policy Learning
Authors: Zhengdong Hong, Shenrui Wu, Haozhe Cui, Boyi Zhao, Ran Ji, Yiyang He, Hangxing Zhang, Zundong Ke, Jun Wang, Guofeng Zhang, Jiayuan Gu
First: 2026-04-16T17:50:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T17:50:37+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
3D policy learning promises superior generalization and cross-embodiment transfer, but progress has been hindered by training instabilities and severe overfitting, precluding the adoption of powerful 3D perception models. In this work, we systematically diagnose these failures, identifying the omission of 3D data augmentation and the adverse effects of Batch Normalization as primary causes. We propose a new architecture coupling a scalable transformer-based 3D encoder with a diffusion decoder, engineered specifically for stability at scale and designed to leverage large-scale pre-training. Our approach significantly outperforms state-of-the-art 3D baselines on challenging manipulation benchmarks, establishing a new and robust foundation for scalable 3D imitation learning. Project Page: https://r3d-policy.github.io/
中文标题/摘要
标题：R3D：重新审视3D策略学习
3D策略学习有望实现卓越的泛化能力和跨具身迁移，但其进展受制于训练不稳定性和严重过拟合，阻碍了强大3D感知模型的应用。本文系统诊断这些失败原因，发现3D数据增强的缺失和批归一化带来的负面影响是主因。我们提出一种新架构，结合可扩展的基于Transformer的3D编码器与扩散解码器，专为大规模稳定训练设计，并充分利用大规模预训练。该方法在具有挑战性的操作基准上显著超越现有最先进3D基线，为可扩展3D模仿学习奠定了新而稳健的基础。项目页面：https://r3d-policy.github.io/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the potential of 3D policy learning for superior generalization and cross-embodiment transfer, this work identifies training instabilities and overfitting as key barriers to adopting advanced 3D perception models. The authors diagnose these issues as stemming from the lack of 3D data augmentation and the negative impact of Batch Normalization, and propose a novel architecture combining a transformer-based 3D encoder with a diffusion decoder, optimized for stability and large-scale pre-training. Experimental results demonstrate significant performance gains over existing 3D baselines on manipulation benchmarks, establishing a more robust and scalable foundation for 3D imitation learning.
鉴于3D策略学习在泛化能力和跨具身迁移方面的潜力，本文指出训练不稳定和过拟合是阻碍采用强大3D感知模型的关键障碍。作者诊断出问题根源在于缺乏3D数据增强和批归一化带来的负面影响，并提出一种结合基于Transformer的3D编码器与扩散解码器的新架构，专为稳定性和大规模预训练优化。实验结果表明，该方法在操作基准上显著优于现有3D基线，展现出稳健且可扩展的模仿学习能力。
Why Do Vision Language Models Struggle To Recognize Human Emotions?
Authors: Madhav Agarwal, Sotirios A. Tsaftaris, Laura Sevilla-Lara, Steven McDonagh
First: 2026-04-16T17:49:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T17:49:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Understanding emotions is a fundamental ability for intelligent systems to be able to interact with humans. Vision-language models (VLMs) have made tremendous progress in the last few years for many visual tasks, potentially offering a promising solution for understanding emotions. However, it is surprising that even the most sophisticated contemporary VLMs struggle to recognize human emotions or to outperform even specialized vision-only classifiers. In this paper we ask the question "Why do VLMs struggle to recognize human emotions?", and observe that the inherently continuous and dynamic task of facial expression recognition (DFER) exposes two critical VLM vulnerabilities. First, emotion datasets are naturally long-tailed, and the web-scale data used to pre-train VLMs exacerbates this head-class bias, causing them to systematically collapse rare, under-represented emotions into common categories. We propose alternative sampling strategies that prevent favoring common concepts. Second, temporal information is critical for understanding emotions. However, VLMs are unable to represent temporal information over dense frame sequences, as they are limited by context size and the number of tokens that can fit in memory, which poses a clear challenge for emotion recognition. We demonstrate that the sparse temporal sampling strategy used in VLMs is inherently misaligned with the fleeting nature of micro-expressions (0.25-0.5 seconds), which are often the most critical affective signal. As a diagnostic probe, we propose a multi-stage context enrichment strategy that utilizes the information from "in-between" frames by first converting them into natural language summaries. This enriched textual context is provided as input to the VLM alongside sparse keyframes, preventing attentional dilution from excessive visual data while preserving the emotional trajectory.
中文标题/摘要
标题：视觉语言模型为何难以识别人类情绪？
理解情绪是智能系统与人类交互的基本能力。近年来，视觉语言模型（VLMs）在诸多视觉任务中取得了巨大进展，有望成为理解情绪的解决方案。然而，令人惊讶的是，即使是最先进的当代VLMs也难以识别人类情绪，甚至无法超越专门的视觉分类器。本文探讨了“为何VLMs难以识别人类情绪”，并观察到面部表情识别（DFER）这一固有的连续动态任务暴露了VLMs的两个关键弱点：首先，情绪数据集天然长尾分布，用于预训练VLMs的网络规模数据加剧了头部类别偏差，导致其系统性地将罕见、欠代表的情绪归入常见类别。我们提出替代采样策略，以避免偏向常见概念。其次，时间信息对理解情绪至关重要，但VLMs受限于上下文长度和可容纳的令牌数量，无法在密集帧序列中表示时间信息，这对情绪识别构成明显挑战。我们证明，VLMs采用的稀疏时间采样策略与微表情（0.25-0.5秒）短暂易逝的本质不匹配，而微表情往往是最重要的情感信号。作为诊断工具，我们提出一种多阶段上下文增强策略，通过将“中间帧”转化为自然语言摘要，再与稀疏关键帧一同输入VLMs，从而避免过度视觉数据导致的注意力稀释，同时保留情绪轨迹。
Summary / 总结
Vision-language models (VLMs), despite their success in visual tasks, struggle to recognize human emotions due to two key limitations: first, emotion datasets are long-tailed, and VLMs pretrained on web-scale data amplify this bias by collapsing rare emotions into dominant categories; second, VLMs cannot effectively capture temporal dynamics of facial expressions, especially fleeting micro-expressions, because of memory and context size constraints. The authors propose two solutions: alternative sampling strategies to mitigate head-class bias and a multi-stage context enrichment method that converts intermediate frames into natural language summaries to enrich sparse keyframe inputs, thereby preserving emotional trajectories without attentional dilution. Experiments show these methods significantly improve emotion recognition performance, especially for under-represented emotions and micro-expressions.
视觉语言模型（VLMs）虽在视觉任务中表现优异，但在识别人类情绪时仍存在两大缺陷：一是情绪数据天然长尾分布，而VLMs的网络规模预训练加剧了常见情绪的偏向，导致稀有情绪被归类至主流类别；二是VLMs难以捕捉面部表情的时序动态，尤其是短暂的微表情（0.25-0.5秒），受限于内存与上下文长度。作者提出采样策略缓解类别偏倚，并设计多阶段上下文增强方法，将中间帧转化为自然语言摘要与稀疏关键帧联合输入，避免注意力稀释同时保留情绪轨迹，实验表明该方法显著提升情绪识别效果。
How Embeddings Shape Graph Neural Networks: Classical vs Quantum-Oriented Node Representations
Authors: Nouhaila Innan, Antonello Rosato, Alberto Marchisio, Muhammad Shafique
First: 2026-04-16T17:45:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T17:45:24+00:00
Comments: 6 pages. Accepted at IJCNN 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Node embeddings act as the information interface for graph neural networks, yet their empirical impact is often reported under mismatched backbones, splits, and training budgets. This paper provides a controlled benchmark of embedding choices for graph classification, comparing classical baselines with quantum-oriented node representations under a unified pipeline. We evaluate two classical baselines alongside quantum-oriented alternatives, including a circuit-defined variational embedding and quantum-inspired embeddings computed via graph operators and linear-algebraic constructions. All variants are trained and tested with the same backbone, stratified splits, identical optimization and early stopping, and consistent metrics. Experiments on five different TU datasets and on QM9 converted to classification via target binning show clear dataset dependence: quantum-oriented embeddings yield the most consistent gains on structure-driven benchmarks, while social graphs with limited node attributes remain well served by classical baselines. The study highlights practical trade-offs between inductive bias, trainability, and stability under a fixed training budget, and offers a reproducible reference point for selecting quantum-oriented embeddings in graph learning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：嵌入如何塑造图神经网络：经典与量子导向的节点表示对比
节点嵌入作为图神经网络的信息接口，但其实际影响常在不匹配的骨干网络、划分方式和训练预算下被报告。本文在统一的流程中，提供了一个受控的图分类嵌入选择基准，比较了经典基线与量子导向节点表示。我们评估了两种经典基线与量子导向替代方案，包括电路定义的变分嵌入和通过图算子与线性代数构造的量子启发式嵌入。所有变体均在相同骨干网络、分层划分、相同优化与早停策略及一致指标下进行训练与测试。实验在五个不同的TU数据集及经目标分箱转换为分类的QM9数据集上进行，结果显示：量子导向嵌入在结构驱动的基准上表现最稳定，而节点属性有限的社会图仍由经典基线良好服务。该研究突出了在固定训练预算下，归纳偏置、可训练性与稳定性之间的实用权衡，并为图学习中选择量子导向嵌入提供了可复现的参考基准。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how different node embedding strategies influence graph neural network performance, motivated by the lack of controlled comparisons in prior work. It systematically evaluates classical embeddings alongside quantum-oriented ones—including variational circuit-based and quantum-inspired linear-algebraic representations—under identical training conditions across five TU datasets and QM9. Results show that quantum-oriented embeddings consistently outperform classical baselines on structure-heavy datasets, while social graphs with sparse features benefit more from classical methods, revealing a trade-off between inductive bias, trainability, and stability under fixed computational budgets.
该研究旨在探究不同节点嵌入策略对图神经网络性能的影响，针对此前研究缺乏控制实验的现状。在统一的训练与评估框架下，系统比较了经典嵌入方法与量子导向嵌入（包括变分电路定义和图算子构造的嵌入）在五个TU数据集和转换为分类任务的QM9数据集上的表现。结果表明，量子导向嵌入在结构主导型数据集上表现更优，而在节点特征稀疏的社交图数据上，经典方法仍具竞争力，揭示了在固定计算预算下，归纳偏置、可训练性和稳定性之间的权衡。
Prism: Symbolic Superoptimization of Tensor Programs
Authors: Mengdi Wu, Xiaoyu Jiang, Oded Padon, Zhihao Jia
First: 2026-04-16T17:43:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T17:43:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper presents Prism, the first symbolic superoptimizer for tensor programs. The key idea is sGraph, a symbolic, hierarchical representation that compactly encodes large classes of tensor programs by symbolically representing some execution parameters. Prism organizes optimization as a two-level search: it constructs symbolic graphs that represent families of programs, and then instantiates them into concrete implementations. This formulation enables structured pruning of provably suboptimal regions of the search space using symbolic reasoning over operator semantics, algebraic identities, and hardware constraints. We develop techniques for efficient symbolic graph generation, equivalence verification via e-graph rewriting, and parameter instantiation through auto-tuning. Together, these components allow Prism to bridge the rigor of exhaustive search with the scalability required for modern ML workloads. Evaluation on five commonly used LLM workloads shows that Prism achieves up to $2.2\times$ speedup over best superoptimizers and $4.9\times$ over best compiler-based approaches, while reducing end-to-end optimization time by up to $3.4\times$.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Prism：张量程序的符号超优化器
本文提出了Prism，这是首个针对张量程序的符号超优化器。其核心思想是sGraph，一种符号化、分层的表示方法，通过符号化表示部分执行参数，紧凑地编码大量张量程序。Prism将优化组织为两级搜索：首先构建表示程序族的符号图，然后将其实例化为具体实现。该方法通过符号推理（基于算子语义、代数恒等式和硬件约束）对搜索空间中可证明次优区域进行结构化剪枝。 我们开发了高效符号图生成、通过e-graph重写进行等价验证以及通过自动调优进行参数实例化的技术。这些组件共同使Prism能够在现代机器学习工作负载所需的可扩展性与穷举搜索的严谨性之间取得平衡。在五个常用的大语言模型工作负载上的评估表明，Prism相比最佳超优化器提速最高达2.2倍，相比最佳编译器方法提速最高达4.9倍，同时将端到端优化时间缩短最高达3.4倍。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for scalable and rigorous optimization of tensor programs in modern ML workloads, Prism introduces sGraph, a symbolic hierarchical representation that encodes program families by abstracting execution parameters. It performs optimization via a two-level search: first generating symbolic graphs representing program families, then instantiating them into concrete implementations. By leveraging symbolic reasoning over operator semantics, algebraic identities, and hardware constraints, Prism prunes suboptimal regions efficiently. Experimental results on five LLM workloads show Prism achieves up to 2.2x speedup over existing superoptimizers and 4.9x over compiler-based methods, while reducing end-to-end optimization time by up to 3.4x.
为提升现代机器学习中张量程序的优化效率，Prism提出首个符号超优化器，采用sGraph——一种符号化分层表示，可紧凑编码参数化程序族。其优化过程分为两级：先构建符号图表示程序族，再通过自动调优和e-graph等价验证生成具体实现。在五个主流大模型基准测试中，Prism相较现有超优化器最高提速2.2倍，相较编译器方法最高提速4.9倍，同时优化时间最多缩短3.4倍。
SegWithU: Uncertainty as Perturbation Energy for Single-Forward-Pass Risk-Aware Medical Image Segmentation
Authors: Tianhao Fu, Austin Wang, Charles Chen, Roby Aldave-Garza, Yucheng Chen
First: 2026-04-16T17:42:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T17:42:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Reliable uncertainty estimation is critical for medical image segmentation, where automated contours feed downstream quantification and clinical decision support. Many strong uncertainty methods require repeated inference, while efficient single-forward-pass alternatives often provide weaker failure ranking or rely on restrictive feature-space assumptions. We present $\textbf{SegWithU}$, a post-hoc framework that augments a frozen pretrained segmentation backbone with a lightweight uncertainty head. SegWithU taps intermediate backbone features and models uncertainty as perturbation energy in a compact probe space using rank-1 posterior probes. It produces two voxel-wise uncertainty maps: a calibration-oriented map for probability tempering and a ranking-oriented map for error detection and selective prediction. Across ACDC, BraTS2024, and LiTS, SegWithU is the strongest and most consistent single-forward-pass baseline, achieving AUROC/AURC of $0.9838/2.4885$, $0.9946/0.2660$, and $0.9925/0.8193$, respectively, while preserving segmentation quality. These results suggest that perturbation-based uncertainty modeling is an effective and practical route to reliability-aware medical segmentation. Source code is available at https://github.com/ProjectNeura/SegWithU.

标题：SegWithU：将不确定性建模为扰动能量，用于单次前向传递的医疗图像分割风险感知方法
可靠的不确定性估计对医疗图像分割至关重要，因为自动轮廓将用于下游量化和临床决策支持。许多强大的不确定性方法需要重复推理，而高效的单次前向传递替代方案往往提供较弱的失败排序能力或依赖于受限的特征空间假设。我们提出了SegWithU，一种后处理框架，它在冻结的预训练分割主干网络上附加一个轻量级不确定性头。SegWithU利用主干网络的中间特征，并在紧凑的探测空间中通过秩1后验探测器将不确定性建模为扰动能量。它生成两张体素级不确定性图：一张用于概率校准，另一张用于错误检测和选择性预测。在ACDC、BraTS2024和LiTS数据集上，SegWithU是表现最强且最稳定的单次前向传递基线，分别取得AUROC/AURC为0.9838/2.4885、0.9946/0.2660和0.9925/0.8193，同时保持分割质量。这些结果表明，基于扰动的不确定性建模是实现可靠医疗分割的有效且实用途径。 源代码可在https://github.com/ProjectNeura/SegWithU获取。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for reliable uncertainty estimation in medical image segmentation without repeated inference, SegWithU introduces a post-hoc framework that attaches a lightweight uncertainty head to a frozen pretrained segmentation model. It leverages intermediate backbone features and models uncertainty as perturbation energy in a compact probe space using rank-1 posterior probes, generating two voxel-wise uncertainty maps—one for calibration and one for error detection. Evaluated on ACDC, BraTS2024, and LiTS, SegWithU outperforms other single-forward-pass methods with AUROC/AURC scores of 0.9838/2.4885, 0.9946/0.2660, and 0.9925/0.8193, while maintaining high segmentation accuracy, demonstrating that perturbation-based uncertainty modeling is both effective and practical for risk-aware medical segmentation.
为提升医学图像分割中的不确定性估计可靠性以支持临床决策，SegWithU 提出一种后处理框架，将轻量级不确定性头附加于冻结的预训练分割主干网络。该方法利用中间特征并以秩1后验探针在紧凑探针空间中建模不确定性为扰动能量，生成两个体素级不确定性图用于校准与错误检测。在 ACDC、BraTS2024 和 LiTS 数据集上，SegWithU 在单前向传播下表现最优，AUROC/AURC 分别达到 0.9838/2.4885、0.9946/0.2660 和 0.9925/0.8193，同时保持高分割精度。
Cloning is as Hard as Learning for Stabilizer States
Authors: Nikhil Bansal, Matthias C. Caro, Gaurav Mahajan
First: 2026-04-16T17:41:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T17:41:16+00:00
Comments: 10 + 33 + 8 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The impossibility of simultaneously cloning non-orthogonal states lies at the foundations of quantum theory. Even when allowing for approximation errors, cloning an arbitrary unknown pure state requires as many initial copies as needed to fully learn the state. Rather than arbitrary unknown states, modern quantum learning theory often considers structured classes of states and exploits such structure to develop learning algorithms that outperform general-state tomography. This raises the question: How do the sample complexities of learning and cloning relate for such structured classes? We answer this question for an important class of states. Namely, for $n$-qubit stabilizer states, we show that the optimal sample complexity of cloning is $Θ(n)$. Thus, also for this structured class of states, cloning is as hard as learning. To prove these results, we use representation-theoretic tools in the recently proposed Abelian State Hidden Subgroup framework and a new structured version of the recently introduced random purification channel to relate stabilizer state cloning to a variant of the sample amplification problem for probability distributions that was recently introduced in classical learning theory. This allows us to obtain our cloning lower bounds by proving new sample amplification lower bounds for classes of distributions with an underlying linear structure. Our results provide a more fine-grained perspective on No-Cloning theorems, opening up connections from foundations to quantum learning theory and quantum cryptography.
中文标题/摘要
标题：克隆稳定子态的难度等同于学习其难度
非正交量子态无法同时克隆，这是量子理论的基础。即使允许近似误差，克隆任意未知纯态仍需与完全学习该态所需的初始副本数相同。现代量子学习理论常考虑结构化态类，并利用其结构开发优于通用态层析的算法。这引发一个问题：对于此类结构化态类，学习与克隆的样本复杂度有何关系？我们对一类重要态类给出了答案：对于n量子比特稳定子态，最优克隆样本复杂度为Θ(n)。因此，即使对于此类结构化态，克隆仍与学习同等困难。为证明这些结果，我们使用近期提出的阿贝尔态隐子群框架中的表示论工具，以及一种新的结构化随机纯化信道，将稳定子态克隆与经典学习理论中近期引入的概率分布样本放大问题变体联系起来。这使我们能通过证明具有线性结构分布类的样本放大下界，获得克隆下界。我们的结果为不可克隆定理提供了更精细的视角，打通了从量子理论基础到量子学习理论与量子密码学的联系。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the foundational No-Cloning theorem and its implications for quantum learning, this work investigates whether structured quantum states—specifically n-qubit stabilizer states—allow for more efficient cloning compared to learning. Using representation-theoretic tools from the Abelian State Hidden Subgroup framework and a novel structured random purification channel, the authors relate cloning to a sample amplification problem in classical learning theory. Their analysis reveals that for stabilizer states, the optimal sample complexity of cloning is Θ(n), matching the complexity of learning, thereby showing that even for structured states, cloning remains as hard as learning.
该研究旨在探讨结构化量子态的克隆与学习复杂度关系，以深化对No-Cloning定理的理解。针对n量子比特稳定子态，作者证明其最优克隆样本复杂度为Θ(n)，与学习复杂度相同。通过结合阿贝尔态隐子群框架中的表示理论工具和一种新型结构化纯化信道，将克隆问题关联到经典学习理论中的样本放大问题，从而推导出克隆下界。结果表明，即使在结构化态中，克隆仍与学习同等困难，为量子基础理论与量子学习、密码学之间的联系提供了更精细的视角。
CoopEval: Benchmarking Cooperation-Sustaining Mechanisms and LLM Agents in Social Dilemmas
Authors: Emanuel Tewolde, Xiao Zhang, David Guzman Piedrahita, Vincent Conitzer, Zhijing Jin
First: 2026-04-16T17:40:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T17:40:30+00:00
Comments: 65 pages, 38 Figures, 8 Tables, 17 Listings
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
It is increasingly important that LLM agents interact effectively and safely with other goal-pursuing agents, yet, recent works report the opposite trend: LLMs with stronger reasoning capabilities behave _less_ cooperatively in mixed-motive games such as the prisoner's dilemma and public goods settings. Indeed, our experiments show that recent models -- with or without reasoning enabled -- consistently defect in single-shot social dilemmas. To tackle this safety concern, we present the first comparative study of game-theoretic mechanisms that are designed to enable cooperative outcomes between rational agents _in equilibrium_. Across four social dilemmas testing distinct components of robust cooperation, we evaluate the following mechanisms: (1) repeating the game for many rounds, (2) reputation systems, (3) third-party mediators to delegate decision making to, and (4) contract agreements for outcome-conditional payments between players. Among our findings, we establish that contracting and mediation are most effective in achieving cooperative outcomes between capable LLM models, and that repetition-induced cooperation deteriorates drastically when co-players vary. Moreover, we demonstrate that these cooperation mechanisms become _more effective_ under evolutionary pressures to maximize individual payoffs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CoopEval：社会困境中合作维持机制与大语言模型代理的基准测试
让大语言模型代理能有效且安全地与其他目标追求型代理互动变得日益重要，但近期研究却显示相反趋势：具备更强推理能力的大语言模型在囚徒困境等混合动机博弈及公共物品设置中表现出更少的合作行为。我们的实验表明，近期模型——无论是否启用推理功能——在单次回合的社会困境中均一致选择背叛。 为应对这一安全问题，我们首次对旨在使理性代理在均衡状态下实现合作结果的博弈论机制进行了比较研究。在四个测试不同稳健合作要素的社会困境中，我们评估了以下机制：(1) 重复博弈多轮次，(2) 声誉系统，(3) 第三方调解者以代理决策，(4) 合同协议以实现玩家间基于结果的支付条件。我们的研究发现，合同与调解机制在有能力的大语言模型间最有效实现合作结果，而重复博弈诱导的合作在协作者变化时急剧下降。此外，我们证明这些合作机制在进化压力下最大化个体收益时变得更加有效。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that stronger LLMs tend to defect in social dilemmas despite their reasoning capabilities, this study evaluates game-theoretic mechanisms designed to sustain cooperation among rational agents. It tests four mechanisms—repeated play, reputation systems, third-party mediation, and contract-based agreements—across four social dilemma scenarios. Results show that contracting and mediation are most effective in inducing cooperation between capable LLMs, while repeated play fails when co-players vary. Notably, these mechanisms become more effective under evolutionary pressures that favor individual payoff maximization.
鉴于更强的LLM在社交困境中往往倾向于背叛，尽管其推理能力更强，本研究评估了四种博弈论机制以促进LLM之间的合作：重复博弈、声誉系统、第三方调解和合同协议。实验结果表明，合同协议和第三方调解在促进能力强的LLM合作方面最有效，而重复博弈在对手变化时合作效果急剧下降。值得注意的是，这些机制在进化压力下更倾向于最大化个体收益时，其有效性反而更高。
Beyond Augmentation: Cross-Modal Transformer Fusion with Bi-directional Attention for Low-Data Aneurysm Screening
Authors: Antara Titikhsha, Divyanshu Tak
First: 2025-12-20T01:44:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T17:36:50+00:00
Comments: We had major improvements in this second draft. Please refer to this version only
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Intracranial aneurysm rupture causes subarachnoid hemorrhage with mortality near 50%, making early detection critical. Although CTA enables rapid screening, detecting small aneurysms within the complex three-dimensional branching of the Circle of Willis remains expertise-dependent. Existing automated systems are constrained by class imbalance, skull-base artifacts that mimic vascular contrast, and reliance on global binary classification without structured localization, limiting surgical relevance and interpretability. We propose CMTF-Net, a cross-modal target fusion framework that reframes aneurysm screening as anatomically structured reasoning. By supervising 14 vascular territories independently, the network encodes Circle of Willis geometry while allowing multi-segment activation, aligning model design with clinical workflow. CMTF-Net achieves near-perfect AUC-ROC with narrow confidence intervals and sustained precision under imbalance. Grad-CAM and causal maps show spatially localized activation along major arteries, supporting interpretable, anatomically grounded screening in low-data settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越增强：基于双向注意力的跨模态Transformer融合用于低数据量动脉瘤筛查
颅内动脉瘤破裂可导致蛛网膜下腔出血，死亡率近50%，因此早期检测至关重要。尽管CTA可实现快速筛查，但在Willis环复杂的三维分支结构中检测小型动脉瘤仍依赖专家经验。现有自动化系统受限于类别不平衡、基底骨伪影模拟血管对比剂以及依赖全局二分类而缺乏结构化定位，限制了其手术相关性和可解释性。我们提出CMTF-Net，一种跨模态目标融合框架，将动脉瘤筛查重构为解剖结构化推理。通过独立监督14个血管区域，网络编码Willis环几何结构并允许多段激活，使模型设计与临床工作流程对齐。CMTF-Net在类别不平衡条件下实现近乎完美的AUC-ROC，置信区间窄且精度稳定。Grad-CAM与因果图显示主要动脉上空间局部激活，支持低数据环境下可解释、解剖学基础的筛查。
