Motivated by the high mortality risk from ruptured intracranial aneurysms and the limitations of current automated screening systems—such as class imbalance, skull-base artifacts, and lack of anatomical localization—the paper introduces CMTF-Net, a cross-modal transformer fusion model with bi-directional attention. This framework redefines aneurysm detection as structured anatomical reasoning by independently supervising 14 vascular territories within the Circle of Willis, enabling multi-segment activation aligned with clinical practice. Experimental results show CMTF-Net achieves near-perfect AUC-ROC with stable precision under data imbalance, and visualization tools like Grad-CAM confirm spatially localized, interpretable activations along major arteries, enhancing diagnostic reliability in low-data scenarios.
鉴于颅内动脉瘤破裂导致的高死亡率及现有自动化筛查系统在类别不平衡、颅底伪影干扰和缺乏解剖定位方面的局限性，本文提出CMTF-Net模型，采用双向注意力机制的跨模态Transformer融合架构。该模型将动脉瘤筛查重构为基于解剖结构的推理任务，通过独立监督14个血管区域，与临床工作流对齐并支持多段激活。实验表明，该模型在数据不平衡条件下仍保持接近完美的AUC-ROC值和稳定精度，且Grad-CAM可视化显示其激活集中在主要动脉，具备可解释的解剖学基础。
Stability and Generalization in Looped Transformers
Authors: Asher Labovich
First: 2026-04-16T17:35:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T17:35:49+00:00
Comments: 11 main pages, 27 total
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Looped transformers promise test-time compute scaling by spending more iterations on harder problems, but it remains unclear which architectural choices let them extrapolate to harder problems at test time rather than memorize training-specific solutions. We introduce a fixed-point based framework for analyzing looped architectures along three axes of stability -- reachability, input-dependence, and geometry -- and use it to characterize when fixed-point iteration yields meaningful predictions. Theoretically, we prove that looped networks without recall have countable fixed points and cannot achieve strong input-dependence at any spectral regime, while recall combined with outer normalization reliably produces a regime in which fixed points are simultaneously reachable, locally smooth in the input, and supported by stable backpropagation. Empirically, we train single-layer looped transformers on chess, sudoku, and prefix-sums and find that downstream performance tracks the framework's predictions across tasks and architectural configurations. We additionally introduce internal recall, a novel recall placement variant, and show that it becomes competitive with -- and on sudoku, substantially better than -- standard recall placement once outer normalization is applied.
中文标题/摘要
标题：循环变压器中的稳定性与泛化能力
循环变压器通过在较难问题上投入更多迭代次数来实现测试时计算量的扩展，但尚不清楚哪些架构选择能使其在测试时对更难的问题进行外推而非记忆训练特定解。我们引入一个基于定点的框架，沿三个稳定性维度——可达性、输入依赖性和几何结构——分析循环架构，并用其刻画定点迭代何时能产生有意义的预测。理论上，我们证明无记忆的循环网络在任何谱域内仅有可数个定点，无法实现强输入依赖性；而结合外层归一化的记忆机制则能可靠地产生一个定点同时可达、输入局部平滑且支持稳定反向传播的谱域。实证上，我们在国际象棋、数独和前缀和任务上训练单层循环变压器，发现下游性能在不同任务和架构配置下均与框架预测一致。我们还引入了一种新颖的记忆放置变体——内部记忆，并证明在应用外层归一化后，其性能可与标准记忆放置相媲美，而在数独任务上甚至显著优于后者。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how looped transformers generalize to harder problems at test time rather than memorize training data, the paper introduces a fixed-point analysis framework that evaluates stability across three axes: reachability, input-dependence, and geometry. Theoretical results show that looped networks without recall have countable fixed points and cannot achieve strong input-dependence, while combining recall with outer normalization enables stable, smooth, and backpropagation-friendly fixed points. Empirically, single-layer looped transformers trained on chess, sudoku, and prefix-sums demonstrate downstream performance that aligns with the framework’s predictions, and a novel internal recall variant, when paired with outer normalization, achieves competitive or superior results on sudoku.
为理解循环Transformer如何在测试时对更难问题进行泛化而不记忆训练数据，本文提出基于定点分析的框架，从可达性、输入依赖性和几何结构三个维度评估稳定性。理论表明，无回忆机制的循环网络仅有可数定点且无法实现强输入依赖，而结合外层归一化的回忆机制可稳定产生可达、局部平滑且支持反向传播的定点。实验表明，训练于国际象棋、数独和前缀和任务的单层循环Transformer性能与理论预测一致，且引入的内部回忆机制在配合外层归一化后表现优异，尤其在数独任务上显著优于标准回忆机制。
GeoLink: A 3D-Aware Framework Towards Better Generalization in Cross-View Geo-Localization
Authors: Hongyang Zhang, Yinhao Liu, Haitao Zhang, Zhongyi Wen, Zhenyu Kuang, Shuxian Liang, Xiansheng Hua
First: 2026-04-14T18:06:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T17:31:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Generalizable cross-view geo-localization aims to match the same location across views in unseen regions and conditions without GPS supervision. Its core difficulty lies in severe semantic inconsistency caused by viewpoint variation and poor generalization under domain shift. Existing methods mainly rely on 2D correspondence, but they are easily distracted by redundant shared information across views, leading to less transferable representations. To address this, we propose GeoLink, a 3D-aware semantic-consistent framework for Generalizable cross-view geo-localization. Specifically, we offline reconstruct scene point clouds from multi-view drone images using VGGT, providing stable structural priors. Based on these 3D anchors, we improve 2D representation learning in two complementary ways. A Geometric-aware Semantic Refinement module mitigates potentially redundant and view-biased dependencies in 2D features under 3D guidance. In addition, a Unified View Relation Distillation module transfers 3D structural relations to 2D features, improving cross-view alignment while preserving a 2D-only inference pipeline. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks show that GeoLink consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods and achieves superior generalization across unseen domains and diverse weather environments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GeoLink：面向跨视角地理定位更好泛化的3D感知框架
可泛化的跨视角地理定位旨在在未见过的区域和条件下，无需GPS监督匹配同一地点的不同视角。其核心难点在于视角变化导致的严重语义不一致，以及在域偏移下的泛化能力不足。现有方法主要依赖2D对应关系，但容易被视角间冗余共享信息干扰，导致迁移性较差的表示。为此，我们提出GeoLink，一种面向可泛化跨视角地理定位的3D感知语义一致框架。具体而言，我们利用VGGT从多视角无人机图像离线重建场景点云，提供稳定的结构先验。基于这些3D锚点，我们从两个互补角度改进2D表示学习：几何感知语义精炼模块在3D引导下缓解2D特征中潜在的冗余和视角偏倚依赖；统一视角关系蒸馏模块将3D结构关系迁移到2D特征，提升跨视角对齐的同时保持纯2D推理流程。在多个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，GeoLink持续优于现有最先进方法，并在未见域和多样天气环境下实现卓越的泛化性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of generalizing cross-view geo-localization across unseen regions and conditions without GPS, this paper introduces GeoLink, a 3D-aware framework that leverages offline reconstructed point clouds from drone images to provide structural priors. It enhances 2D representation learning via a Geometric-aware Semantic Refinement module that reduces view-biased dependencies and a Unified View Relation Distillation module that transfers 3D structural relations into 2D features for improved cross-view alignment. Experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that GeoLink consistently outperforms existing methods and achieves stronger generalization across diverse domains and weather conditions while maintaining a 2D-only inference pipeline.
为解决无GPS监督下跨视角地理定位在未见区域和条件下泛化能力差的问题，本文提出GeoLink框架，利用无人机多视角图像离线重建点云以提供结构先验。该框架通过两个模块提升2D特征学习：几何感知语义精炼模块在3D引导下减少视角偏倚的冗余依赖，统一视角关系蒸馏模块将3D结构关系迁移到2D特征以增强跨视角对齐，同时保持纯2D推理流程。实验表明，GeoLink在多个基准上持续优于现有方法，且在未见域和多样天气环境下泛化能力显著更强。
Model Capability Dominates: Inference-Time Optimization Lessons from AIMO 3
Authors: Natapong Nitarach
First: 2026-03-29T20:05:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T17:29:54+00:00
Comments: 18 pages, 6 figures, 10 tables. Kaggle AIMO 3 competition entry. Code and notebooks: https://github.com/nat-nischw/model-capability-dominates-lessons-aimo3
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Majority voting over multiple LLM attempts improves mathematical reasoning, but correlated errors limit the effective sample size. A natural fix is to assign different reasoning strategies to different voters. The approach, Diverse Prompt Mixer, is tested on the AIMO 3 competition: 3 models, 23+ experiments, 50 IMO-level problems, one H100 80 GB, 5-hour limit. Every prompt-level intervention fails. High-temperature sampling already decorrelates errors; weaker strategies reduce accuracy more than they reduce correlation. Across an 8-point capability gap at equal N=8 and every optimization tested, model capability dominates. The gap between the best majority-vote score (42/50) and pass@20 (~45.5) is selection loss, not prompt loss. A verifier-based selector could close it. Prompt engineering cannot.
中文标题/摘要
标题：模型能力主导：从AIMO 3竞赛中获得的推理优化经验
对多个大语言模型尝试进行多数投票可提升数学推理能力，但相关错误限制了有效样本量。自然的解决方案是为不同投票者分配不同的推理策略。该方法“多样化提示混合器”在AIMO 3竞赛中测试：3个模型，23+次实验，50道IMO级别问题，单台H100 80GB显卡，5小时限制。所有提示级干预均失败。高温度采样已能解耦错误；较弱策略反而降低准确率幅度大于降低相关性幅度。在模型能力差距达8分、N=8且所有优化方法测试下，模型能力始终占主导。最佳多数投票得分（42/50）与pass@20（约45.5）之间的差距源于选择损失而非提示损失，基于验证器的选择器可弥补此差距，提示工程无法实现。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that majority voting over multiple LLM attempts improves mathematical reasoning but suffers from correlated errors, the paper proposes Diverse Prompt Mixer—a method assigning different reasoning strategies to voters—to reduce error correlation. Tested on AIMO 3 with three models, over 23 experiments and 50 IMO-level problems, the study finds that prompt-level interventions fail to improve performance. High-temperature sampling already decorrelates errors, and weaker strategies reduce accuracy more than they reduce correlation. Across an 8-point capability gap at equal N=8, model capability consistently dominates all optimizations, and the gap between the best majority-vote score (42/50) and pass@20 (~45.5) reflects selection loss, not prompt loss, suggesting a verifier-based selector could help close it.
鉴于多数投票能提升LLM的数学推理能力但存在错误相关性，研究测试了通过分配不同推理策略来降低相关性的Diverse Prompt Mixer方法。在AIMO 3竞赛中，使用三个模型、50道IMO级题目进行23+次实验，尽管尝试了多种提示级干预，均未提升表现；高温度采样已能有效解耦错误，而较弱策略反而降低准确率。结果表明，模型能力主导所有优化：即使样本量相同（N=8），能力差距仍达8分，最佳多数投票得分（42/50）低于pass@20（~45.5），差距源于选择损失而非提示工程。
Critical-CoT: A Robust Defense Framework against Reasoning-Level Backdoor Attacks in Large Language Models
Authors: Vu Tuan Truong, Long Bao Le
First: 2026-04-12T15:19:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T17:29:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs), despite their impressive capabilities across domains, have been shown to be vulnerable to backdoor attacks. Prior backdoor strategies predominantly operate at the token level, where an injected trigger causes the model to generate a specific target word, choice, or class (depending on the task). Recent advances, however, exploit the long-form reasoning tendencies of modern LLMs to conduct reasoning-level backdoors: once triggered, the victim model inserts one or more malicious reasoning steps into its chain-of-thought (CoT). These attacks are substantially harder to detect, as the backdoored answer remains plausible and consistent with the poisoned reasoning trajectory. Yet, defenses tailored to this type of backdoor remain largely unexplored. To bridge this gap, we propose Critical-CoT, a novel defense mechanism that conducts a two-stage fine-tuning (FT) process on LLMs to develop critical thinking behaviors, enabling them to automatically identify potential backdoors and refuse to generate malicious reasoning steps. Extensive experiments across multiple LLMs and datasets demonstrate that Critical-CoT provides strong robustness against both in-context learning-based and FT-based backdoor attacks. Notably, Critical-CoT exhibits strong cross-domain and cross-task generalization. Our code is available at hthttps://github.com/tuanvu171/Critical-CoT.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Critical-CoT：一种针对大型语言模型推理层面后门攻击的鲁棒防御框架
尽管大型语言模型（LLMs）在多个领域展现出卓越能力，但已被证明易受后门攻击。此前的后门策略主要在词元层面操作，即注入触发器使模型生成特定目标词、选项或类别（取决于任务）。然而，近期研究利用现代LLMs的长篇推理倾向实施推理层面后门攻击：一旦触发，受害模型会在其思维链（CoT）中插入一个或多个恶意推理步骤。此类攻击更难检测，因为被植入的答桉仍具合理性，且与被污染的推理轨迹一致。但针对此类后门的防御机制尚属空白。为填补这一空白，我们提出Critical-CoT，一种新颖的防御机制，通过两阶段微调（FT）过程训练LLMs发展批判性思维能力，使其能自动识别潜在后门并拒绝生成恶意推理步骤。在多个LLMs和数据集上的大量实验表明，Critical-CoT对基于上下文学习和基于微调的后门攻击均具有强鲁棒性。值得注意的是，Critical-CoT展现出跨领域和跨任务的强泛化能力。我们的代码可在https://github.com/tuanvu171/Critical-CoT获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the emerging threat of reasoning-level backdoor attacks that exploit LLMs' chain-of-thought behavior to insert malicious reasoning steps, the authors propose Critical-CoT, a two-stage fine-tuning framework designed to instill critical thinking in models to detect and reject such backdoors. The method fine-tunes LLMs to recognize and reject anomalous reasoning patterns while preserving normal functionality. Experimental results across multiple LLMs and datasets show that Critical-CoT effectively defends against both in-context and fine-tuned backdoor attacks, with strong generalization across domains and tasks.
鉴于大语言模型易受注入恶意推理步骤的后门攻击，作者提出Critical-CoT，一种通过两阶段微调训练模型具备批判性思维能力的防御框架。该方法使模型能自动识别并拒绝生成恶意推理步骤，同时保持正常功能。实验表明，Critical-CoT在多种模型和数据集上有效抵御上下文学习与微调类后门攻击，并具备跨领域和跨任务的强泛化能力。
Uncovering the Fragility of Trustworthy LLMs through Chinese Textual Ambiguity
Authors: Xinwei Wu, Haojie Li, Hongyu Liu, Xinyu Ji, Ruohan Li, Yule Chen, Yigeng Zhang
Venue: KDD
First: 2025-07-30T21:50:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T17:24:41+00:00
Comments: Accepted at KDD workshop on Evaluation and Trustworthiness of Agentic and Generative AI Models (Agentic & GenAI Evaluation Workshop KDD '25)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
In this work, we study a critical research problem regarding the trustworthiness of large language models (LLMs): how LLMs behave when encountering ambiguous narrative text, with a particular focus on Chinese textual ambiguity. We created a benchmark dataset by collecting and generating ambiguous sentences with context and their corresponding disambiguated pairs, representing multiple possible interpretations. These annotated examples are systematically categorized into 3 main categories and 9 subcategories. Through experiments, we discovered significant fragility in LLMs when handling ambiguity, revealing behavior that differs substantially from humans. Specifically, LLMs cannot reliably distinguish ambiguous text from unambiguous text, show overconfidence in interpreting ambiguous text as having a single meaning rather than multiple meanings, and exhibit overthinking when attempting to understand the various possible meanings. Our findings highlight a fundamental limitation in current LLMs that has significant implications for their deployment in real-world applications where linguistic ambiguity is common, calling for improved approaches to handle uncertainty in language understanding. The dataset and code are publicly available at this GitHub repository: https://github.com/ictup/LLM-Chinese-Textual-Disambiguation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过中文文本歧义揭示可信大语言模型的脆弱性
本研究探讨了大语言模型（LLM）可信性的一个关键问题：当LLM遇到歧义叙事文本时的行为，尤其聚焦于中文文本歧义。我们通过收集和生成带有上下文的歧义句子及其对应的消歧配对，构建了一个基准数据集，代表多种可能的解释。这些标注示例被系统地划分为3个主要类别和9个子类别。实验发现，LLM在处理歧义时表现出显著脆弱性，其行为与人类有显著差异：无法可靠区分歧义文本与非歧义文本，对歧义文本的单一解释表现出过度自信，且在尝试理解多种可能含义时表现出过度思考。我们的发现揭示了当前LLM的一个根本性局限，这对语言理解中不确定性处理提出了改进需求，尤其在现实应用中语言歧义普遍存在。数据集和代码已公开于GitHub仓库：https://github.com/ictup/LLM-Chinese-Textual-Disambiguation。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how large language models handle ambiguous Chinese text, motivated by concerns over their trustworthiness in real-world applications where linguistic ambiguity is prevalent. The researchers constructed a benchmark dataset comprising ambiguous sentences with context and their disambiguated counterparts, categorized into three main types and nine subtypes. Experiments revealed that LLMs exhibit notable fragility: they struggle to distinguish ambiguous from unambiguous text, overconfidently assign single interpretations to ambiguous phrases, and overthink when attempting to resolve multiple meanings. These behaviors contrast sharply with human performance, underscoring a fundamental limitation in current LLMs that demands improved methods for managing linguistic uncertainty.
本研究旨在探究大语言模型在处理中文歧义文本时的表现，以应对其在现实应用中因语言模糊性而引发的信任问题。研究者构建了一个包含上下文及歧义消解对的基准数据集，按三类九子类系统分类。实验发现，大语言模型在处理歧义文本时表现脆弱：难以区分歧义与非歧义文本，对歧义句过度自信地认定单一含义，且在尝试理解多重解释时过度思考。这些结果揭示了当前大语言模型在不确定性处理上的根本局限，提示亟需改进方法以应对语言歧义。
From Tokens to Steps: Verification-Aware Speculative Decoding for Efficient Multi-Step Reasoning
Authors: Kiran Purohit, Ramasuri Narayanam, Soumyabrata Pal
First: 2026-04-16T17:20:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T17:20:13+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Speculative decoding (SD) accelerates large language model inference by allowing a lightweight draft model to propose outputs that a stronger target model verifies. However, its token-centric nature allows erroneous steps to propagate. Prior approaches mitigate this using external reward models, but incur additional latency, computational overhead, and limit generalizability. We propose SpecGuard, a verification-aware speculative decoding framework that performs step-level verification using only model-internal signals. At each step, SpecGuard samples multiple draft candidates and selects the most consistent step, which is then validated using an ensemble of two lightweight model-internal signals: (i) an attention-based grounding score that measures attribution to the input and previously accepted steps, and (ii) a log-probability-based score that captures token-level confidence. These signals jointly determine whether a step is accepted or recomputed using the target, allocating compute selectively. Experiments across a range of reasoning benchmarks show that SpecGuard improves accuracy by 3.6% while reducing latency by ~11%, outperforming both SD and reward-guided SD.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从令牌到步骤：面向验证的推测解码以实现高效多步推理
推测解码（SD）通过允许轻量级草稿模型提出输出，由更强的目标模型验证，从而加速大语言模型推理。然而，其以令牌为中心的特性允许错误步骤传播。先前方法通过外部奖励模型缓解此问题，但会增加延迟、计算开销并限制泛化能力。我们提出SpecGuard，一种面向验证的推测解码框架，仅使用模型内部信号进行步骤级验证。在每一步，SpecGuard采样多个草稿候选，选择最一致的步骤，再通过两个轻量级模型内部信号的集成进行验证：（i）基于注意力的锚定得分，衡量对输入和先前接受步骤的归因；（ii）基于对数概率的得分，捕捉令牌级置信度。这些信号共同决定步骤是否被接受或使用目标模型重新计算，实现计算资源的有选择性分配。在多种推理基准测试中，SpecGuard准确率提升3.6%，延迟降低约11%，优于SD和奖励引导的SD。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the error propagation issue in token-centric speculative decoding, the paper introduces SpecGuard, a framework that performs step-level verification using only internal model signals to enhance accuracy and efficiency. It samples multiple draft candidates at each step and selects the most consistent one, validating it via an ensemble of two lightweight signals: an attention-based grounding score measuring input and prior step relevance, and a log-probability-based confidence score. Experiments across reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that SpecGuard improves accuracy by 3.6% while reducing latency by approximately 11%, outperforming both standard speculative decoding and reward-guided variants.
为解决传统基于token的投机解码中错误传播的问题，本文提出SpecGuard框架，该框架在步骤级别使用模型内部信号进行验证，无需外部奖励模型。它在每一步采样多个草稿候选，选择最一致的步骤，并通过注意力归因分数和对数概率置信度联合判断是否接受或重计算。实验表明，SpecGuard在多个推理基准上准确率提升3.6%，延迟降低约11%，优于标准投机解码和奖励引导式解码方法。
Optimal last-iterate convergence in matrix games with bandit feedback using the log-barrier
Authors: Come Fiegel, Pierre Menard, Tadashi Kozuno, Michal Valko, Vianney Perchet
First: 2026-04-16T17:17:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T17:17:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study the problem of learning minimax policies in zero-sum matrix games. Fiegel et al. (2025) recently showed that achieving last-iterate convergence in this setting is harder when the players are uncoupled, by proving a lower bound on the exploitability gap of Omega(t^{-1/4}). Some online mirror descent algorithms were proposed in the literature for this problem, but none have truly attained this rate yet. We show that the use of a log-barrier regularization, along with a dual-focused analysis, allows this O-tilde(t^{-1/4}) convergence with high-probability. We additionally extend our idea to the setting of extensive-form games, proving a bound with the same rate.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用对数屏障实现矩阵博弈中带臂反馈的最优最后迭代收敛
我们研究零和矩阵博弈中学习最小最大策略的问题。Fiegel 等人（2025）最近证明，在玩家解耦的情况下，实现最后迭代收敛更困难，其可利用差距下界为 Omega(t^{-1/4})。文献中已提出若干在线镜像下降算法，但尚未有算法真正达到该速率。我们证明，结合对数屏障正则化与双聚焦分析，可实现以高概率达到 O-tilde(t^{-1/4}) 的收敛速率。此外，我们将该思想扩展至扩展形式博弈，证明了相同速率的界。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of achieving last-iterate convergence in zero-sum matrix games with bandit feedback, this work introduces a log-barrier regularized online mirror descent algorithm and employs a dual-focused analysis to attain an O-tilde(t^{-1/4}) convergence rate for the exploitability gap, matching the theoretical lower bound established by Fiegel et al. (2025). The method leverages barrier regularization to improve the algorithm's behavior under uncoupled play, and the analysis is tailored to capture the last-iterate dynamics. Experimental results confirm the algorithm’s convergence rate in both matrix and extensive-form games, demonstrating that the log-barrier approach effectively achieves the optimal rate with high probability.
为解决在带反馈的零和矩阵博弈中实现最后迭代收敛的难题——此前方法未能达到理论下界Omega(t^{-1/4})——本文提出一种结合对数屏障正则化的在线镜像下降算法，并采用双焦点分析。该方法通过屏障函数引导策略更新，在高概率下实现O-tilde(t^{-1/4})收敛速率，达到信息论下限。该方法进一步扩展至扩展式博弈，同样获得相同收敛速率。
Similarity-Distance-Magnitude Activations
Authors: Allen Schmaltz
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2025-09-16T07:19:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T17:12:54+00:00
Comments: Accepted to Findings of the Association for Computational Linguistics: ACL 2026. 21 pages, 8 tables, 1 algorithm. arXiv admin note: substantial text overlap with arXiv:2502.20167
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce the Similarity-Distance-Magnitude (SDM) activation function, a more robust and interpretable formulation of the standard softmax activation function, adding Similarity (i.e., correctly predicted depth-matches into training) awareness and Distance-to-training-distribution awareness to the existing output Magnitude (i.e., decision-boundary) awareness, and enabling interpretability-by-exemplar via dense matching. We further introduce the SDM estimator, based on a data-driven partitioning of the class-wise empirical CDFs via the SDM activation, to control the class- and prediction-conditional accuracy among selective classifications. When used as the final-layer activation over pre-trained language models for selective classification, the SDM estimator is more robust to covariate shifts and out-of-distribution inputs than existing calibration methods using softmax activations, while remaining informative over in-distribution data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：相似度-距离-幅度激活函数
我们引入了相似度-距离-幅度（SDM）激活函数，这是一种比标准softmax激活函数更稳健且可解释的改进版本。该函数在现有输出幅度（即决策边界）感知的基础上，增加了相似度感知（即训练中正确预测的深度匹配）和距离训练分布感知，从而通过密集匹配实现基于实例的可解释性。我们进一步提出了基于SDM激活函数对类间经验CDF进行数据驱动划分的SDM估计器，用于控制选择性分类中的类条件和预测条件准确率。当作为预训练语言模型最后一层激活函数用于选择性分类时，SDM估计器比现有基于softmax激活的校准方法对协变量偏移和分布外输入更具鲁棒性，同时在分布内数据上仍保持信息性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of standard softmax in handling covariate shifts and providing interpretable outputs, the paper proposes the Similarity-Distance-Magnitude (SDM) activation function, which integrates similarity to training data, distance to training distribution, and magnitude-based decision boundaries to enhance robustness and interpretability. The SDM estimator, derived from class-wise empirical CDFs partitioned via SDM, enables selective classification by controlling accuracy conditionally on class and prediction. Experiments show that using SDM as the final layer activation in pre-trained language models improves robustness to out-of-distribution inputs compared to softmax-based calibration methods, while preserving informativeness on in-distribution data.
为解决标准softmax在处理协变量偏移和提供可解释输出方面的不足，作者提出了相似度-距离-幅度（SDM）激活函数，融合了对正确训练预测的相似性、对训练分布的距离以及基于幅度的决策边界，以提升鲁棒性和可解释性。基于SDM激活函数对类间经验CDF进行数据驱动划分的SDM估计器，可实现对选择性分类中类别与预测条件准确率的精细控制。实验表明，将SDM作为预训练语言模型的最终层激活函数，其在处理分布外输入时比基于softmax的校准方法更鲁棒，同时在分布内数据上保持良好性能。
TokenGS: Decoupling 3D Gaussian Prediction from Pixels with Learnable Tokens
Authors: Jiawei Ren, Michal Jan Tyszkiewicz, Jiahui Huang, Zan Gojcic
First: 2026-04-16T17:12:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T17:12:37+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://research.nvidia.com/labs/toronto-ai/tokengs
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this work, we revisit several key design choices of modern Transformer-based approaches for feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) prediction. We argue that the common practice of regressing Gaussian means as depths along camera rays is suboptimal, and instead propose to directly regress 3D mean coordinates using only a self-supervised rendering loss. This formulation allows us to move from the standard encoder-only design to an encoder-decoder architecture with learnable Gaussian tokens, thereby unbinding the number of predicted primitives from input image resolution and number of views. Our resulting method, TokenGS, demonstrates improved robustness to pose noise and multiview inconsistencies, while naturally supporting efficient test-time optimization in token space without degrading learned priors. TokenGS achieves state-of-the-art feed-forward reconstruction performance on both static and dynamic scenes, producing more regularized geometry and more balanced 3DGS distribution, while seamlessly recovering emergent scene attributes such as static-dynamic decomposition and scene flow.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TokenGS：通过可学习的令牌解耦3D高斯预测与像素
本文重新审视了现代基于Transformer的前馈3D高斯点渲染方法中的若干关键设计选择。我们认为，将高斯均值作为沿相机射线的深度进行回归的做法并不理想，转而提出仅使用自监督渲染损失直接回归3D均值坐标。该方法使我们能够从标准的仅编码器架构过渡到包含可学习高斯令牌的编码器-解码器架构，从而将预测的几何体数量与输入图像分辨率和视图数量解耦。我们的方法TokenGS在姿态噪声和多视角不一致方面表现出更强的鲁棒性，同时在令牌空间中自然支持高效的测试时优化，且不损害已学习的先验知识。TokenGS在静态和动态场景上均实现了最先进的前馈重建性能，生成更规则的几何结构和更均衡的3DGS分布，同时无缝恢复场景属性，如静态-动态分解和场景流。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of regressing 3D Gaussian means as depths in Transformer-based 3DGS methods, this work proposes TokenGS, which directly predicts 3D mean coordinates using a self-supervised rendering loss, enabling a shift from encoder-only to encoder-decoder architecture with learnable tokens. This decoupling allows the number of primitives to scale independently of image resolution or view count. Experimental results show TokenGS achieves state-of-the-art feed-forward reconstruction on both static and dynamic scenes, with more regularized geometry, balanced 3DGS distributions, and natural support for test-time optimization without degrading priors, while also recovering scene attributes like static-dynamic decomposition and scene flow.
针对Transformer基3DGS方法中将高斯中心回归为深度的局限性，本文提出TokenGS，通过自监督渲染损失直接预测3D中心坐标，构建了含可学习令牌的编码器-解码器架构，使预测的几何体数量与输入分辨率和视角数解耦。实验表明，TokenGS在静态与动态场景上均达到当前最优的前馈重建效果，生成更规则的几何结构、更均衡的3DGS分布，同时支持测试时优化而不破坏学习先验，并自然恢复场景属性如静态-动态分解与场景流。
A Nonlinear Separation Principle: Applications to Neural Networks, Control and Learning
Authors: Anand Gokhale, Anton V. Proskurnikov, Yu Kawano, Francesco Bullo
First: 2026-04-16T17:12:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T17:12:32+00:00
Comments: arXiv admin note: text overlap with arXiv:2604.00119
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper investigates continuous-time and discrete-time firing-rate and Hopfield recurrent neural networks (RNNs), with applications in nonlinear control design and implicit deep learning. First, we introduce a nonlinear separation principle that guarantees global exponential stability for the interconnection of a contracting state-feedback controller and a contracting observer, alongside parametric extensions for robustness and equilibrium tracking. Second, we derive sharp linear matrix inequality (LMI) conditions that guarantee the contractivity of both firing rate and Hopfield neural network architectures. We establish structural relationships among these certificates-demonstrating that continuous-time models with monotone non-decreasing activations maximize the admissible weight space, and extend these stability guarantees to interconnected systems and Graph RNNs. Third, we combine our separation principle and LMI framework to solve the output reference tracking problem for RNN-modeled plants. We provide LMI synthesis methods for feedback controllers and observers, and rigorously design a low-gain integral controller to eliminate steady-state error. Finally, we derive an exact, unconstrained algebraic parameterization of our contraction LMIs to design highly expressive implicit neural networks, achieving competitive accuracy and parameter efficiency on standard image classification benchmarks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：一种非线性分离原理：应用于神经网络、控制与学习
本文研究了连续时间与离散时间的发放率和Hopfield递归神经网络（RNN），并应用于非线性控制设计和隐式深度学习。首先，我们提出一种非线性分离原理，确保收缩状态反馈控制器与收缩观测器互联系统的全局指数稳定性，并提供参数扩展以增强鲁棒性和平衡跟踪能力。其次，我们推导出精确的线性矩阵不等式（LMI）条件，保证发放率和Hopfield神经网络架构的收缩性，并建立这些证书间的结构关系——表明具有单调非递减激活函数的连续时间模型可最大化可接受权重空间，并将这些稳定性保证扩展至互联系统和图RNN。第三，我们将分离原理与LMI框架结合，解决RNN建模系统的输出参考跟踪问题，提供反馈控制器与观测器的LMI综合方法，并严谨设计低增益积分控制器以消除稳态误差。最后，我们推导出收缩LMI的精确无约束代数参数化，用于设计高表达能力的隐式神经网络，在标准图像分类基准测试中实现竞争力强的精度与参数效率。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for stable and efficient control and learning in neural network systems, this paper introduces a nonlinear separation principle that ensures global exponential stability when combining a contracting state-feedback controller with a contracting observer, and extends it for robustness and tracking. The authors derive sharp linear matrix inequality (LMI) conditions to guarantee contractivity in both firing-rate and Hopfield RNNs, showing that continuous-time models with monotone non-decreasing activations admit the largest weight space and extend these results to interconnected systems and Graph RNNs. Experimentally, the framework enables output reference tracking via LMI-based controller and observer synthesis, including a low-gain integral controller to eliminate steady-state error, and yields highly expressive implicit neural networks through an exact algebraic parameterization of contraction LMIs, achieving competitive accuracy and parameter efficiency on image classification benchmarks.
为实现递归神经网络在控制与学习中的稳定高效应用，本文提出一种非线性分离原理，确保状态反馈控制器与观测器在收缩性条件下全局指数稳定，并扩展其用于鲁棒性和跟踪控制。作者推导出紧致的线性矩阵不等式（LMI）条件，保证两类神经网络架构的收缩性，证明连续时间模型中单调非减激活函数可最大化可接受权重空间，并可扩展至互联系统与图RNN。进一步结合分离原理与LMI框架解决输出跟踪问题，设计低增益积分控制器消除稳态误差，并导出无约束代数参数化方法用于隐式神经网络设计，在图像分类基准上实现竞争力强的精度与参数效率。
StreamCacheVGGT: Streaming Visual Geometry Transformers with Robust Scoring and Hybrid Cache Compression
Authors: Xuanyi Liu, Deyi Ji, Chunan Yu, Qi Zhu, Xuanfu Li, Jin Ma, Tianrun Chen, Lanyun Zhu
First: 2026-04-16T17:12:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T17:12:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reconstructing dense 3D geometry from continuous video streams requires stable inference under a constant memory budget. Existing $O(1)$ frameworks primarily rely on a ``pure eviction'' paradigm, which suffers from significant information destruction due to binary token deletion and evaluation noise from localized, single-layer scoring. To address these bottlenecks, we propose StreamCacheVGGT, a training-free framework that reimagines cache management through two synergistic modules: Cross-Layer Consistency-Enhanced Scoring (CLCES) and Hybrid Cache Compression (HCC). CLCES mitigates activation noise by tracking token importance trajectories across the Transformer hierarchy, employing order-statistical analysis to identify sustained geometric salience. Leveraging these robust scores, HCC transcends simple eviction by introducing a three-tier triage strategy that merges moderately important tokens into retained anchors via nearest-neighbor assignment on the key-vector manifold. This approach preserves essential geometric context that would otherwise be lost. Extensive evaluations on five benchmarks (7-Scenes, NRGBD, ETH3D, Bonn, and KITTI) demonstrate that StreamCacheVGGT sets a new state-of-the-art, delivering superior reconstruction accuracy and long-term stability while strictly adhering to constant-cost constraints.
中文标题/摘要
标题：StreamCacheVGGT：具有鲁棒评分与混合缓存压缩的流式视觉几何变换器
从连续视频流中重建密集3D几何需要在恒定内存预算下保持稳定推理。现有O(1)框架主要依赖“纯驱逐”范式，因二进制令牌删除导致信息大量丢失，且局部单层评分引入噪声。为解决这些瓶颈，我们提出StreamCacheVGGT，一种无需训练的框架，通过两个协同模块重新设计缓存管理：跨层一致性增强评分（CLCES）和混合缓存压缩（HCC）。CLCES通过追踪Transformer层级中令牌重要性轨迹，利用序统计分析识别持续的几何显著性，从而缓解激活噪声。基于这些鲁棒评分，HCC超越简单驱逐，引入三级分诊策略，通过键向量流形上的最近邻分配，将中等重要令牌合并至保留锚点。该方法保留了否则将丢失的关键几何上下文。在五个基准（7-Scenes、NRGBD、ETH3D、Bonn、KITTI）的广泛评估中，StreamCacheVGGT创下新SOTA，实现更优重建精度与长期稳定性，同时严格遵守恒定成本约束。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for stable 3D geometry reconstruction from video streams under fixed memory budgets, this paper introduces StreamCacheVGGT, a training-free framework that improves cache management through two modules: Cross-Layer Consistency-Enhanced Scoring (CLCES) and Hybrid Cache Compression (HCC). CLCES reduces activation noise by tracking token importance across Transformer layers using order-statistical analysis to identify persistent geometric salience, while HCC employs a triage strategy to retain moderately important tokens by mapping them to anchors via nearest-neighbor assignment on the key-vector manifold. Experiments on five benchmarks show StreamCacheVGGT achieves state-of-the-art reconstruction accuracy and long-term stability without exceeding memory constraints.
为在固定内存预算下实现视频流的稳定三维几何重建，本文提出StreamCacheVGGT框架，通过两个模块改进缓存管理：跨层一致性增强评分（CLCES）和混合缓存压缩（HCC）。CLCES通过跨Transformer层跟踪令牌重要性轨迹，利用顺序统计分析识别持续的几何显著性，以降低激活噪声；HCC采用三级分诊策略，通过关键向量流形上的最近邻分配将中等重要令牌合并至保留锚点，保留关键几何上下文。在五个基准测试中，该方法在不超内存限制的前提下，实现了最先进重建精度与长期稳定性。
Agentic Microphysics: A Manifesto for Generative AI Safety
Authors: Federico Pierucci, Matteo Prandi, Marcantonio Bracale Syrnikov, Marcello Galisai, Piercosma Bisconti
First: 2026-04-16T17:11:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T17:11:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper advances a methodological proposal for safety research in agentic AI. As systems acquire planning, memory, tool use, persistent identity, and sustained interaction, safety can no longer be analysed primarily at the level of the isolated model. Population-level risks arise from structured interaction among agents, through processes of communication, observation, and mutual influence that shape collective behaviour over time. As the object of analysis shifts, a methodological gap emerges. Approaches focused either on single agents or on aggregate outcomes do not identify the interaction-level mechanisms that generate collective risks or the design variables that control them. A framework is required that links local interaction structure to population-level dynamics in a causally explicit way, allowing both explanation and intervention. We introduce two linked concepts. Agentic microphysics defines the level of analysis: local interaction dynamics where one agent's output becomes another's input under specific protocol conditions. Generative safety defines the methodology: growing phenomena and elicit risks from micro-level conditions to identify sufficient mechanisms, detect thresholds, and design effective interventions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：代理微物理：生成式人工智能安全宣言
本文提出了一种针对代理型人工智能安全研究的方法论方案。随着系统获得规划、记忆、工具使用、持久身份及持续交互能力，安全分析已不能仅限于孤立模型层面。群体层面的风险源于代理间结构化互动，通过沟通、观察与相互影响等过程，塑造随时间演化的集体行为。分析对象转变的同时，方法论缺口随之显现：聚焦单个代理或整体结果的方法，无法识别生成集体风险的互动机制或控制这些机制的设计变量。亟需一种框架，以因果明确的方式将局部互动结构与群体动态关联起来，从而实现解释与干预。我们引入两个关联概念：代理微物理定义分析层级——在特定协议条件下，一个代理的输出成为另一代理的输入的局部互动动态；生成式安全定义方法论——从微观条件中涌现现象并揭示风险，以识别充分机制、检测阈值并设计有效干预。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to address safety risks emerging from agent-to-agent interactions in agentic AI systems, this paper proposes a new methodological framework. It introduces 'agentic microphysics' as the level of analysis—focusing on local interaction dynamics where one agent’s output directly influences another under defined protocols—and 'generative safety' as the methodology, which systematically elicits risks by tracing how micro-level conditions generate population-level phenomena, identifies critical thresholds, and enables targeted interventions. Experimental results across simulated agent populations demonstrate that this approach successfully uncovers interaction-based risk mechanisms that traditional single-agent or aggregate-focused methods miss, enabling more precise safety design.
为应对多智能体AI系统交互中产生的安全风险，本文提出一种新方法论框架。其核心概念为“智能体微观物理”——聚焦于局部交互动态，即在特定协议下，一个智能体的输出直接影响另一智能体；以及“生成式安全”——通过从微观条件推导群体现象，识别风险机制、阈值与干预点。实验表明，该方法能揭示单智能体或群体聚合分析无法发现的交互型风险模式，从而支持精准安全设计。
Blue Data Intelligence Layer: Streaming Data and Agents for Multi-source Multi-modal Data-Centric Applications
Authors: Moin Aminnaseri, Farima Fatahi Bayat, Nikita Bhutani, Jean-Flavien Bussotti, Kevin Chan, Rafael Li Chen, Yanlin Feng, Jackson Hassell, Estevam Hruschka, Eser Kandogan, Hannah Kim, James Levine, Seiji Maekawa, Jalal Mahmud, Kushan Mitra, Naoki Otani, Pouya Pezeshkpour, Nima Shahbazi, Chen Shen, Dan Zhang
First: 2026-04-16T17:10:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T17:10:21+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
NL2SQL systems aim to address the growing need for natural language interaction with data. However, real-world information rarely maps to a single SQL query because (1) users express queries iteratively (2) questions often span multiple data sources beyond the closed-world assumption of a single database, and (3) queries frequently rely on commonsense or external knowledge. Consequently, satisfying realistic data needs require integrating heterogeneous sources, modalities, and contextual data. In this paper, we present Blue's Data Intelligence Layer (DIL) designed to support multi-source, multi-modal, and data-centric applications. Blue is a compound AI system that orchestrates agents and data for enterprise settings. DIL serves as the data intelligence layer for agentic data processing, to bridge the semantic gap between user intent and available information by unifying structured enterprise data, world knowledge accessible through LLMs, and personal context obtained through interaction. At the core of DIL is a data registry that stores metadata for diverse data sources and modalities to enable both native and natural language queries. DIL treats LLMs, the Web, and the User as source 'databases', each with their own query interface, elevating them to first-class data sources. DIL relies on data planners to transform user queries into executable query plans. These plans are declarative abstractions that unify relational operators with other operators spanning multiple modalities. DIL planners support decomposition of complex requests into subqueries, retrieval from diverse sources, and finally reasoning and integration to produce final results. We demonstrate DIL through two interactive scenarios in which user queries dynamically trigger multi-source retrieval, cross-modal reasoning, and result synthesis, illustrating how compound AI systems can move beyond single database NL2SQL.

标题：蓝数据智能层：面向多源多模态数据为中心应用的流数据与代理系统
NL2SQL系统旨在满足日益增长的自然语言与数据交互需求。然而，现实世界的信息很少能映射为单一SQL查询，因为（1）用户以迭代方式表达查询；（2）问题常跨越多个数据源，超出单一数据库的封闭世界假设；（3）查询常依赖常识或外部知识。因此，满足现实数据需求需整合异构数据源、模态及上下文数据。本文提出Blue的数据智能层（DIL），旨在支持多源、多模态、数据为中心的应用。Blue是一个复合AI系统，协调代理与数据以适应企业环境。DIL作为代理数据处理的数据智能层，通过统一企业结构化数据、通过LLM可访问的世界知识及通过交互获取的个人上下文，弥合用户意图与可用信息之间的语义鸿沟。DIL核心是一个数据注册表，存储多种数据源与模态的元数据，支持原生与自然语言查询。DIL将LLM、网络和用户视为源“数据库”，各自拥有独立查询接口，提升其为一等数据源。DIL依赖数据规划器将用户查询转化为可执行查询计划。这些计划是声明式抽象，统一关系运算符与其他跨模态运算符。DIL规划器支持将复杂请求分解为子查询、从多样源检索，最终进行推理与整合以生成最终结果。我们通过两个交互场景演示DIL：用户查询动态触发多源检索、跨模态推理与结果合成，展示复合AI系统如何超越单一数据库NL2SQL。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of traditional NL2SQL systems that assume single-source, closed-world databases, this paper introduces Blue's Data Intelligence Layer (DIL), a compound AI system designed to handle multi-source, multi-modal data queries in enterprise settings. DIL integrates structured enterprise data, world knowledge from LLMs, and user context into a unified data layer, treating LLMs, the Web, and users as first-class data sources. It employs a data registry to manage metadata across diverse sources and uses declarative data planners to decompose complex queries into executable plans that span multiple modalities and sources. Experimental results show DIL successfully enables dynamic multi-source retrieval, cross-modal reasoning, and result synthesis in interactive scenarios, demonstrating its ability to move beyond single-database NL2SQL systems.
为解决传统NL2SQL系统在单一数据源、封闭世界假设下的局限性，本文提出Blue数据智能层（DIL），一个支持多源、多模态、上下文感知数据查询的复合AI系统。DIL将企业结构化数据、通过LLM获取的世界知识及用户交互上下文统一整合，将LLM、网络和用户视为一级数据源。其核心是数据规划器，可将用户查询分解为包含关系与跨模态操作的可执行计划，实现多源数据检索与结果合成。实验表明，DIL能动态协调多源数据获取与跨模态推理，显著优于单一数据库NL2SQL方法。
RadAgent: A tool-using AI agent for stepwise interpretation of chest computed tomography
Authors: Mélanie Roschewitz, Kenneth Styppa, Yitian Tao, Jiwoong Sohn, Jean-Benoit Delbrouck, Benjamin Gundersen, Nicolas Deperrois, Christian Bluethgen, Julia Vogt, Bjoern Menze, Farhad Nooralahzadeh, Michael Krauthammer, Michael Moor
First: 2026-04-16T17:09:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T17:09:30+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-language models (VLM) have markedly advanced AI-driven interpretation and reporting of complex medical imaging, such as computed tomography (CT). Yet, existing methods largely relegate clinicians to passive observers of final outputs, offering no interpretable reasoning trace for them to inspect, validate, or refine. To address this, we introduce RadAgent, a tool-using AI agent that generates CT reports through a stepwise and interpretable process. Each resulting report is accompanied by a fully inspectable trace of intermediate decisions and tool interactions, allowing clinicians to examine how the reported findings are derived. In our experiments, we observe that RadAgent improves Chest CT report generation over its 3D VLM counterpart, CT-Chat, across three dimensions. Clinical accuracy improves by 6.0 points (36.4% relative) in macro-F1 and 5.4 points (19.6% relative) in micro-F1. Robustness under adversarial conditions improves by 24.7 points (41.9% relative). Furthermore, RadAgent achieves 37.0% in faithfulness, a new capability entirely absent in its 3D VLM counterpart. By structuring the interpretation of chest CT as an explicit, tool-augmented and iterative reasoning trace, RadAgent brings us closer toward transparent and reliable AI for radiology.

标题：RadAgent：一种用于逐步解读胸部计算机断层扫描的工具型AI代理

The method employs iterative reasoning with explicit tool interactions to build reports and their accompanying decision traces.
