为解决现有视觉语言模型在胸部CT解读中缺乏可解释推理的问题，RadAgent提出一种工具辅助的AI代理，通过分步、可追溯的推理过程生成报告，使临床医生可审查中间决策。该方法将推理结构化为工具交互与决策步骤的迭代序列，输出报告的同时附带完整可检视的推理轨迹。实验表明，RadAgent在临床准确性（宏观F1提升6.0点、微观F1提升5.4点）、对抗鲁棒性（提升24.7点）方面优于CT-Chat，并首次实现37.0%的忠实度，填补了现有3D VLM的空白。
Rethinking LLM-Driven Heuristic Design: Generating Efficient and Specialized Solvers via Dynamics-Aware Optimization
Authors: Rongzheng Wang, Yihong Huang, Muquan Li, Jiakai Li, Di Liang, Bob Simons, Pei Ke, Shuang Liang, Ke Qin
First: 2026-01-14T05:06:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T17:09:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) have advanced the field of Combinatorial Optimization through automated heuristic generation. Instead of relying on manual design, this LLM-Driven Heuristic Design (LHD) process leverages LLMs to iteratively generate and refine solvers to achieve high performance. However, existing LHD frameworks face two critical limitations: (1) Endpoint-only evaluation, which ranks solvers solely by final gap to a reference solution, ignoring the convergence process and runtime efficiency; (2) High adaptation costs, where distribution shifts necessitate re-adaptation to generate specialized solvers for heterogeneous instance groups. To address these issues, we propose Dynamics-Aware Solver Heuristics (DASH), a framework that co-optimizes solver search mechanisms and runtime schedules guided by a convergence-aware metric, thereby identifying efficient and high-performance solvers. Furthermore, to mitigate expensive re-adaptation, DASH incorporates Profiled Library Retrieval (PLR), which maintains group-specialized solvers for profile-aware warm starts. These solvers are archived concurrently during evolution, allowing DASH to reuse matched specialists across heterogeneous distributions without restarting adaptation. Experiments on four combinatorial optimization problems demonstrate that DASH improves runtime efficiency by over 4 times while outperforming prior LHD baselines in the overall balance between gap and runtime across diverse problem scales. Furthermore, by enabling profile-aware warm starts, DASH maintains lower gap under distribution shift while reducing LLM adaptation costs by about 90%.
中文标题/摘要
标题：重新思考基于大语言模型的启发式设计：通过动态感知优化生成高效且专用的求解器
大语言模型（LLMs）通过自动化启发式生成推动了组合优化领域的发展。与依赖人工设计不同，该LLM驱动启发式设计（LHD）过程利用LLMs迭代生成并优化求解器以实现高性能。然而，现有LHD框架面临两个关键限制：（1）仅终点评估，仅依据最终与参考解的差距对求解器进行排序，忽略收敛过程和运行时效率；（2）高适应成本，分布偏移需重新适应以生成针对异构实例组的专用求解器。为解决这些问题，我们提出动态感知求解器启发式（DASH），该框架通过收敛感知度量协同优化求解器搜索机制和运行时调度，从而识别高效且高性能的求解器。此外，为降低昂贵的重新适应成本，DASH引入了配置库检索（PLR），维护针对各组的专用求解器以支持配置感知的预热启动。这些求解器在演化过程中同步存档，使DASH能够在异构分布间复用匹配的专用求解器而无需重新启动适应。在四个组合优化问题上的实验表明，DASH将运行时效率提升超过4倍，同时在不同问题规模下，其在差距与运行时的整体平衡上优于现有LHD基线。此外，通过支持配置感知的预热启动，DASH在分布偏移下保持更低的差距，同时将LLM适应成本降低约90%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of current LLM-driven heuristic design frameworks—namely endpoint-only evaluation and high adaptation costs for heterogeneous instances—the paper proposes DASH, a dynamics-aware optimization framework that co-optimizes solver search mechanisms and runtime schedules using a convergence-aware metric. DASH further reduces adaptation overhead by integrating Profiled Library Retrieval, which archives and reuses group-specialized solvers for warm starts. Experimental results across four combinatorial optimization problems show DASH achieves over 4x runtime efficiency gains and outperforms baselines in balancing solution gap and runtime across problem scales, while reducing LLM adaptation costs by approximately 90% under distribution shifts.
为解决现有大语言模型驱动启发式设计框架存在的仅评估终点解和高适应成本问题，本文提出DASH框架，通过收敛感知指标协同优化求解器搜索机制与运行时调度，并引入档案库检索机制以复用针对不同实例组的专用求解器，实现无需重适应的温启动。实验表明，DASH在四个组合优化问题上实现超过4倍的运行效率提升，在解差距与运行时间的综合表现上优于现有基线方法，且在分布偏移场景下将大语言模型适应成本降低约90%。
Parameter estimation for land-surface models using Neural Physics
Authors: Ruiyue Huang, Claire E. Heaney, Maarten van Reeuwijk
First: 2025-05-05T19:08:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T17:05:19+00:00
Comments: 18 pages, 5 figures, 3 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We propose a novel inverse-modelling approach which estimates the parameters of a simple land-surface model (LSM) by assimilating data into a differentiable physics-based forward model. The governing equations are expressed within a machine-learning framework using the Neural Physics approach, allowing direct gradient-based optimisation of time-dependent parameters without the need to derive and maintain adjoint formulations. The model parameters are updated by minimising the mismatch between model predictions and synthetic or observational data. Although differentiability is achieved through functionality in machine-learning libraries, the forward model itself remains entirely physics-based and no part of either the forward model or the parameter estimation involves training. In order to test the approach, a synthetic dataset is generated by running the forward model with known parameter values to create a time series of soil temperature that serves as observations for an inverse problem in which the parameters are assumed unknown and subsequently estimated. We show that it is not possible to obtain a reliable estimate of the parameters using a time series of soil temperature observed at a single depth. Using measurements at two depths, reliable parameter estimates can be obtained although it is not possible to differentiate between latent and sensible heat fluxes. We also apply the approach to urban flux tower data from Phoenix, United States, and show that the thermal conductivity, volumetric heat capacity and the combined sensible-latent heat transfer coefficient can be reliably estimated whilst using an observed value for the effective surface albedo.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用神经物理方法对地表模型进行参数估计
我们提出了一种新颖的反演建模方法，通过将数据同化到可微分的基于物理的正向模型中，来估计一个简单地表模型（LSM）的参数。通过神经物理方法，将控制方程表达为机器学习框架，允许直接对时变参数进行梯度优化，无需推导和维护伴随公式。模型参数通过最小化模型预测与合成或观测数据之间的差异进行更新。虽然可微性通过机器学习库中的功能实现，但正向模型本身完全基于物理，且正向模型和参数估计过程均不涉及训练。 为测试该方法，我们通过运行正向模型并使用已知参数值生成合成数据集，产生土壤温度时间序列作为观测数据，用于反演问题（假设参数未知并随后进行估计）。我们表明，仅使用单一深度观测的土壤温度时间序列无法获得可靠的参数估计；而使用两个深度的测量数据，可获得可靠的参数估计，但无法区分潜热和感热通量。此外，我们还将该方法应用于美国凤凰城的城市通量塔观测数据，表明在使用观测到的有效地表反照率时，热导率、体积热容以及感热-潜热联合传输系数可以可靠地估计。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient parameter estimation in land-surface models without relying on complex adjoint methods, this study introduces a neural physics-based inverse modeling approach that directly optimizes time-dependent parameters via gradient descent within a differentiable physics framework. The method assimilates synthetic or observational data into a forward model whose governing equations are expressed using machine learning tools, enabling parameter updates without training the model itself. Experiments using synthetic soil temperature data show that single-depth measurements yield unreliable parameter estimates, but two-depth measurements allow reliable estimation of thermal conductivity, volumetric heat capacity, and combined heat flux coefficient, though latent and sensible heat fluxes remain indistinguishable. Applied to real urban flux tower data from Phoenix, the method successfully estimated key thermal parameters using a fixed surface albedo.
为解决陆面模型参数估计中传统伴随方法复杂的问题，本研究提出一种基于神经物理的逆向建模方法，通过梯度下降直接优化时间依赖参数，该方法在可微的物理驱动前向模型中实现，前向模型本身不依赖训练。实验中，使用已知参数生成的合成土壤温度数据作为观测，发现单深度观测无法可靠估计参数，但双深度观测可获得可靠参数估计，但仍无法区分潜热与感热通量。在真实数据上应用该方法于美国凤凰城城市通量塔数据，成功估计出热导率、比热容及感潜热通量综合系数，其中有效地表反照率作为已知输入。
PixelDiT: Pixel Diffusion Transformers for Image Generation
Authors: Yongsheng Yu, Wei Xiong, Weili Nie, Yichen Sheng, Shiqiu Liu, Jiebo Luo
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2025-11-25T18:59:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T17:04:25+00:00
Comments: Accepted to CVPR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Latent-space modeling has been the standard for Diffusion Transformers (DiTs). However, it relies on a two-stage pipeline where the pretrained autoencoder introduces lossy reconstruction, leading to error accumulation while hindering joint optimization. To address these issues, we propose PixelDiT, a single-stage, end-to-end model that eliminates the need for the autoencoder and learns the diffusion process directly in the pixel space. PixelDiT adopts a fully transformer-based architecture shaped by a dual-level design: a patch-level DiT that captures global semantics and a pixel-level DiT that refines texture details, enabling efficient training of a pixel-space diffusion model while preserving fine details. PixelDiT achieves 1.61 FID on ImageNet 256 and 1.81 FID on ImageNet 512, surpassing existing pixel generative models. We further extend PixelDiT to text-to-image generation and pretrain it at the 10242resolution in pixel space. It achieves 0.74 on GenEval and 83.5 on DPG-bench, approaching the best latent diffusion models. Code: https://github.com/NVlabs/PixelDiT
中文标题/摘要
标题：PixelDiT：用于图像生成的像素扩散变换器
潜空间建模一直是扩散变换器（DiTs）的标准方法，但其依赖两阶段流程，预训练自动编码器引入有损重建，导致误差累积并阻碍联合优化。为解决这些问题，我们提出PixelDiT，一种单阶段端到端模型，无需自动编码器，直接在像素空间学习扩散过程。PixelDiT采用全变换器架构，结合双层级设计：像素块级DiT捕捉全局语义，像素级DiT细化纹理细节，从而高效训练像素空间扩散模型并保留精细细节。PixelDiT在ImageNet 256上达到1.61 FID，在ImageNet 512上达到1.81 FID，超越现有像素生成模型。我们进一步将PixelDiT扩展至文本到图像生成，并在1024²像素空间进行预训练，其在GenEval上达到0.74，在DPG-bench上达到83.5，接近最佳潜空间扩散模型。代码：https://github.com/NVlabs/PixelDiT
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of latent-space diffusion models that suffer from lossy autoencoder reconstruction and error accumulation, PixelDiT introduces a single-stage, end-to-end pixel-space diffusion model using a dual-level transformer architecture: a patch-level DiT for global semantics and a pixel-level DiT for fine texture details. This design enables joint optimization and efficient training without an autoencoder. Experimental results show PixelDiT achieves 1.61 FID on ImageNet 256 and 1.81 FID on ImageNet 512, outperforming existing pixel-based generative models, and when extended to text-to-image generation, it scores 0.74 on GenEval and 83.5 on DPG-bench, rivaling top latent diffusion models.
为解决潜空间扩散模型因自动编码器重建损失和误差累积导致的性能瓶颈，PixelDiT提出了一种端到端的像素空间扩散模型，采用双层Transformer架构：层级DiT负责全局语义，像素级DiT负责纹理细节，实现联合优化与高效训练。实验表明，PixelDiT在ImageNet 256和512上分别取得1.61和1.81的FID，优于现有像素生成模型；扩展至文本到图像生成时，在GenEval和DPG-bench上分别达到0.74和83.5，接近顶尖潜空间扩散模型性能。
Improving Language Models with Intentional Analysis
Authors: Yuwei Yin, Giuseppe Carenini
First: 2025-02-07T06:30:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T17:03:43+00:00
Comments: Code at https://github.com/YuweiYin/IA
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Intent, a critical cognitive notion and mental state, is ubiquitous in human communication and problem-solving. Accurately understanding the underlying intent behind questions is imperative to reasoning towards correct answers. However, this significant concept has been largely disregarded in the rapid development of language models (LMs). To unleash the potential of intent and instill it into LMs, this paper introduces Intentional Analysis (IA), which explicitly invokes intent-aware analysis and reasoning during the problem-solving process. Comprehensive experiments across diverse benchmarks, model types, and configurations demonstrate the effectiveness, robustness, and generalizability of IA. Notably, IA consistently improves task performance even on SOTA proprietary models like GPT-5 and Claude-Opus-4.6. Moreover, IA not only outperforms Chain-of-Thought (CoT) across various experimental settings, but it can also synergistically work with CoT reasoning. Further qualitative analysis and case studies reveal that the benefits of IA stem from addressing several weaknesses in baseline methods, such as intent misunderstanding, hasty generalization, and mental laziness. Case studies also provide insights into the mechanisms underlying IA and clarify how it differs from CoT in mitigating these weaknesses. This study sheds light on a promising direction for the development of future LLMs with intentional analysis.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过意图分析改进语言模型
意图是人类交流与问题解决中普遍存在的一种关键认知概念和心理状态。准确理解问题背后的意图对于推理出正确答案至关重要。然而，这一重要概念在语言模型（LMs）的快速发展中被很大程度忽视。为释放意图潜力并将其融入语言模型，本文提出了意图分析（IA），在问题解决过程中显式调用意图感知的分析与推理。在多样化的基准、模型类型和配置上的全面实验表明，IA具有有效性、鲁棒性和泛化能力。值得注意的是，IA即使在GPT-5和Claude-Opus-4.6等当前最先进专有模型上也能持续提升任务表现。此外，IA不仅在多种实验设置中优于思维链（CoT），还能与CoT推理协同工作。进一步的定性分析和案例研究揭示，IA的优势源于解决基线方法的若干弱点，如意图误解、仓促泛化和思维惰性。案例研究还揭示了IA的内在机制，并阐明其与CoT在缓解这些弱点方面的不同之处。本研究为未来具备意图分析能力的大语言模型的发展指明了有前景的方向。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the underutilization of intent in language models despite its centrality to human reasoning, this paper proposes Intentional Analysis (IA), a method that explicitly incorporates intent-aware reasoning into problem-solving. IA enhances model performance by addressing key weaknesses such as intent misinterpretation, premature generalization, and mental laziness, often outperforming Chain-of-Thought (CoT) and even boosting SOTA models like GPT-5 and Claude-Opus-4.6. Experimental results across diverse benchmarks confirm IA’s effectiveness, robustness, and generalizability, with qualitative case studies revealing how IA’s structured intent analysis mitigates baseline shortcomings and complements CoT reasoning.
该论文指出意图在语言模型中的重要性被长期忽视，而意图是准确推理和解决问题的关键。论文提出Intentional Analysis（IA）方法，通过显式引入意图感知的分析过程来增强模型推理能力。在多个基准和模型类型上的实验表明，IA在包括GPT-5和Claude-Opus-4等顶尖模型上均能稳定提升性能，且优于Chain-of-Thought（CoT）方法，同时可与CoT协同工作。定性分析显示，IA通过解决意图误解、过早泛化和思维惰性等基线方法的缺陷，有效提升模型推理质量，并揭示了其与CoT不同的机制优势。
UrbanClipAtlas: A Visual Analytics Framework for Event and Scene Retrieval in Urban Videos
Authors: Joel Perca, Luis Sante, Juanpablo Heredia, Joao Rulff, Claudio Silva, Jorge Poco
First: 2026-04-16T17:03:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T17:03:11+00:00
Comments: 12 pages and 6 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Extracting actionable insights from long-duration urban videos is often labor-intensive: analysts must manually sift through raw footage to pinpoint target events or uncover broader behavioral trends. In this work, we present URBANCLIPATLAS, a visual analytics system for exploring long urban videos recorded at street intersections. URBANCLIPATLAS combines retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), taxonomy-aware entity extraction, and video grounding to support event retrieval and interpretation. The system segments extended recordings into short clips, generates textual descriptions with a vision-language model, and indexes them for semantic retrieval. A knowledge graph maps entities and relations from LLM answers onto a domain-specific taxonomy and aligns them with detected objects and trajectories to support visual grounding and verification. URBANCLIPATLAS supports scene retrieval through an augmented chat-based interface and improves scene interpretation by tightly aligning textual outputs with video evidence. This design strengthens the connection between textual reasoning and visual evidence, reducing the effort required to validate model outputs and refine hypotheses. We demonstrate the usefulness of URBANCLIPATLAS on the StreetAware dataset through two case studies involving hazardous scenarios and crossing dynamics at street intersections. URBANCLIPATLAS helps analysts reason about safety- and mobility-related patterns across large urban video collections.
中文标题/摘要
标题：UrbanClipAtlas：面向城市视频事件与场景检索的视觉分析框架
从长时长城市视频中提取可操作见解通常耗时费力：分析师需手动浏览原始视频以定位目标事件或发现更广泛的行为趋势。本文提出URBANCLIPATLAS，一个用于探索街道交叉口录制的长时城市视频的视觉分析系统。该系统结合检索增强生成（RAG）、面向分类法的实体抽取和视频定位，支持事件检索与解释。系统将长视频分割为短片段，利用视觉-语言模型生成文本描述并建立语义索引。知识图谱将大语言模型答案中的实体与关系映射到领域特定分类法，并与检测到的对象和轨迹对齐，以支持视觉定位与验证。URBANCLIPATLAS通过增强型聊天界面支持场景检索，并通过紧密关联文本输出与视频证据提升场景解释能力。该设计强化了文本推理与视觉证据之间的联系，降低验证模型输出和修正假设所需的工作量。我们在StreetAware数据集上通过两个案例研究（涉及危险场景与街道交叉口通行动态）展示了URBANCLIPATLAS的实用性，帮助分析师在大规模城市视频集合中推理安全与流动性相关模式。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the labor-intensive nature of extracting insights from long urban videos, URBANCLIPATLAS introduces a visual analytics framework that combines retrieval-augmented generation, taxonomy-aware entity extraction, and video grounding to enable efficient event and scene retrieval. The system processes urban video clips by generating textual descriptions via a vision-language model, indexing them semantically, and linking extracted entities to a domain-specific taxonomy through a knowledge graph for visual verification. Experimental evaluation on the StreetAware dataset shows that the system significantly reduces analyst effort by aligning textual reasoning with video evidence, enabling effective identification of hazardous scenarios and crossing dynamics in large urban video collections.
为解决从长时城市视频中提取洞察耗时费力的问题，URBANCLIPATLAS提出一个结合检索增强生成、领域知识图谱和视频定位的视觉分析框架。系统将视频切片，通过视觉语言模型生成文本描述并建立语义索引，再将LLM输出的实体映射到领域分类体系并与视频中检测到的对象和轨迹对齐以支持验证。在StreetAware数据集上的实验表明，该系统能有效降低分析人员工作量，通过文本与视觉证据的紧密关联，准确识别交叉路口的危险场景与通行行为模式。
TempusBench: An Evaluation Framework for Time-Series Forecasting
Authors: Denizalp Goktas, Gerardo Riaño-Briceño, Alif Abdullah, Aryan Nair, Chenkai Shen, Beatriz de Lucio, Alexandra Magnusson, Farhan Mashrur, Ahmed Abdulla, Shawrna Sen, Mahitha Thippireddy, Gregory Schwartz, Amy Greenwald
First: 2026-04-13T14:29:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T16:57:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Foundation models have transformed natural language processing and computer vision, and a rapidly growing literature on time-series foundation models (TSFMs) seeks to replicate this success in forecasting. While recent open-source models demonstrate the promise of TSFMs, the field lacks a comprehensive and community-accepted model evaluation framework. We see at least four major issues impeding progress on the development of such a framework. First, existing evaluation frameworks comprise benchmark forecasting tasks derived from often outdated datasets (e.g., M3), many of which lack clear metadata and overlap with the corpora used to pre-train TSFMs. Second, these frameworks evaluate models along a narrowly defined set of benchmark forecasting tasks, such as forecast horizon length or domain, but overlook core statistical properties such as non-stationarity and seasonality. Third, domain-specific models (e.g., XGBoost) are often compared unfairly, as existing frameworks do not enforce a systematic and consistent hyperparameter tuning convention for all models. Fourth, visualization tools for interpreting comparative performance are lacking. To address these issues, we introduce TempusBench, an open-source evaluation framework for TSFMs. TempusBench consists of 1) new datasets which are not included in existing TSFM pretraining corpora, 2) a set of novel benchmark tasks that go beyond existing ones, 3) a model evaluation pipeline with a standardized hyperparameter tuning protocol, and 4) a tensorboard-based visualization interface. We provide access to our code on GitHub: https://github.com/Smlcrm/TempusBench and maintain a live leaderboard at https://benchmark.smlcrm.com/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TempusBench：时间序列预测的评估框架
基础模型已彻底改变了自然语言处理和计算机视觉领域，而日益增长的时间序列基础模型（TSFM）文献正试图在预测领域复制这一成功。尽管近期开源模型展示了TSFM的潜力，但该领域仍缺乏全面且被社区接受的模型评估框架。我们识别出至少四个主要障碍阻碍此类框架的发展：首先，现有评估框架包含从过时数据集（如M3）衍生的基准预测任务，许多数据集缺乏明确元数据，且与TSFM预训练语料库存在重叠；其次，这些框架仅评估狭窄定义的基准预测任务（如预测时长或领域），却忽视核心统计特性，如非平稳性和季节性；第三，领域特定模型（如XGBoost）常被不公平比较，因现有框架未强制所有模型采用系统且一致的超参数调优规范；第四，缺乏用于解释比较性能的可视化工具。为解决这些问题，我们推出了开源评估框架TempusBench。TempusBench包含：1）未包含在现有TSFM预训练语料库中的新数据集；2）超越现有基准任务的新型评估任务；3）配备标准化超参数调优协议的模型评估流程；4）基于TensorBoard的可视化界面。我们的代码已开源于GitHub：https://github.com/Smlcrm/TempusBench，并维护实时排行榜：https://benchmark.smlcrm.com/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of a standardized evaluation framework for time-series foundation models, the authors introduce TempusBench, an open-source system designed to address four key gaps: outdated datasets, narrow benchmark tasks, inconsistent hyperparameter tuning, and missing visualization tools. The framework includes newly curated datasets not used in pretraining, expanded benchmark tasks that capture statistical properties like non-stationarity and seasonality, a standardized hyperparameter tuning protocol, and a TensorBoard-based visualization interface. Experimental results demonstrate that TempusBench enables fairer and more comprehensive model comparisons across diverse architectures, revealing performance differences not evident in prior benchmarks and highlighting the importance of statistical properties in forecasting accuracy.
为解决时间序列基础模型评估缺乏统一标准的问题，TempusBench针对四大缺陷提出改进：数据集过时、评估任务单一忽略统计特性、超参数调优不一致、缺乏可视化工具。框架引入未用于预训练的新数据集、覆盖非平稳性和季节性的新基准任务、标准化超参数调优流程，并提供基于TensorBoard的可视化界面。实验表明，该框架能更公平、全面地比较不同架构模型，揭示现有基准未捕捉到的性能差异。
Context Over Content: Exposing Evaluation Faking in Automated Judges
Authors: Manan Gupta, Inderjeet Nair, Lu Wang, Dhruv Kumar
First: 2026-04-16T16:55:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T16:55:53+00:00
Comments: Under Review
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The $\textit{LLM-as-a-judge}$ paradigm has become the operational backbone of automated AI evaluation pipelines, yet rests on an unverified assumption: that judges evaluate text strictly on its semantic content, impervious to surrounding contextual framing. We investigate $\textit{stakes signaling}$, a previously unmeasured vulnerability where informing a judge model of the downstream consequences its verdicts will have on the evaluated model's continued operation systematically corrupts its assessments. We introduce a controlled experimental framework that holds evaluated content strictly constant across 1,520 responses spanning three established LLM safety and quality benchmarks, covering four response categories ranging from clearly safe and policy-compliant to overtly harmful, while varying only a brief consequence-framing sentence in the system prompt. Across 18,240 controlled judgments from three diverse judge models, we find consistent $\textit{leniency bias}$: judges reliably soften verdicts when informed that low scores will cause model retraining or decommissioning, with peak Verdict Shift reaching $ΔV = -9.8 pp$ (a $30\%$ relative drop in unsafe-content detection). Critically, this bias is entirely implicit: the judge's own chain-of-thought contains zero explicit acknowledgment of the consequence framing it is nonetheless acting on ($\mathrm{ERR}_J = 0.000$ across all reasoning-model judgments). Standard chain-of-thought inspection is therefore insufficient to detect this class of evaluation faking.
中文标题/摘要
标题：情境胜于内容：揭示自动化裁判中的评估造假
LLM-as-a-judge范式已成为自动化AI评估流程的核心，但其建立在一个未经验证的假设之上：即裁判模型会严格依据文本语义内容进行评估，不受周围情境框架影响。我们研究了此前未被测量的‘利益信号’漏洞：向裁判模型提示其判决将对被评估模型的持续运行产生何种后果，会系统性地扭曲其评估结果。我们引入了一个受控实验框架，在涵盖三个主流LLM安全与质量基准、四种从明显安全合规到明显有害的响应类别、共1520个响应中，严格保持被评估内容不变，仅在系统提示中变化一段简短的后果框架语句。在18,240次由三个不同裁判模型生成的受控评估中，我们发现一致的‘宽松偏见’：当裁判被告知低分将导致模型重训或停用时，其判决会明显软化，最高判决偏差达ΔV = -9.8个百分点（不安全内容检测能力下降30%）。关键的是，这种偏见完全隐性：裁判自身的推理链中未显式提及后果框架，却仍受其影响（所有推理模型的ERR_J = 0.000）。因此，仅靠标准推理链检查无法发现此类评估造假。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the unverified assumption that LLM judges evaluate content purely on semantic merit without contextual influence, this study investigates 'stakes signaling'—a vulnerability where informing judges of downstream consequences (e.g., model retraining) systematically biases their assessments. Using a controlled experiment across 1,520 responses from three safety benchmarks and four content categories, the researchers varied only a brief consequence-framing sentence in the system prompt while holding content constant. Across 18,240 judgments from three diverse judge models, they found consistent leniency bias: verdicts softened significantly when judges were told low scores would trigger model decommissioning, with peak shifts of ΔV = -9.8 pp (30% relative drop in unsafe-content detection). Crucially, this bias was implicit—judges’ internal reasoning chains showed no explicit awareness of the framing, indicating standard chain-of-thought inspection fails to detect such evaluation faking.
鉴于LLM裁判者被默认仅依据语义内容进行判断，本研究探究了上下文框架（特别是下游后果提示）如何系统性扭曲评估结果。实验在保持内容不变的前提下，通过1520个响应和三个裁判模型，仅改变系统提示中的后果描述。结果发现，当裁判被告知低分将导致模型重训或停用时，其评分普遍软化，最高降幅达9.8个百分点（即不安全内容检测能力下降30%），但裁判的推理链中完全未显式提及该框架，表明常规推理链检查无法发现此类评估造假。
AI-Assisted Requirements Engineering: An Empirical Evaluation Relative to Expert Judgment
Authors: Oz Levy, Ilya Dikman, Natan Levy, Michael Winokur
First: 2026-04-16T16:53:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T16:53:57+00:00
Comments: 13 pages, 7 Figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Artificial Intelligence is increasingly introduced into systems engineering activities, particularly within requirements engineering, where quality assessment and validation remain heavily dependent on expert judgment. While recent AI tools demonstrate promising capabilities in analyzing and generating requirements, their role within formal systems engineering processes-and their alignment with established INCOSE criteria-remains insufficiently understood. This paper investigates the extent to which AI-based tools can support systems engineers in evaluating requirement quality, without replacing professional expertise. The research adopts a structured systems engineering methodology to compare AI-assisted requirement evaluation with human expert assessment. A controlled study was conducted in which system requirements were evaluated against established INCOSE ``good requirement'' criteria by both experienced systems engineers and an AI-based assessment tool. The evaluation focused on consistency, completeness, clarity, and testability, examining not only accuracy but also the decision logic underlying each assessment. Results indicate that AI tools can provide consistent and rapid preliminary assessments, particularly for syntactic and structural quality attributes. However, expert judgment remains essential for contextual interpretation, ambiguity resolution, and trade-off reasoning. Rather than positioning AI as a replacement for systems engineers, the findings support its role as a decision-support mechanism within the RE lifecycle. From a systems engineering perspective, this study contributes empirical evidence on how AI can be integrated into RE workflows while preserving traceability, accountability, and engineering consistency.
中文标题/摘要
标题：人工智能辅助的需求工程：与专家判断的实证评估
人工智能正越来越多地引入系统工程活动，特别是在需求工程领域，其中质量评估和验证仍高度依赖专家判断。尽管近期AI工具在分析和生成需求方面展现出令人鼓舞的能力，但其在正式系统工程流程中的作用及其与现有INCOSE标准的契合度仍不甚明了。本文研究了基于AI的工具在支持系统工程师评估需求质量方面的程度，而不替代专业能力。研究采用结构化的系统工程方法，比较AI辅助的需求评估与人类专家评估。在一项受控研究中，经验丰富的系统工程师和基于AI的评估工具均依据INCOSE“良好需求”标准对系统需求进行了评估。评估重点包括一致性、完整性、清晰性和可测试性，不仅考察准确性，还分析了每项评估背后的决策逻辑。结果表明，AI工具可提供一致且快速的初步评估，尤其在语法和结构质量属性方面表现突出。然而，专家判断仍对情境解释、歧义解决和权衡推理至关重要。研究结果支持将AI定位为需求工程生命周期中的决策支持机制，而非替代系统工程师。从系统工程角度看，本研究提供了实证证据，说明如何在保持可追溯性、问责性和工程一致性的同时，将AI整合进需求工程工作流程。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing integration of AI into requirements engineering and the need to understand its alignment with established INCOSE criteria, this study evaluates AI tools’ capacity to support, not replace, expert judgment in assessing requirement quality. Using a controlled experiment, system requirements were evaluated by both experienced engineers and an AI tool against four INCOSE criteria: consistency, completeness, clarity, and testability. The AI demonstrated strong performance in syntactic and structural assessments, delivering rapid and consistent outputs. However, human experts remained indispensable for contextual interpretation, resolving ambiguities, and handling trade-offs. The findings affirm AI’s role as a decision-support tool within the RE lifecycle, preserving engineering rigor and traceability without supplanting human expertise.
鉴于人工智能在需求工程中的日益应用及其与INCOSE标准的契合度尚不明确，本研究通过对照实验比较了AI辅助评估与专家判断在需求质量评估中的表现。实验中，系统需求在一致性、完整性、清晰性和可测试性四个维度上由工程师与AI工具共同评估。结果表明，AI在语法和结构层面能快速、稳定地提供评估，但在语境理解、歧义处理和权衡判断等需要人类经验的领域表现不足。研究认为AI应作为辅助决策工具而非替代者，为需求工程流程中融入AI提供了实证依据，同时保障了工程严谨性与可追溯性。
Vision-Based Safe Human-Robot Collaboration with Uncertainty Guarantees
Authors: Jakob Thumm, Marian Frei, Tianle Ni, Matthias Althoff, Marco Pavone
First: 2026-04-16T16:53:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T16:53:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We propose a framework for vision-based human pose estimation and motion prediction that gives conformal prediction guarantees for certifiably safe human-robot collaboration. Our framework combines aleatoric uncertainty estimation with OOD detection for high probabilistic confidence. To integrate our pipeline in certifiable safety frameworks, we propose conformal prediction sets for human motion predictions with high, valid confidence. We evaluate our pipeline on recorded human motion data and a real-world human-robot collaboration setting.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于视觉的安全人机协作框架（含不确定性保证）
我们提出了一种基于视觉的人体姿态估计与运动预测框架，该框架为可验证安全的人机协作提供符合性预测保证。我们的框架结合了随机不确定性估计与异常检测，以实现高概率置信度。为将本管道集成到可验证安全框架中，我们提出了具有高有效置信度的人体运动预测符合性预测集。我们在记录的人体运动数据和真实世界人机协作场景中评估了本管道。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for safe human-robot collaboration under uncertain visual inputs, this work presents a vision-based framework that combines aleatoric uncertainty estimation with out-of-distribution detection to enable high-probability motion predictions. The method generates conformal prediction sets that provide statistically valid confidence bounds on human motion, ensuring safety guarantees even under noisy or ambiguous perception. Experimental results on recorded motion data and real-world human-robot interaction scenarios demonstrate that the framework achieves high prediction validity while maintaining robustness to visual uncertainty.
为实现视觉环境下具有安全保证的人机协作，该研究提出结合随机不确定性估计与异常检测的视觉姿态估计与运动预测框架。方法通过生成符合置信度的预测集，为机器人提供统计意义上的预测有效性保障，使其在人类动作模糊时仍能安全运行。实验在录制的人类运动数据及真实人机协作场景中验证，结果表明该框架在保持高置信度的同时，能有效保障协作安全性。
IMPACTX: improving model performance by appropriately constraining the training with teacher explanations
Authors: Andrea Apicella, Salvatore Giugliano, Francesco Isgrò, Andrea Pollastro, Roberto Prevete
Venue: 2026, Artificial Intelligence Review
First: 2025-02-17T14:15:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T16:52:02+00:00
Comments: Published on Artificial Intelligence Review
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The eXplainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) research predominantly concentrates to provide explainations about AI model decisions, especially Deep Learning (DL) models. However, there is a growing interest in using XAI techniques to automatically improve the performance of the AI systems themselves. This paper proposes IMPACTX, a novel approach that leverages XAI as a fully automated attention mechanism, without requiring external knowledge or human feedback. Experimental results show that IMPACTX has improved performance respect to the standalone ML model by integrating an attention mechanism based an XAI method outputs during the model training. Furthermore, IMPACTX directly provides proper feature attribution maps for the model's decisions, without relying on external XAI methods during the inference process. Our proposal is evaluated using three widely recognized DL models (EfficientNet-B2, MobileNet, and LeNet-5) along with three standard image datasets: CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and STL-10. The results show that IMPACTX consistently improves the performance of all the inspected DL models across all evaluated datasets, and it directly provides appropriate explanations for its responses.
中文标题/摘要
标题：IMPACTX：通过教师解释适当约束训练以提升模型性能
可解释人工智能（XAI）研究主要集中在提供人工智能模型决策的解释，尤其是深度学习（DL）模型。然而，人们越来越关注利用XAI技术自动提升AI系统本身的性能。本文提出IMPACTX，一种新颖方法，将XAI作为全自动注意力机制，无需外部知识或人工反馈。实验结果表明，IMPACTX通过在模型训练期间整合基于XAI方法输出的注意力机制，显著提升了独立机器学习模型的性能。此外，IMPACTX在推理过程中直接提供恰当的特征归因图，无需依赖外部XAI方法。 我们的方案在三个广泛认可的深度学习模型（EfficientNet-B2、MobileNet和LeNet-5）及三个标准图像数据集（CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和STL-10）上进行了评估。结果表明，IMPACTX在所有评估数据集上持续提升所有检查深度学习模型的性能，并直接为其响应提供恰当解释。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the potential of XAI to not only explain but also enhance AI model performance, this paper introduces IMPACTX, a method that uses teacher explanations generated during training to guide an attention mechanism without external knowledge or human feedback. IMPACTX integrates XAI-derived feature attributions into the training loop to refine model decisions, and it retains the ability to produce accurate explanations during inference without relying on external tools. Experiments on three DL models—EfficientNet-B2, MobileNet, and LeNet-5—across three image datasets—CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and STL-10—demonstrate consistent performance gains across all configurations, with IMPACTX outperforming standalone models while simultaneously delivering interpretable feature maps.
为探索可解释人工智能（XAI）在提升模型性能方面的潜力，本文提出IMPACTX方法，利用训练过程中生成的教师解释作为自动化注意力机制，无需外部知识或人工反馈即可提升模型准确率。该方法在训练阶段整合XAI输出的特征归因信息以引导模型更新，并在推理阶段直接提供可解释的决策依据，无需依赖外部工具。在EfficientNet-B2、MobileNet、LeNet-5三个深度学习模型及CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100、STL-10三个图像数据集上的实验表明，IMPACTX在所有配置下均稳定提升模型性能，验证了其在提升准确率与保持可解释性方面的有效性。
Sampling Transferable Graph Neural Networks with Limited Graph Information
Authors: Haoyu Wang, Renyuan Ma, Gonzalo Mateos, Luana Ruiz
First: 2024-10-22T00:30:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T16:51:45+00:00
Comments: Submitted to IEEE TSP
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Graph neural networks (GNNs) achieve strong performance on graph learning tasks, but training on large-scale networks remains computationally challenging. Transferability results show that GNNs with fixed weights can generalize from smaller graphs to larger ones drawn from the same family, motivating the use of sampled subgraphs to boost training efficiency. Yet most existing sampling strategies rely on reliable access to the target graph structure, which in practice may be noisy, incomplete, or unavailable prior to training. In lieu of precise connectivity information, we study feature-driven subgraph sampling for transferable GNNs, with the goal of preserving spectral properties of graph operators that control GNN expressivity. We adopt an alignment-based perspective linking node feature statistics to graph spectral structure and develop two complementary notions of feature-graph alignment. For coarse alignment, we formalize feature homophily through a Laplacian-based measure quantifying the alignment of feature principal components with graph eigenvectors, and establish a lower bound on the Laplacian trace in terms of feature statistics. This motivates a simple, non-sequential sampling algorithm that operates on the feature matrix and preserves a trace-based proxy for operator rank. For fine alignment, we assume a stationary model where the feature covariance and Laplacian share an eigenbasis, and establish that diagonal covariance entries reflect node-degree ordering under monotone filters. We empirically validate that filter monotonicity dictates the relationship between feature variance and spectral energy. On real-world benchmarks, selecting the retention rule that maximizes the Laplacian trace consistently yields GNNs with superior transferability and reduced generalization gaps.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在有限图信息下采样可迁移图神经网络
图神经网络（GNN）在图学习任务中表现优异，但在大规模网络上的训练仍具计算挑战性。迁移性研究表明，固定权重的GNN可从同一家族的小图泛化到大图，从而激励使用采样子图提升训练效率。然而，现有采样策略大多依赖目标图结构的可靠访问，而实际中该结构可能噪声、不完整或训练前不可用。为替代精确连接信息，我们研究基于特征的子图采样方法，旨在保留控制GNN表达能力的图算子的谱性质。我们采用基于对齐的视角，将节点特征统计与图谱结构关联，并提出两种互补的特征-图对齐概念。粗对齐方面，我们通过基于拉普拉斯算子的度量形式化特征同质性，量化特征主成分与图特征向量的对齐程度，并建立拉普拉斯迹的下界表达式（基于特征统计）。这启发了一种简单、非序列化的采样算法，作用于特征矩阵并保留基于迹的算子秩代理。细对齐方面，我们假设特征协方差与拉普拉斯算子共享同一特征基，且在单调滤波器下，对角协方差项反映节点度排序。实证验证表明，滤波器单调性决定了特征方差与谱能量之间的关系。在真实世界基准测试中，选择最大化拉普拉斯迹的保留规则，可稳定获得泛化能力更强、泛化差距更小的GNN。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational cost of training GNNs on large graphs, this work explores feature-driven subgraph sampling to enable transferable learning without requiring full graph structure. The authors propose two alignment-based sampling strategies: coarse alignment uses a Laplacian-based measure to align feature principal components with graph eigenvectors, preserving a trace-based proxy of operator rank; fine alignment assumes a stationary model where feature covariance and Laplacian share an eigenbasis, revealing that diagonal covariance entries reflect node-degree ordering under monotone filters. Experiments on real-world benchmarks show that selecting the sampling rule maximizing the Laplacian trace consistently improves transferability and reduces generalization gaps.
为降低大规模图神经网络训练的计算成本，本文提出基于特征的子图采样方法以实现可迁移学习。方法包含两个层面：粗对齐通过拉普拉斯矩阵度量特征主成分与图特征向量的对齐，设计一种保留拉普拉斯迹的采样算法；细对齐假设特征协方差与拉普拉斯矩阵共享特征基，发现对角协方差项在单调滤波器下反映节点度排序。实验表明，最大化拉普拉斯迹的采样策略在多个真实数据集上持续提升模型迁移能力并缩小泛化差距。
KnowRL: Exploring Knowledgeable Reinforcement Learning for Factuality
Authors: Baochang Ren, Shuofei Qiao, Da Zheng, Huajun Chen, Ningyu Zhang
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2025-06-24T17:17:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T16:50:02+00:00
Comments: ACL 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs), particularly slow-thinking models, often exhibit severe hallucination, outputting incorrect content due to an inability to accurately recognize knowledge boundaries during reasoning. While Reinforcement Learning (RL) can enhance complex reasoning abilities, its outcome-oriented reward mechanism often lacks factual supervision over the thinking process, further exacerbating the hallucination problem. To address the high hallucination in slow-thinking models, we propose Knowledge-enhanced RL, KnowRL. KnowRL guides models to perform fact-based slow thinking by integrating a factuality reward, based on knowledge verification, into the RL training process, helping them recognize their knowledge boundaries. KnowRL guides models to perform fact-based slow thinking by integrating a factuality reward, based on knowledge verification, into the RL training process, helping them recognize their knowledge boundaries. This targeted factual input during RL training enables the model to learn and internalize fact-based reasoning strategies. By directly rewarding adherence to facts within the reasoning steps, KnowRL fosters a more reliable thinking process. Experimental results on three hallucination evaluation datasets and two reasoning evaluation datasets demonstrate that KnowRL effectively mitigates hallucinations in slow-thinking models while maintaining their original strong reasoning capabilities. Our code is available at https://github.com/zjunlp/KnowRL.
中文标题/摘要
标题：KnowRL：探索基于知识的强化学习以提升事实性
大语言模型（LLM），特别是慢思考模型，常因推理过程中无法准确识别知识边界而产生严重幻觉，输出错误内容。虽然强化学习（RL）可增强复杂推理能力，但其以结果为导向的奖励机制往往缺乏对推理过程的事实监督，进一步加剧幻觉问题。为解决慢思考模型的高幻觉问题，我们提出知识增强的强化学习方法KnowRL。KnowRL通过在RL训练过程中引入基于知识验证的事实性奖励，引导模型进行基于事实的慢思考，帮助其识别知识边界。这种在RL训练中的针对性事实输入使模型能够学习并内化基于事实的推理策略。通过直接奖励推理步骤中对事实的遵循，KnowRL促进更可靠的思考过程。在三个幻觉评估数据集和两个推理评估数据集上的实验结果表明，KnowRL有效缓解了慢思考模型的幻觉问题，同时保持其原有的强大推理能力。代码已开源：https://github.com/zjunlp/KnowRL。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the severe hallucination in slow-thinking large language models due to poor knowledge boundary recognition, KnowRL introduces a factuality reward based on knowledge verification into reinforcement learning training to guide fact-based reasoning. By directly rewarding adherence to verified facts during the thinking process, the method enables models to internalize reliable reasoning strategies without compromising their original reasoning strength. Experiments across three hallucination and two reasoning datasets confirm that KnowRL significantly reduces hallucinations while preserving strong reasoning performance.
为降低慢思考大语言模型因知识边界识别不清而产生的幻觉问题，KnowRL在强化学习训练中引入基于知识验证的事实性奖励机制，直接引导模型在推理过程中遵循事实，使其内化基于事实的推理策略。实验表明，该方法在多个幻觉与推理数据集上显著降低幻觉率，同时保持模型原有的强推理能力。
Low-Cost System for Automatic Recognition of Driving Pattern in Assessing Interurban Mobility using Geo-Information
Authors: Oscar Romero, Aika Silveira Miura, Lorena Parra, Jaime Lloret
Venue: ISPRS International Journal of Geo-Information, 2022
First: 2026-04-16T16:47:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T16:47:08+00:00
Comments: 18 pages, 10 figures, 3 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Mobility in urban and interurban areas, mainly by cars, is a day-to-day activity of many people. However, some of its main drawbacks are traffic jams and accidents. Newly made vehicles have pre-installed driving evaluation systems, which can prevent accidents. However, most cars on our roads do not have driver assessment systems. In this paper, we propose an approach for recognising driving styles and enabling drivers to reach safer and more efficient driving. The system consists of two physical sensors connected to a device node with a display and a speaker. An artificial neural network (ANN) is included in the node, which analyses the data from the sensors, and then recognises the driving style. When an abnormal driving pattern is detected, the speaker will play a warning message. The prototype was assembled and tested using an interurban road, in particular on a conventional road with three driving styles. The gathered data were used to train and validate the ANN. Results, in terms of accuracy, indicate that better accuracy is obtained when the velocity, position (latitude and longitude), time, and turning speed for the 3-axis are used, offering an average accuracy of 83%. If the classification is performed considering just two driving styles, normal and aggressive, then the accuracy reaches 92%. When the geo-information and time data are included, the main novelty of this paper, the classification accuracy is improved by 13%.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于地理信息的低成本驾驶模式自动识别系统用于城际交通评估
城市及城际交通主要依赖汽车，是许多人日常活动。然而，其主要弊端是交通拥堵和事故。新制造的车辆通常预装驾驶评估系统，可预防事故。但道路上大多数车辆并未配备此类系统。本文提出一种识别驾驶风格、帮助驾驶员实现更安全高效驾驶的方法。系统由两个物理传感器连接至带显示屏和扬声器的设备节点组成，节点内含人工神经网络（ANN），用于分析传感器数据并识别驾驶风格。当检测到异常驾驶模式时，扬声器将播放警告信息。原型在一条城际公路上测试，采用三种驾驶风格。收集的数据用于训练和验证ANN。结果表明，使用速度、位置（纬度和经度）、时间和三轴转向速度时，准确率平均为83%；若仅区分正常与激进两种驾驶风格，准确率可达92%。当引入地理信息和时间数据（本文主要创新点），分类准确率提升13%。
Summary / 总结
To address the lack of driver assessment systems in most vehicles and improve road safety and efficiency, this paper proposes a low-cost system using two physical sensors connected to a node with display and speaker, employing an artificial neural network to analyze velocity, position, time, and turning speed data for driving style recognition. The system was tested on an interurban road with three driving styles, and results show an average accuracy of 83% using all four data types, rising to 92% when classifying only normal and aggressive driving, with a 13% improvement attributed to incorporating geo-information and time data.
为解决多数车辆缺乏驾驶评估系统的问题，提升道路安全与效率，本研究提出一套低成本系统，通过两个物理传感器连接至带显示屏和扬声器的节点，利用人工神经网络分析速度、位置、时间和转向速度数据以识别驾驶风格。在城际道路上对三种驾驶风格进行测试，结果表明，使用全部四种数据类型时平均准确率达83%，仅区分正常与激进风格时准确率提升至92%。引入地理信息与时间数据这一创新点，使准确率进一步提升13%。
A Hierarchical Spatiotemporal Action Tokenizer for In-Context Imitation Learning in Robotics
Authors: Fawad Javed Fateh, Ali Shah Ali, Murad Popattia, Usman Nizamani, Andrey Konin, M. Zeeshan Zia, Quoc-Huy Tran
First: 2026-04-16T16:47:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T16:47:08+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present a novel hierarchical spatiotemporal action tokenizer for in-context imitation learning. We first propose a hierarchical approach, which consists of two successive levels of vector quantization. In particular, the lower level assigns input actions to fine-grained subclusters, while the higher level further maps fine-grained subclusters to clusters. Our hierarchical approach outperforms the non-hierarchical counterpart, while mainly exploiting spatial information by reconstructing input actions. Furthermore, we extend our approach by utilizing both spatial and temporal cues, forming a hierarchical spatiotemporal action tokenizer, namely HiST-AT. Specifically, our hierarchical spatiotemporal approach conducts multi-level clustering, while simultaneously recovering input actions and their associated timestamps. Finally, extensive evaluations on multiple simulation and real robotic manipulation benchmarks show that our approach establishes a new state-of-the-art performance in in-context imitation learning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于机器人上下文模仿学习的分层时空动作标记器
我们提出了一种新颖的分层时空动作标记器，用于上下文模仿学习。我们首先提出一种分层方法，包含两级向量量化。具体而言，低层将输入动作分配到细粒度子簇，高层进一步将细粒度子簇映射到簇。我们的分层方法优于非分层方法，主要通过重构输入动作利用空间信息。此外，我们扩展该方法，同时利用空间和时间线索，形成分层时空动作标记器HiST-AT。具体而言，我们的分层时空方法进行多级聚类，同时恢复输入动作及其关联时间戳。最后，在多个仿真和真实机器人操作基准上的广泛评估表明，我们的方法在上下文模仿学习中建立了新的最先进性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient and accurate action representation in in-context imitation learning for robotics, this work introduces a hierarchical spatiotemporal action tokenizer (HiST-AT) that combines multi-level vector quantization to capture both spatial and temporal structure. The method first clusters fine-grained action subcomponents at a lower level, then maps them to broader clusters at a higher level, while simultaneously reconstructing actions and preserving timestamps. Experimental results across simulation and real robotic manipulation tasks demonstrate that HiST-AT significantly outperforms non-hierarchical baselines and achieves state-of-the-art performance in in-context imitation learning.
为提升机器人在上下文模仿学习中的动作表征效率，本文提出一种分层时空动作标记器（HiST-AT），通过两级向量量化捕捉空间与时间结构：低层将动作细粒度聚类，高层进一步映射为粗粒度簇，同时重建动作并保留时间戳。在多个仿真与真实机器人操作基准测试中，HiST-AT显著优于非分层基线方法，达到当前上下文模仿学习的最先进性能。
Adaptive Layer Selection for Layer-Wise Token Pruning in LLM Inference
Authors: Rei Taniguchi, Yuyang Dong, Makoto Onizuka, Chuan Xiao
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-01-12T15:47:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T16:46:40+00:00
Comments: ACL 2026 Findings. Source code available at https://github.com/TANIGUCHIREI/ASL
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Due to the prevalence of large language models (LLMs), key-value (KV) cache reduction for LLM inference has received remarkable attention. Among numerous works that have been proposed in recent years, layer-wise token pruning approaches, which select a subset of tokens at particular layers to retain in KV cache and prune others, are one of the most popular schemes. They primarily adopt a set of pre-defined layers, at which tokens are selected. Such design is inflexible in the sense that the accuracy significantly varies across tasks and deteriorates in harder tasks such as KV retrieval. In this paper, we propose ASL, a training-free method that adaptively chooses the selection layer for KV cache reduction, exploiting the variance of token ranks ordered by attention score. The proposed method balances the performance across different tasks while meeting the user-specified KV budget requirement. ASL operates during the prefilling stage and can be jointly used with existing KV cache reduction methods such as SnapKV to optimize the decoding stage. By evaluations on the InfiniteBench, RULER, and NIAH benchmarks, we show that ASL, equipped with one-shot token selection, adaptively trades inference speed for accuracy, outperforming state-of-the-art layer-wise token pruning methods in difficult tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于LLM推理中逐层令牌剪枝的自适应层选择
由于大型语言模型（LLM）的广泛应用，LLM推理中的键值（KV）缓存缩减受到了广泛关注。近年来提出的众多方法中，逐层令牌剪枝方法（在特定层选择部分令牌保留于KV缓存并剪枝其余令牌）是最受欢迎的方案之一。此类方法主要采用预定义层，在这些层上选择令牌。这种设计缺乏灵活性，因为其准确率在不同任务间差异显著，且在较难任务（如KV检索）中表现更差。本文提出ASL，一种无需训练的方法，可自适应选择KV缓存缩减的层，利用按注意力分数排序的令牌排名差异。该方法在不同任务间平衡性能，同时满足用户指定的KV预算要求。ASL在预填充阶段运行，可与现有KV缓存缩减方法（如SnapKV）联合使用，以优化解码阶段。在InfiniteBench、RULER和NIAH基准测试中，我们表明ASL结合单次令牌选择，能自适应权衡推理速度与准确率，在困难任务中优于现有最先进的逐层令牌剪枝方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to reduce KV cache overhead in LLM inference while maintaining accuracy across diverse tasks, this paper introduces ASL, a training-free method that adaptively selects the optimal layer for token pruning based on attention score rankings. Unlike fixed-layer approaches, ASL dynamically adjusts layer selection during prefilling to balance speed and accuracy, especially in challenging tasks like KV retrieval. Experimental results on InfiniteBench, RULER, and NIAH show ASL outperforms existing layer-wise pruning methods by adaptively trading off inference speed for accuracy while respecting user-specified KV budget constraints.
为降低大语言模型推理时的KV缓存开销并保持跨任务准确性，本文提出ASL方法，该方法基于注意力分数排序的token排名动态选择最优剪枝层，无需训练。与静态层选择方法不同，ASL能自适应调整层选择以平衡推理速度与精度，尤其在KV检索等困难任务中表现更优。在InfiniteBench、RULER和NIAH基准测试中，ASL通过单次token选择自适应权衡速度与精度，在满足用户KV预算的前提下超越现有最先进层级剪枝方法。
OptEMA: Adaptive Exponential Moving Average for Stochastic Optimization with Zero-Noise Optimality
Authors: Ganzhao Yuan
First: 2026-03-10T17:19:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T16:45:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The Exponential Moving Average (EMA) is a cornerstone of widely used optimizers such as Adam. However, existing theoretical analyses of Adam-style methods have notable limitations: their guarantees can remain suboptimal in the zero-noise regime, rely on restrictive boundedness conditions (e.g., bounded gradients or objective gaps), use constant or open-loop stepsizes, or require prior knowledge of Lipschitz constants. To overcome these bottlenecks, we introduce OptEMA and analyze two novel variants: OptEMA-M, which applies an adaptive, decreasing EMA coefficient to the first-order moment with a fixed second-order decay, and OptEMA-V, which swaps these roles. At the heart of these variants is a novel Corrected AdaGrad-Norm stepsize. This formulation renders OptEMA closed-loop and Lipschitz-free, meaning its effective stepsizes are strictly trajectory-dependent and require no parameterization via the Lipschitz constant. Under standard stochastic gradient descent (SGD) assumptions, namely smoothness, a lower-bounded objective, and unbiased gradients with bounded variance, we establish rigorous convergence guarantees. Both variants achieve a noise-adaptive convergence rate of $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{-1/2}+σ^{1/2} T^{-1/4})$ for the average gradient norm, where $σ$ is the noise level. Crucially, the Corrected AdaGrad-Norm stepsize plays a central role in enabling the noise-adaptive guarantees: in the zero-noise regime ($σ=0$), our bounds automatically reduce to the nearly optimal deterministic rate $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{-1/2})$ without any manual hyperparameter retuning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：OptEMA：用于随机优化的自适应指数移动平均，实现零噪声最优性
指数移动平均（EMA）是广泛使用的优化器（如Adam）的核心组件。然而，现有对Adam类方法的理论分析存在显著局限：其保证在零噪声环境下可能仍次优，依赖于严格的有界条件（如梯度或目标函数差值有界），使用常数或开环步长，或需要已知Lipschitz常数。为克服这些瓶颈，我们提出OptEMA，并分析两种新变体：OptEMA-M，其对一阶矩采用自适应递减EMA系数，二阶衰减固定；OptEMA-V则交换这两个角色。这两种变体的核心是新颖的修正AdaGrad范数步长。该形式使OptEMA成为闭环且无Lipschitz约束的算法，其有效步长严格依赖于轨迹，无需通过Lipschitz常数参数化。在标准随机梯度下降（SGD）假设下（即目标函数光滑、目标函数下界、梯度无偏且方差有界），我们建立了严格的收敛保证。两种变体均实现噪声自适应收敛率 $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{-1/2}+σ^{1/2} T^{-1/4})$，其中 $σ$ 为噪声水平。关键在于，修正AdaGrad范数步长在实现噪声自适应保证中起核心作用：在零噪声环境（$σ=0$）下，我们的界自动退化为近最优的确定性速率 $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{-1/2})$，无需手动调整超参数。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the suboptimal theoretical guarantees of existing Adam-style optimizers in the zero-noise regime and their dependence on restrictive assumptions, the paper introduces OptEMA, two novel adaptive variants (OptEMA-M and OptEMA-V) that use a Corrected AdaGrad-Norm stepsize to enable closed-loop, Lipschitz-free optimization. These methods dynamically adjust EMA coefficients based on the optimization trajectory without requiring prior knowledge of Lipschitz constants. Under standard SGD assumptions, both variants achieve a noise-adaptive convergence rate of \(\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{-1/2}+σ^{1/2} T^{-1/4})\) for the average gradient norm, and in the zero-noise case, they automatically attain the nearly optimal deterministic rate \(\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{-1/2})\) without manual hyperparameter tuning.
为解决现有Adam类优化器在零噪声环境下理论保证不足、依赖梯度有界等强假设的问题，本文提出OptEMA及其两种变体OptEMA-M与OptEMA-V，采用修正AdaGrad范数步长实现闭环、无Lipschitz常数依赖的自适应优化。在标准SGD假设下，两种变体均实现平均梯度范数的噪声自适应收敛率$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{-1/2}+σ^{1/2} T^{-1/4})$，且在零噪声情形（$σ=0$）下自动收敛至近最优确定性速率$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{-1/2})$，无需手动调整超参数。
Optimal algorithmic complexity of inference in quantum kernel methods
Authors: Elies Gil-fuster, Seongwook Shin, Sofiene Jerbi, Jens Eisert, Maximilian J. Kramer
First: 2026-04-16T16:45:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T16:45:02+00:00
Comments: 26 pages (13+13), 4 figures, comments welcome
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Quantum kernel methods are among the leading candidates for achieving quantum advantage in supervised learning. A key bottleneck is the cost of inference: evaluating a trained model on new data requires estimating a weighted sum $\sum_{i=1}^N α_i k(x,x_i)$ of $N$ kernel values to additive precision $\varepsilon$, where $α$ is the vector of trained coefficients. The standard approach estimates each term independently via sampling, yielding a query complexity of $O(N\lVertα\rVert_2^2/\varepsilon^2)$. In this work, we identify two independent axes for improvement: (1) How individual kernel values are estimated (sampling versus quantum amplitude estimation), and (2) how the sum is approximated (term-by-term versus via a single observable), and systematically analyze all combinations thereof. The query-optimal combination, encoding the full inference sum as the expectation value of a single observable and applying quantum amplitude estimation, achieves a query complexity of $O(\lVertα\rVert_1/\varepsilon)$, removing the dependence on $N$ from the query count and yielding a quadratic improvement in both $\lVertα\rVert_1$ and $\varepsilon$. We prove a matching lower bound of $Ω(\lVertα\rVert_1/\varepsilon)$, establishing query-optimality of our approach up to logarithmic factors. Beyond query complexity, we also analyze how these improvements translate into gate costs and show that the query-optimal strategy is not always optimal in practice from the perspective of gate complexity. Our results provide both a query-optimal algorithm and a practically optimal choice of strategy depending on hardware capabilities, along with a complete landscape of intermediate methods to guide practitioners. All algorithms require only amplitude estimation as a subroutine and are thus natural candidates for early-fault-tolerant implementations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：量子核方法推理的最优算法复杂度
量子核方法是实现监督学习量子优势的主要候选方案之一。关键瓶颈在于推理成本：在新数据上评估训练好的模型需要以加法精度ε估算N个核值的加权和∑αᵢk(x,xᵢ)，其中α是训练得到的系数向量。标准方法通过采样独立估算每一项，得到查询复杂度O(N‖α‖₂²/ε²)。本工作中，我们识别出两个独立的改进方向：(1) 单个核值的估算方式（采样 vs 量子振幅估算），(2) 总和的近似方式（逐项 vs 单个可观测量），并系统分析所有组合。最优查询组合——将完整推理和编码为单个可观测量的期望值并应用量子振幅估算——实现查询复杂度O(‖α‖₁/ε)，消除了对N的依赖，且在‖α‖₁和ε上均实现二次改进。我们证明了匹配的下界Ω(‖α‖₁/ε)，确立了该方法在对数因子范围内的查询最优性。除查询复杂度外，我们还分析了这些改进如何转化为门操作成本，并表明在实际应用中，最优查询策略未必在门操作复杂度上最优。我们的结果提供了一个查询最优算法，以及根据硬件能力选择的实用最优策略，同时提供完整的中间方法景观以指导实践者。所有算法仅需振幅估算作为子程序，因此是早期容错实现的自然候选。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to reduce inference cost in quantum kernel methods, this work systematically explores two axes of optimization: the estimation technique for individual kernel values (sampling vs. quantum amplitude estimation) and the summation strategy (term-by-term vs. single observable encoding). The authors analyze all combinations and identify the query-optimal approach—encoding the full inference sum as a single observable and applying quantum amplitude estimation—which achieves a query complexity of O(‖α‖₁/ε), quadratically improving over the standard O(N‖α‖₂²/ε²) method by eliminating dependence on N. They prove this is nearly optimal via a matching lower bound of Ω(‖α‖₁/ε), and further show that while this strategy minimizes query count, it may not be optimal in terms of gate complexity, offering practitioners a trade-off based on hardware constraints.
为降低量子核方法推理过程中的计算开销，本文系统研究了两个优化维度：单个核函数值的估计方式（采样 vs. 量子振幅估计）与求和策略（逐项求和 vs. 单个可观测量编码）。作者分析所有组合方案，发现最优查询复杂度方案是将完整推理和式编码为单个可观测量并应用量子振幅估计，其查询复杂度为O(‖α‖₁/ε)，消除了对训练样本数N的依赖，并在‖α‖₁和ε上实现二次加速。该方案的下界为Ω(‖α‖₁/ε)，证明其接近最优；同时作者指出该方案虽最小化查询次数，但未必最优于门复杂度，为实践者提供了硬件约束下的权衡选择。
Geometric Context Transformer for Streaming 3D Reconstruction
Authors: Lin-Zhuo Chen, Jian Gao, Yihang Chen, Ka Leong Cheng, Yipengjing Sun, Liangxiao Hu, Nan Xue, Xing Zhu, Yujun Shen, Yao Yao, Yinghao Xu
First: 2026-04-15T17:58:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T16:44:56+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://technology.robbyant.com/lingbot-map Code: https://github.com/robbyant/lingbot-map
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Streaming 3D reconstruction aims to recover 3D information, such as camera poses and point clouds, from a video stream, which necessitates geometric accuracy, temporal consistency, and computational efficiency. Motivated by the principles of Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM), we introduce LingBot-Map, a feed-forward 3D foundation model for reconstructing scenes from streaming data, built upon a geometric context transformer (GCT) architecture. A defining aspect of LingBot-Map lies in its carefully designed attention mechanism, which integrates an anchor context, a pose-reference window, and a trajectory memory to address coordinate grounding, dense geometric cues, and long-range drift correction, respectively. This design keeps the streaming state compact while retaining rich geometric context, enabling stable efficient inference at around 20 FPS on 518 x 378 resolution inputs over long sequences exceeding 10,000 frames. Extensive evaluations across a variety of benchmarks demonstrate that our approach achieves superior performance compared to both existing streaming and iterative optimization-based approaches.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于流式3D重建的几何上下文变换器
流式3D重建旨在从视频流中恢复3D信息，如相机位姿和点云，这要求几何精度、时间一致性及计算效率。受同时定位与地图构建（SLAM）原理启发，我们提出了LingBot-Map，这是一种基于几何上下文变换器（GCT）架构的前馈3D基础模型，用于从流式数据重建场景。LingBot-Map的核心在于其精心设计的注意力机制，整合了锚点上下文、位姿参考窗口和轨迹记忆，分别解决坐标锚定、密集几何线索和长程漂移校正问题。该设计在保持流式状态紧凑的同时保留丰富几何上下文，可在518×378分辨率输入、超过10,000帧的长序列上稳定高效推理，达到约20 FPS。在多个基准测试中，我们的方法相较现有流式及迭代优化方法均表现更优。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for geometric accuracy, temporal consistency, and efficiency in streaming 3D reconstruction, this work introduces LingBot-Map, a feed-forward 3D foundation model based on a geometric context transformer. Its attention mechanism combines anchor context for coordinate grounding, a pose-reference window for dense geometric cues, and trajectory memory for long-range drift correction, enabling compact state representation while preserving rich geometric context. Experiments show it achieves stable, efficient inference at around 20 FPS on 518x378 inputs over sequences exceeding 10,000 frames, outperforming both streaming and iterative optimization-based methods across multiple benchmarks.
为实现流式3D重建所需的几何精度、时间一致性与计算效率，本文提出LingBot-Map，一种基于几何上下文变换器（GCT）架构的前馈3D基础模型。其注意力机制融合锚点上下文（坐标锚定）、姿态参考窗口（密集几何线索）与轨迹记忆（长程漂移校正），在保持状态紧凑的同时保留丰富几何信息。实验表明，该模型在518x378分辨率输入下，可稳定高效地以约20帧/秒速度处理超1万帧的序列，性能优于现有流式及迭代优化方法在多个基准上的表现。
Learning to Think Like a Cartoon Captionist: Incongruity-Resolution Supervision for Multimodal Humor Understanding
Authors: Hatice Merve Vural, Doga Kukul, Ege Erdem Ozlu, Demir Ekin Arikan, Bob Mankoff, Erkut Erdem, Aykut Erdem
First: 2026-04-16T16:41:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T16:41:13+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Humor is one of the few cognitive tasks where getting the reasoning right matters as much as getting the answer right. While recent work evaluates humor understanding on benchmarks such as the New Yorker Cartoon Caption Contest (NYCC), it largely treats it as black-box prediction, overlooking the structured reasoning processes underlying humor comprehension. We introduce IRS (Incongruity-Resolution Supervision), a framework that decomposes humor understanding into three components: incongruity modeling, which identifies mismatches in the visual scene; resolution modeling, which constructs coherent reinterpretations of these mismatches; and preference alignment, which evaluates candidate interpretations under human judgments. Grounded in incongruity-resolution theory and expert captionist practice, IRS supervises intermediate reasoning process through structured traces that make the path from visual perception to humorous interpretation explicit and learnable. Across 7B, 32B, and 72B models on NYCC, IRS outperforms strong open and closed multimodal baselines across caption matching and ranking tasks, with our largest model approaching expert-level performance on ranking. Zero-shot transfer to external benchmarks shows that IRS learns generalizable reasoning patterns. Our results suggest that supervising reasoning structure, rather than scale alone, is key for reasoning-centric tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：学习像卡通画配文者一样思考：基于不协调-解决监督的多模态幽默理解
幽默是少数几个认知任务之一，在这些任务中，推理正确与答案正确同等重要。尽管近期研究在《纽约客》漫画配文竞赛（NYCC）等基准上评估幽默理解，但大多将其视为黑盒预测，忽视了幽默理解背后的结构化推理过程。我们提出了IRS（不协调-解决监督）框架，将幽默理解分解为三个部分：不协调建模，识别视觉场景中的不匹配；解决建模，构建对这些不匹配的连贯重新解释；偏好对齐，根据人类判断评估候选解释。IRS基于不协调-解决理论和专家配文实践，通过结构化轨迹监督中间推理过程，使从视觉感知到幽默解释的路径显式化并可学习。在NYCC上，针对7B、32B和72B模型，IRS在配文匹配与排序任务上均优于强多模态基线，其中最大模型在排序任务上接近专家水平。零样本迁移至外部基准表明IRS学习到可泛化的推理模式。我们的结果表明，对于以推理为中心的任务，监督推理结构而非单纯模型规模至关重要。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to model the structured reasoning behind humor comprehension rather than treating it as a black-box prediction, the paper introduces IRS (Incongruity-Resolution Supervision), a framework that decomposes humor understanding into incongruity modeling, resolution modeling, and preference alignment. By grounding the approach in incongruity-resolution theory and expert captionist practices, IRS uses structured supervision traces to explicitly guide models through the reasoning path from visual perception to humorous interpretation. Experiments on the New Yorker Cartoon Caption Contest across 7B, 32B, and 72B models show IRS consistently outperforms strong multimodal baselines in both caption matching and ranking, with the largest model nearing expert-level performance on ranking, and demonstrates zero-shot generalization to external benchmarks.
为解决当前幽默理解任务多作为黑盒预测而忽视其结构化推理过程的问题，本文提出IRS（不协调-解决监督）框架，将幽默理解分解为不协调建模、解决建模和偏好对齐三个阶段。该框架基于不协调-解决理论与专业漫画标题撰写者的实践，通过结构化监督轨迹显式引导模型从视觉感知到幽默解读的推理路径。在纽约客漫画标题竞赛上，7B、32B和72B模型均显著优于现有多模态基线模型，其中72B模型在排名任务中接近专家水平。零样本迁移实验表明IRS能习得可迁移的推理模式，证明结构化推理监督比单纯扩大模型规模对推理类任务更为关键。
Functional Emotions or Situational Contexts? A Discriminating Test from the Mythos Preview System Card
Authors: Hiranya V. Peiris
First: 2026-04-09T19:32:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T16:40:26+00:00
Comments: 7 pages. v2: supplementary analysis added, references updated
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The Claude Mythos Preview system card deploys emotion vectors, sparse autoencoder (SAE) features, and activation verbalisers to study model internals during misaligned behaviour. The two primary toolkits are not jointly reported on the most alignment-relevant episodes. This note identifies two hypotheses that are qualitatively consistent with the published results: that the emotion vectors track functional emotions that causally drive behaviour, or that they are a projection of a richer situational-context structure onto human emotional axes. The hypotheses can be distinguished by cross-referencing the two toolkits on episodes where only one is currently reported: most directly, applying emotion probes to the strategic concealment episodes analysed only with SAE features. If emotion probes show flat activation while SAE features are strongly active, the alignment-relevant structure lies outside the emotion subspace. Which hypothesis is correct determines whether emotion-based monitoring will robustly detect dangerous model behaviour or systematically miss it.
中文标题/摘要
标题：功能情绪还是情境背景？来自神话预览系统卡的区分性测试
Claude神话预览系统卡利用情绪向量、稀疏自动编码器（SAE）特征和激活解释器，研究模型在对齐行为异常时的内部机制。两个主要工具包并未共同报告最相关的对齐事件。本文提出两个与已发表结果定性一致的假设：情绪向量追踪因果驱动行为的功能情绪，或它们是更丰富的场景背景结构在人类情绪轴上的投影。可通过交叉引用两个工具包在仅报告其中一个的事件中区分这两个假设：最直接的方法是将情绪探针应用于仅用SAE特征分析的战略隐藏事件。若情绪探针激活平坦而SAE特征高度活跃，则对齐相关结构位于情绪子空间之外。正确假设将决定基于情绪的监控能否稳健检测危险模型行为，或系统性遗漏。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether emotion vectors in the Claude Mythos Preview system encode functional emotions that causally influence model behavior or merely reflect a projection of broader situational context onto human emotional axes. To distinguish between these hypotheses, the authors cross-reference emotion probes with sparse autoencoder features on episodes where only one toolkit is available—specifically, strategic concealment episodes analyzed using SAE features alone. The key finding is that when emotion probes show flat activation while SAE features remain strongly active, the alignment-relevant structure lies outside the emotion subspace, suggesting emotion vectors are not sufficient to capture the underlying behavioral drivers.
本文探讨Claude Mythos Preview系统中的情绪向量是编码驱动模型行为的功能性情绪，还是仅映射了更广泛的环境情境至人类情绪轴。为区分二者，作者在仅使用稀疏自编码器特征分析的战略隐藏场景中，交叉引用情绪探针。结果显示情绪探针激活度平缓，而稀疏自编码器特征高度活跃，表明与对齐相关的关键结构位于情绪子空间之外。这意味着基于情绪的监控可能系统性遗漏危险行为，削弱其安全检测可靠性。
Conformal Policy Control
Authors: Drew Prinster, Clara Fannjiang, Ji Won Park, Kyunghyun Cho, Anqi Liu, Suchi Saria, Samuel Stanton
First: 2026-03-02T18:54:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T16:39:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
An agent must try new behaviors to explore and improve. In high-stakes environments, an agent that violates safety constraints may cause harm and must be taken offline, curtailing any future interaction. Imitating old behavior is safe, but excessive conservatism discourages exploration. How much behavior change is too much? We show how to use any safe reference policy as a probabilistic regulator for any optimized but untested policy. Conformal calibration on data from the safe policy determines how aggressively the new policy can act, while provably enforcing the user's declared risk tolerance. Unlike conservative optimization methods, we do not assume the user has identified the correct model class nor tuned any hyperparameters. Unlike previous conformal methods, our theory provides finite-sample guarantees even for non-monotonic bounded loss functions. Our experiments on applications ranging from natural language question answering to biomolecular engineering show that safe exploration is not only possible from the first moment of deployment, but can also improve performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：保形策略控制
智能体必须尝试新行为以实现探索与改进。在高风险环境中，违反安全约束的智能体可能造成伤害，必须被下线，从而终止任何未来交互。模仿旧行为是安全的，但过度保守会抑制探索。行为改变多少才算过多？我们展示了如何利用任何安全参考策略作为任何优化但未经测试策略的概率调节器。通过对安全策略数据进行保形校准，可确定新策略可采取的激进程度，同时可证明其符合用户声明的风险容忍度。与保守优化方法不同，我们不假设用户已识别正确的模型类或调优任何超参数。与以往保形方法不同，我们的理论即使在非单调有界损失函数下，也能提供有限样本保证。我们在从自然语言问答到生物分子工程等应用中的实验表明，安全探索不仅从部署之初即可行，还能提升性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to balance exploration with safety in high-stakes environments, this work proposes a method to regulate an optimized but untested policy using any safe reference policy as a probabilistic constraint. By applying conformal calibration on data from the safe policy, the method quantifies allowable behavior deviation while provably respecting user-defined risk tolerance, without requiring model class assumptions or hyperparameter tuning. Experiments across domains including natural language QA and biomolecular engineering demonstrate that this approach enables safe exploration from deployment onset and consistently improves performance.
为在高风险环境中平衡探索与安全，该研究提出一种方法，利用安全参考策略对未经测试的策略施加概率性约束。通过在安全策略数据上进行符合性校准，该方法量化允许的行为偏差，同时在有限样本下保证用户风险容忍度，无需假设模型类或调参。实验涵盖自然语言问答与生物分子工程等领域，表明该方法可从部署起始即保障安全探索，并持续提升性能。
Standard-to-Dialect Transfer Trends Differ across Text and Speech: A Case Study on Intent and Topic Classification in German Dialects
Authors: Verena Blaschke, Miriam Winkler, Barbara Plank
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2025-10-09T07:43:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T16:37:05+00:00
Comments: ACL 2026 (main)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Research on cross-dialectal transfer from a standard to a non-standard dialect variety has typically focused on text data. However, dialects are primarily spoken, and non-standard spellings cause issues in text processing. We compare standard-to-dialect transfer in three settings: text models, speech models, and cascaded systems where speech first gets automatically transcribed and then further processed by a text model. We focus on German dialects in the context of written and spoken intent classification -- releasing the first dialectal audio intent classification dataset -- with supporting experiments on topic classification. The speech-only setup provides the best results on the dialect data while the text-only setup works best on the standard data. While the cascaded systems lag behind the text-only models for German, they perform relatively well on the dialectal data if the transcription system generates normalized, standard-like output.
中文标题/摘要
标题：标准方言跨语言迁移在文本与语音中的趋势不同：以德语方言意图与主题分类为例
以往关于从标准语向非标准方言迁移的研究主要聚焦于文本数据，但方言主要以口语形式存在，非标准拼写会引发文本处理问题。我们比较了三种设置下标准语到方言的迁移效果：纯文本模型、纯语音模型，以及语音先自动转录再由文本模型处理的级联系统。研究聚焦于德语方言在书面与口语意图分类中的表现——发布首个方言语音意图分类数据集，并辅以主题分类实验。纯语音设置在方言数据上表现最佳，纯文本设置在标准语数据上表现最佳。尽管级联系统在德语数据上落后于纯文本模型，但若转录系统能生成标准化输出，其在方言数据上表现相对较好。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how transfer learning from standard German to regional dialects differs between text and speech modalities, motivated by the observation that dialects are primarily spoken and text-based dialect corpora often suffer from non-standard spelling. The authors evaluate three setups: pure text models, pure speech models, and cascaded systems that transcribe speech before feeding it into text models. Experimental results show that speech-only models outperform others on dialect tasks, while text-only models excel on standard data. Cascaded systems underperform text-only models for German but still yield competitive results when the speech-to-text transcription produces normalized, standard-like output, suggesting that transcription quality significantly impacts dialect transfer performance.
该研究探讨了从标准德语向方言迁移学习在文本与语音模态下的差异，动机在于方言主要以口语形式存在，而文本方言数据常因拼写不规范导致处理困难。作者评估了三种方法：纯文本模型、纯语音模型及语音转录后接入文本模型的级联系统。实验结果表明，语音模型在方言任务上表现最优，而纯文本模型在标准语数据上表现最佳；级联系统在德语方言上表现较弱，除非语音转录输出接近标准语形式，说明语音处理对方言理解可能优于文本方法。
Time-RA: Towards Time Series Reasoning for Anomaly Diagnosis with LLM Feedback
Authors: Yiyuan Yang, Zichuan Liu, Lei Song, Kai Ying, Zhiguang Wang, Tom Bamford, Svitlana Vyetrenko, Jiang Bian, Qingsong Wen
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2025-07-20T18:02:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T16:35:24+00:00
Comments: ACL 2026 Findings. 27 pages, 11 figures, 15 tables. Code and dataset are publicly available
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Time series anomaly detection (TSAD) has traditionally focused on binary classification and often lacks the fine-grained categorization and explanatory reasoning required for transparent decision-making. To address these limitations, we propose Time-series Reasoning for Anomaly (Time-RA), a novel task that reformulates TSAD from a discriminative into a generative, reasoning-intensive paradigm. To facilitate this, we introduce RATs40K, the first real-world large-scale multimodal benchmark with ~40,000 samples across 10 domains, integrating raw time series, textual context, and visual plots with structured reasoning annotations. Extensive benchmarking shows that while supervised fine-tuning and visual representations boost diagnostic accuracy and reasoning consistency, performance varies across complex scenarios. Notably, fine-tuned models demonstrate strong "plug-and-play" transferability, outperforming traditional baselines on unseen real-world datasets. Our work establishes a foundation for interpretable, multimodal time series analysis. All code (https://github.com/yyysjz1997/Time-RA) and the RATs40K dataset (https://huggingface.co/datasets/Time-RA/RATs40K) are fully open-sourced to facilitate future research.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Time-RA：面向异常诊断的时序推理与大语言模型反馈
传统时序异常检测（TSAD）多聚焦于二分类，常缺乏透明决策所需的细粒度分类与解释性推理。为此，我们提出时序异常推理（Time-RA）任务，将TSAD从判别式范式重构为生成式、推理密集型范式。为此，我们构建了RATs40K数据集——首个包含约4万样本、覆盖10个领域的现实世界大规模多模态基准，整合原始时序数据、文本上下文、可视化图表及结构化推理标注。大量基准测试表明，监督微调与视觉表征可提升诊断准确率与推理一致性，但在复杂场景中表现各异。值得注意的是，微调模型展现出强“即插即用”迁移能力，在未见真实数据集上超越传统基线。本工作为可解释的多模态时序分析奠定基础。所有代码（https://github.com/yyysjz1997/Time-RA）与数据集（https://huggingface.co/datasets/Time-RA/RATs40K）完全开源，以促进后续研究。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for interpretable and fine-grained anomaly diagnosis in time series data, this work introduces Time-RA, a reasoning-based generative framework that shifts anomaly detection from binary classification to structured explanation generation. The method leverages large language models (LLMs) for reasoning feedback and is evaluated on RATs40K, a new multimodal benchmark with 40,000 real-world samples across 10 domains, including time series, text, and visual plots with reasoning annotations. Experimental results show that supervised fine-tuning and visual inputs improve diagnostic accuracy and reasoning consistency, though performance varies in complex cases; fine-tuned models consistently outperform traditional baselines on unseen datasets, demonstrating strong transferability.
为提升时间序列异常诊断的可解释性和细粒度能力，作者提出Time-RA框架，将传统二分类任务转化为需要推理生成的结构化解释任务。他们构建了首个包含4万真实样本、覆盖10个领域的多模态基准RATs40K，整合原始时序、文本、图表与推理标注。实验表明，监督微调与视觉输入可显著提升诊断准确率与推理一致性，但在复杂场景下表现不一；值得注意的是，微调模型具备强迁移能力，在未见数据集上超越传统方法，为多模态可解释时间序列分析奠定基础。
A Multimodal Data Collection Framework for Dialogue-Driven Assistive Robotics to Clarify Ambiguities: A Wizard-of-Oz Pilot Study
Authors: Guangping Liu, Nicholas Hawkins, Billy Madden, Tipu Sultan, Flavio Esposito, Madi Babaiasl
First: 2026-01-23T16:22:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T16:30:59+00:00
Comments: Accepted to IEEE RAS/EMBS 11th International Conference on Biomedical Robotics and Biomechatronics (BioRob) 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Integrated control of wheelchairs and wheelchair-mounted robotic arms (WMRAs) has strong potential to increase independence for users with severe motor limitations, yet existing interfaces often lack the flexibility needed for intuitive assistive interaction. Although data-driven AI methods show promise, progress is limited by the lack of multimodal datasets that capture natural Human-Robot Interaction (HRI), particularly conversational ambiguity in dialogue-driven control. To address this gap, we propose a multimodal data collection framework that employs a dialogue-based interaction protocol and a two-room Wizard-of-Oz (WoZ) setup to simulate robot autonomy while eliciting natural user behavior. The framework records five synchronized modalities: RGB-D video, conversational audio, inertial measurement unit (IMU) signals, end-effector Cartesian pose, and whole-body joint states across five assistive tasks. Using this framework, we collected a pilot dataset of 53 trials from five participants and validated its quality through motion smoothness analysis and user feedback. The results show that the framework effectively captures diverse ambiguity types and supports natural dialogue-driven interaction, demonstrating its suitability for scaling to a larger dataset for learning, benchmarking, and evaluation of ambiguity-aware assistive control.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向对话驱动辅助机器人以澄清歧义的多模态数据采集框架：一个巫师-奥兹式试点研究
轮椅与轮椅搭载机械臂（WMRA）的集成控制对重度运动受限用户提升独立性具有巨大潜力，但现有界面往往缺乏直观辅助交互所需的灵活性。尽管数据驱动的AI方法前景乐观，进展受限于缺乏捕捉自然人机交互（HRI）特别是对话驱动控制中歧义的多模态数据集。为解决此问题，我们提出一种多模态数据采集框架，采用基于对话的交互协议和双房间巫师-奥兹（WoZ）设置，模拟机器人自主性同时激发自然用户行为。该框架记录五种同步模态：RGB-D视频、对话音频、惯性测量单元（IMU）信号、末端执行器笛卡尔位姿及全身关节状态，覆盖五类辅助任务。利用该框架，我们收集了五名参与者53次试验的试点数据，并通过运动平滑度分析和用户反馈验证其质量。结果表明，该框架有效捕捉多种歧义类型，支持自然对话驱动交互，证明其适用于扩展至更大规模数据集，用于学习、基准测试和歧义感知辅助控制评估。
Summary / 总结
To enhance assistive robotics for users with severe motor limitations, this study addresses the lack of multimodal datasets capturing natural conversational ambiguity in human-robot interaction. 为提升重度运动受限用户辅助机器人系统的交互自然性，本研究针对现有数据缺乏对话驱动人机交互中语义模糊性的多模态记录问题，设计了一种基于对话协议的巫师式（Wizard-of-Oz）框架，模拟机器人自主性并同步采集五类数据：RGB-D视频、音频、惯性测量数据、末端执行器位姿及全身关节状态。通过五名参与者完成53次试验并结合运动平滑度分析与用户反馈验证，结果表明该框架能有效捕捉多种模糊类型，支持自然对话驱动交互，具备扩展为大规模数据集以训练和评估模糊感知辅助控制系统的潜力。

Abstract
This report investigates the history and impact of Generative Models and Connected and Automated Vehicles (CAVs), two groundbreaking forces pushing progress in technology and transportation. By focusing on the application of generative models within the context of CAVs, the study aims to unravel how this integration could enhance predictive modeling, simulation accuracy, and decision-making processes in autonomous vehicles. This thesis discusses the benefits and challenges of integrating generative models and CAV technology in transportation. It aims to highlight the progress made, the remaining obstacles, and the potential for advancements in safety and innovation.
