MADE: A Living Benchmark for Multi-Label Text Classification with Uncertainty Quantification of Medical Device Adverse Events
Authors: Raunak Agarwal, Markus Wenzel, Simon Baur, Jonas Zimmer, George Harvey, Jackie Ma
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-04-16T16:28:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T16:28:16+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ACL 2026 Mains
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Machine learning in high-stakes domains such as healthcare requires not only strong predictive performance but also reliable uncertainty quantification (UQ) to support human oversight. Multi-label text classification (MLTC) is a central task in this domain, yet remains challenging due to label imbalances, dependencies, and combinatorial complexity. Existing MLTC benchmarks are increasingly saturated and may be affected by training data contamination, making it difficult to distinguish genuine reasoning capabilities from memorization. We introduce MADE, a living MLTC benchmark derived from {m}edical device {ad}verse {e}vent reports and continuously updated with newly published reports to prevent contamination. MADE features a long-tailed distribution of hierarchical labels and enables reproducible evaluation with strict temporal splits. We establish baselines across more than 20 encoder- and decoder-only models under fine-tuning and few-shot settings (instruction-tuned/reasoning variants, local/API-accessible). We systematically assess entropy-/consistency-based and self-verbalized UQ methods. Results show clear trade-offs: smaller discriminatively fine-tuned decoders achieve the strongest head-to-tail accuracy while maintaining competitive UQ; generative fine-tuning delivers the most reliable UQ; large reasoning models improve performance on rare labels yet exhibit surprisingly weak UQ; and self-verbalized confidence is not a reliable proxy for uncertainty. Our work is publicly available at https://hhi.fraunhofer.de/aml-demonstrator/made-benchmark.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MADE：带不确定性量化医疗设备不良事件的多标签文本分类活体基准
在高风险领域（如医疗健康）中，机器学习不仅需要强大的预测性能，还需要可靠的不确定性量化（UQ）以支持人类监督。多标签文本分类（MLTC）是该领域核心任务，但因标签不平衡、依赖性和组合复杂性而极具挑战。现有MLTC基准日益饱和，且可能受训练数据污染影响，难以区分真实推理能力与记忆能力。我们提出MADE，一个基于医疗设备不良事件报告的活体MLTC基准，持续更新新发布报告以防止污染。MADE具有层次化标签的长尾分布，支持严格时间分割下的可复现评估。我们在超过20种编码器和解码器模型（微调与少样本设置，含指令调优/推理变体、本地/API可访问）上建立基线。系统评估基于熵/一致性及自言自语的UQ方法。结果表明存在明显权衡：小规模判别性微调解码器在头尾准确率上表现最佳且UQ竞争力强；生成式微调提供最可靠的UQ；大型推理模型在罕见标签上性能提升但UQ表现意外薄弱；自言自语置信度并非可靠不确定性代理。本工作公开于https://hhi.fraunhofer.de/aml-demonstrator/made-benchmark。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reliable uncertainty quantification in high-stakes healthcare applications, this paper introduces MADE, a continuously updated benchmark for multi-label text classification using medical device adverse event reports. The benchmark addresses label imbalance, label dependencies, and data contamination issues common in existing datasets. It employs strict temporal splits and hierarchical label structures to enable reproducible evaluation. The study evaluates over 20 encoder- and decoder-only models under fine-tuning and few-shot settings, assessing both entropy- and consistency-based UQ methods alongside self-verbalized confidence. Results reveal that small discriminatively fine-tuned decoders offer the best accuracy across label frequencies while maintaining strong UQ, generative fine-tuning yields the most reliable uncertainty estimates, large reasoning models improve rare-label performance but underperform in UQ, and self-verbalized confidence is an unreliable indicator of uncertainty.
为满足医疗等高风险领域对模型不确定性量化的需求，本文提出MADE基准，基于持续更新的医疗器械不良事件报告构建多标签文本分类数据集，以避免数据污染和标签分布失衡问题。研究在20多个编码器与解码器模型上进行微调与少样本设置下的评估，系统测试熵基、一致性基及自述式置信度等不确定性量化方法。结果表明：小规模判别式微调解码器在标签频次分布上表现最佳且不确定性量化稳定；生成式微调提供最可靠的不确定性估计；大型推理模型虽在罕见标签上表现优异，但不确定性量化能力较弱；而自述式置信度不能作为可靠不确定性代理。
Benchmarking Classical Coverage Path Planning Heuristics on Irregular Hexagonal Grids for Maritime Coverage Scenarios
Authors: Carlos S. Sepúlveda, Gonzalo A. Ruz
First: 2026-04-16T16:28:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T16:28:03+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Coverage path planning on irregular hexagonal grids is relevant to maritime surveillance, search and rescue and environmental monitoring, yet classical methods are often compared on small ad hoc examples or on rectangular grids. This paper presents a reproducible benchmark of deterministic single-vehicle coverage path planning heuristics on irregular hexagonal graphs derived from synthetic but maritime-motivated areas of interest. The benchmark contains 10,000 Hamiltonian-feasible instances spanning compact, elongated, and irregular morphologies, 17 heuristics from seven families, and a common evaluation protocol covering Hamiltonian success, complete-coverage success, revisits, path length, heading changes, and CPU latency. Across the released dataset, heuristics with explicit shortest-path reconnection solve the relaxed coverage task reliably but almost never produce zero-revisit tours. Exact Depth-First Search confirms that every released instance is Hamiltonian-feasible. The strongest classical Hamiltonian baseline is a Warnsdorff variant that uses an index-based tie-break together with a terminal-inclusive residual-degree policy, reaching 79.0% Hamiltonian success. The dominant design choice is not tie-breaking alone, but how the residual degree is defined when the endpoint is reserved until the final move. This shows that underreported implementation details can materially affect performance on sparse geometric graphs with bottlenecks. The benchmark is intended as a controlled testbed for heuristic analysis rather than as a claim of operational optimality at fleet scale.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在不规则六边形网格上对经典覆盖路径规划启发式方法进行基准测试：面向海上覆盖场景
在不规则六边形网格上的覆盖路径规划适用于海上监视、搜救和环境监测，但经典方法常在小规模随机示例或矩形网格上进行比较。本文提出了一种可复现的基准测试，针对从合成但具有海上应用场景的区域导出的不规则六边形图，评估确定性单车辆覆盖路径规划启发式方法。该基准包含10,000个哈密顿可行实例，涵盖紧凑、拉长和不规则形态，17种启发式方法来自七个家族，并采用统一评估协议，涵盖哈密顿成功、完全覆盖成功、重复访问、路径长度、航向变化和CPU延迟。在发布的数据集中，显式最短路径重连的启发式方法能可靠解决松弛覆盖任务，但几乎从不产生零重复访问路径。精确深度优先搜索确认所有发布实例均为哈密顿可行。最强的经典哈密顿基线是采用基于索引的并列处理与包含终点的残余度策略的Warnsdorff变体，实现79.0%的哈密顿成功。主导设计选择并非仅靠并列处理，而是当终点保留至最后一步时如何定义残余度。这表明，未充分报告的实现细节可能显著影响在存在瓶颈的稀疏几何图上的性能。该基准旨在作为启发式分析的受控测试平台，而非声称在舰队规模下具有操作最优性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reliable coverage path planning in maritime scenarios using irregular hexagonal grids, this study benchmarks 17 classical heuristics across 10,000 synthetic, maritime-inspired instances to evaluate their performance under standardized metrics. The method constructs Hamiltonian-feasible instances with varied morphologies and assesses heuristics based on Hamiltonian success, complete coverage, revisits, path length, heading changes, and CPU latency. Results show that heuristics relying on explicit shortest-path reconnection reliably solve the relaxed coverage task but rarely achieve zero revisits. The best-performing heuristic—a Warnsdorff variant with index-based tie-breaking and terminal-inclusive residual-degree policy—achieves 79.0% Hamiltonian success, highlighting that implementation details, particularly how residual degree is defined when reserving the endpoint, critically influence performance on sparse, bottlenecked graphs.
为满足海上监视、搜救和环境监测中不规则六边形网格路径规划的实际需求，本研究对17种经典启发式算法在10,000个合成海上场景实例上进行基准测试。方法采用统一评估协议，衡量哈密顿路径成功率、全覆盖完成率、重复访问、路径长度、转向次数和CPU延迟。结果表明，依赖显式最短路径重连的启发式算法能可靠解决松弛覆盖任务，但几乎无法实现零重复访问。表现最佳的经典哈密顿启发式算法——一种结合索引式并列处理与终点包含残余度策略的Warnsdorff变体——达成79.0%的成功率。研究指出，实现细节（特别是终点保留时残余度的定义方式）对稀疏瓶颈图的性能影响显著。
RL-STPA: Adapting System-Theoretic Hazard Analysis for Safety-Critical Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Steven A. Senczyszyn, Timothy C. Havens, Nathaniel Rice, Jason E. Summers, Benjamin D. Werner, Benjamin J. Schumeg
First: 2026-04-16T16:27:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T16:27:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As reinforcement learning (RL) deployments expand into safety-critical domains, existing evaluation methods fail to systematically identify hazards arising from the black-box nature of neural network enabled policies and distributional shift between training and deployment. This paper introduces Reinforcement Learning System-Theoretic Process Analysis (RL-STPA), a framework that adapts conventional STPA's systematic hazard analysis to address RL's unique challenges through three key contributions: hierarchical subtask decomposition using both temporal phase analysis and domain expertise to capture emergent behaviors, coverage-guided perturbation testing that explores the sensitivity of state-action spaces, and iterative checkpoints that feed identified hazards back into training through reward shaping and curriculum design. We demonstrate RL-STPA in the safety-critical test case of autonomous drone navigation and landing, revealing potential loss scenarios that can be missed by standard RL evaluations. The proposed framework provides practitioners with a toolkit for systematic hazard analysis, quantitative metrics for safety coverage assessment, and actionable guidelines for establishing operational safety bounds. While RL-STPA cannot provide formal guarantees for arbitrary neural policies, it offers a practical methodology for systematically evaluating and improving RL safety and robustness in safety-critical applications where exhaustive verification methods remain intractable.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RL-STPA：适应系统理论危害分析的安全关键强化学习
随着强化学习（RL）在安全关键领域的部署扩展，现有评估方法无法系统性识别由神经网络策略的黑箱性质及训练与部署间分布偏移所引发的危害。本文提出强化学习系统理论过程分析（RL-STPA），通过三项关键贡献将传统STPA的系统性危害分析框架适配于RL的独特挑战：利用时间阶段分析与领域专业知识进行分层子任务分解以捕捉涌现行为；基于覆盖率引导的扰动测试探索状态-动作空间的敏感性；以及通过奖励塑造与课程设计将识别出的危害反馈至训练过程的迭代检查点。我们在安全关键的自主无人机导航与着陆测试案例中验证了RL-STPA，揭示了标准RL评估可能遗漏的潜在损失场景。该框架为从业者提供了系统性危害分析工具、安全覆盖率的量化评估指标及建立操作安全边界的具体指导。尽管RL-STPA无法为任意神经策略提供形式化保证，但其提供了一种实用方法，用于系统性评估和提升安全关键应用中RL的安全性与鲁棒性，尤其在难以进行穷尽验证的场景下。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing evaluation methods in identifying hazards from black-box RL policies and distributional shifts, this paper proposes RL-STPA, a framework adapting System-Theoretic Process Analysis to RL. It employs hierarchical subtask decomposition, coverage-guided perturbation testing, and iterative checkpoint feedback to systematically analyze safety risks. Experiments in autonomous drone navigation reveal previously undetected loss scenarios, demonstrating RL-STPA’s ability to uncover safety gaps missed by standard RL evaluations and offering practical tools for safety coverage assessment and operational bounds design.
为应对强化学习在安全关键领域部署时现有评估方法的不足，本文提出RL-STPA框架，将系统理论过程分析方法适配于强化学习特有的黑箱策略与训练-部署分布偏移问题。该框架通过结合时序阶段与领域知识的分层子任务分解建模涌现行为，通过覆盖引导的扰动测试探测状态-动作空间敏感性，并通过迭代检查点将识别出的隐患反馈至训练过程，以奖励塑造和课程设计提升安全性。在自主无人机导航实验中，该框架揭示了标准评估方法遗漏的潜在失效场景，为从业者提供了可量化的安全覆盖度量与可操作的安全边界指导，虽无法提供任意神经策略的正式保证，但提供了实用的系统性安全评估与改进方法。
Preconditioned Test-Time Adaptation for Out-of-Distribution Debiasing in Narrative Generation
Authors: Hanwen Shen, Ting Ying, Jiajie Lu, Shanshan Wang
First: 2026-03-14T01:37:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T16:26:44+00:00
Comments: This paper has been accepted to ACL2026 main conference
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Although debiased large language models (LLMs) excel at handling known or low-bias prompts, they often fail on unfamiliar and high-bias prompts. We demonstrate via out-of-distribution (OOD) detection that these high-bias prompts cause a distribution shift, degrading static model performance. To enable real-time correction, we propose CAP-TTA, a test-time adaptation framework. CAP-TTA triggers context-aware LoRA updates only when a bias-risk score exceeds a set threshold. By utilizing an offline precomputed diagonal preconditioner, it ensures fast and stable optimization. Across multiple benchmarks and human evaluations, CAP-TTA effectively reduces toxicity/bias score with significantly lower latency than standard optimization methods (e.g., AdamW or SGD). Furthermore, it prevents catastrophic forgetting, and substantially improves narrative fluency over state-of-the-art baselines without compromising debiasing performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于叙事生成中分布外去偏的预处理测试时自适应
尽管去偏大型语言模型（LLMs）擅长处理已知或低偏倚提示，但在面对陌生且高偏倚提示时往往表现不佳。我们通过分布外（OOD）检测表明，这些高偏倚提示会导致分布偏移，从而降低静态模型性能。为实现实时修正，我们提出CAP-TTA，一种测试时自适应框架。CAP-TTA仅在偏倚风险评分超过设定阈值时触发上下文感知的LoRA更新。通过利用离线预计算的对角预条件子，确保快速且稳定的优化。在多个基准测试和人工评估中，CAP-TTA有效降低毒性/偏倚评分，且延迟显著低于标准优化方法（如AdamW或SGD）。此外，它防止灾难性遗忘，并在不损害去偏性能的前提下，显著提升叙事流畅性，优于现有最先进基线。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that debiased LLMs degrade under high-bias, out-of-distribution prompts, the authors propose CAP-TTA, a test-time adaptation method that applies context-aware LoRA updates only when a bias-risk score surpasses a threshold. Leveraging a precomputed diagonal preconditioner, CAP-TTA enables fast, stable, and low-latency optimization without catastrophic forgetting. Experimental results across benchmarks and human evaluations show CAP-TTA significantly reduces toxicity and bias scores while maintaining or improving narrative fluency compared to state-of-the-art baselines.
鉴于去偏语言模型在高偏见、分布外提示下性能下降，本文提出CAP-TTA方法，在偏见风险评分超过阈值时才触发上下文感知的LoRA更新，并利用预计算的对角预条件子加速稳定优化，实现实时修正且避免灾难性遗忘。实验表明，该方法在多个基准和人工评估中显著降低毒性与偏见评分，延迟远低于AdamW或SGD，同时在不牺牲去偏效果的前提下，叙事流畅度优于现有最先进基线。
Unsupervised Skeleton-Based Action Segmentation via Hierarchical Spatiotemporal Vector Quantization
Authors: Umer Ahmed, Syed Ahmed Mahmood, Fawad Javed Fateh, M. Shaheer Luqman, M. Zeeshan Zia, Quoc-Huy Tran
First: 2026-04-16T16:24:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T16:24:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We propose a novel hierarchical spatiotemporal vector quantization framework for unsupervised skeleton-based temporal action segmentation. We first introduce a hierarchical approach, which includes two consecutive levels of vector quantization. Specifically, the lower level associates skeletons with fine-grained subactions, while the higher level further aggregates subactions into action-level representations. Our hierarchical approach outperforms the non-hierarchical baseline, while primarily exploiting spatial cues by reconstructing input skeletons. Next, we extend our approach by leveraging both spatial and temporal information, yielding a hierarchical spatiotemporal vector quantization scheme. In particular, our hierarchical spatiotemporal approach performs multi-level clustering, while simultaneously recovering input skeletons and their corresponding timestamps. Lastly, extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks, including HuGaDB, LARa, and BABEL, demonstrate that our approach establishes a new state-of-the-art performance and reduces segment length bias in unsupervised skeleton-based temporal action segmentation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于层次时空向量量化无监督骨架动作分割
我们提出了一种新颖的层次时空向量量化框架，用于无监督骨架时序动作分割。首先，我们引入层次化方法，包含两级向量量化：低层将骨架与细粒度子动作关联，高层进一步将子动作聚合为动作级表示。该层次方法优于非层次基线，主要通过重构输入骨架利用空间线索。接着，我们扩展方法以同时利用空间和时间信息，形成层次时空向量量化方案。具体而言，该方法进行多级聚类，同时恢复输入骨架及其对应时间戳。最后，在HuGaDB、LARa和BABEL等多个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，我们的方法在无监督骨架时序动作分割中达到新SOTA性能，并减少分段长度偏差。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for unsupervised temporal action segmentation using skeleton data, this work introduces a hierarchical spatiotemporal vector quantization framework. The method first applies two levels of vector quantization: a lower level clusters skeletons into fine-grained subactions, and a higher level aggregates these into action-level representations. It further extends to incorporate temporal cues by jointly clustering and reconstructing skeletons with their timestamps. Experiments on HuGaDB, LARa, and BABEL show the method achieves state-of-the-art performance and effectively reduces segment length bias compared to non-hierarchical baselines.
为解决骨架数据下的无监督时序动作分割问题，本文提出一种分层时空向量量化框架，先通过空间向量量化对细粒度子动作进行聚类，再将子动作聚合为动作级表示。该方法进一步结合时空线索，通过多级聚类同时重建输入骨架及其时间戳。在HuGaDB、LARa和BABEL等多个基准数据集上的实验表明，该方法达到当前最优性能，并有效缓解了无监督骨架动作分割中的片段长度偏差问题。
Meituan Merchant Business Diagnosis via Policy-Guided Dual-Process User Simulation
Authors: Ziyang Chen, Renbing Chen, Daowei Li, Jinzhi Liao, Jiashen Sun, Ke Zeng, Xiang Zhao
Venue: SIGIR 2026
First: 2026-04-16T16:23:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T16:23:13+00:00
Comments: 5 pages, 3 figures, 2 tables, accepted at SIGIR 2026 Industry Track
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Simulating group-level user behavior enables scalable counterfactual evaluation of merchant strategies without costly online experiments. However, building a trustworthy simulator faces two structural challenges. First, information incompleteness causes reasoning-based simulators to over-rationalize when unobserved factors such as offline context and implicit habits are missing. Second, mechanism duality requires capturing both interpretable preferences and implicit statistical regularities, which no single paradigm achieves alone. We propose Policy-Guided Hybrid Simulation (PGHS), a dual-process framework that mines transferable decision policies from behavioral trajectories and uses them as a shared alignment layer. This layer anchors an LLM-based reasoning branch that prevents over-rationalization and an ML-based fitting branch that absorbs implicit regularities. Group-level predictions from both branches are fused for complementary correction. We deploy PGHS on Meituan with 101 merchants and over 26,000 trajectories. PGHS achieves a group simulation error of 8.80%, improving over the best reasoning-based and fitting-based baselines by 45.8% and 40.9% respectively.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于政策引导双过程用户模拟的美团商户业务诊断
模拟群体级用户行为可实现无需昂贵在线实验的商户策略可扩展反事实评估。然而，构建可信模拟器面临两大结构挑战：其一，信息不完整性导致基于推理的模拟器在缺失线下情境和隐性习惯等未观测因素时过度理性化；其二，机制二元性要求同时捕捉可解释偏好与隐性统计规律，单一范式难以兼顾。我们提出政策引导混合模拟（PGHS），一种双过程框架，从行为轨迹中挖掘可迁移决策策略并作为共享对齐层。该层锚定基于大语言模型的推理分支以防止过度理性化，以及基于机器学习的拟合分支以吸收隐性规律。两个分支的群体级预测进行互补修正。我们在美团部署PGHS，覆盖101家商户及26,000+条轨迹，实现群体模拟误差8.80%，相较最佳推理型与拟合型基线分别提升45.8%和40.9%。
Summary / 总结
To enable scalable counterfactual evaluation of merchant strategies without costly online experiments, the paper addresses two key challenges in user behavior simulation: information incompleteness leading to over-rationalization and the need to capture both interpretable preferences and implicit statistical patterns. It introduces Policy-Guided Hybrid Simulation (PGHS), a dual-process framework that combines an LLM-based reasoning module to prevent over-rationalization and an ML-based fitting module to capture implicit regularities, anchored by transferable decision policies mined from behavioral trajectories. When deployed on Meituan with 101 merchants and over 26,000 trajectories, PGHS achieved a group simulation error of 8.80%, outperforming the best reasoning-based and fitting-based baselines by 45.8% and 40.9% respectively.
为实现无需昂贵在线实验的商户策略可扩展反事实评估，该论文解决了用户行为模拟中的两大挑战：信息不完整导致的过度理性化，以及需同时捕捉可解释偏好与隐式统计规律的问题。提出Policy-Guided Hybrid Simulation（PGHS）双过程框架，通过从行为轨迹中挖掘可迁移决策策略作为统一对齐层，连接LLM推理分支（防止过度理性化）与ML拟合分支（捕捉隐式规律），并融合二者组级预测以互补修正。在美团101家商户、超2.6万条轨迹上评估，PGHS实现组级模拟误差8.80%，相较最优推理型与拟合型基线分别提升45.8%和40.9%。
SegviGen: Repurposing 3D Generative Model for Part Segmentation
Authors: Lin Li, Haoran Feng, Zehuan Huang, Haohua Chen, Wenbo Nie, Shaohua Hou, Keqing Fan, Pan Hu, Sheng Wang, Buyu Li, Lu Sheng
First: 2026-03-17T17:59:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T16:21:22+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://fenghora.github.io/SegviGen-Page/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
We introduce SegviGen, a framework that repurposes native 3D generative models for 3D part segmentation. Existing pipelines either lift strong 2D priors into 3D via distillation or multi-view mask aggregation, often suffering from cross-view inconsistency and blurred boundaries, or explore native 3D discriminative segmentation, which typically requires large-scale annotated 3D data and substantial training resources. In contrast, SegviGen leverages the structured priors encoded in pretrained 3D generative model to induce segmentation through distinctive part colorization, establishing a novel and efficient framework for part segmentation. Specifically, SegviGen encodes a 3D asset and predicts part-indicative colors on active voxels of a geometry-aligned reconstruction. It supports interactive part segmentation, full segmentation, and full segmentation with 2D guidance in a unified framework. Extensive experiments show that SegviGen improves over the prior state of the art by 40% on interactive part segmentation and by 15% on full segmentation, while using only 0.32% of the labeled training data. It demonstrates that pretrained 3D generative priors transfer effectively to 3D part segmentation, enabling strong performance with limited supervision. See our project page at https://fenghora.github.io/SegviGen-Page/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SegviGen：复用3D生成模型进行部件分割
我们提出了SegviGen，一种复用原生3D生成模型进行3D部件分割的框架。现有方法要么通过蒸馏或多视角掩码聚合将强2D先验提升至3D，常受跨视角不一致和边界模糊困扰，要么探索原生3D判别性分割，通常需大量标注3D数据和大量训练资源。相比之下，SegviGen利用预训练3D生成模型中编码的结构化先验，通过独特的部件着色诱导分割，建立了一种新颖高效的部件分割框架。具体而言，SegviGen对3D资产进行编码，并在几何对齐重建的活跃体素上预测部件指示性颜色。它在一个统一框架内支持交互式部件分割、全分割及2D引导全分割。大量实验表明，SegviGen在交互式部件分割上比先前最先进方法提升40%，在全分割上提升15%，且仅使用0.32%的标注训练数据。实验证明，预训练3D生成先验可有效迁移到3D部件分割，实现有限监督下的强性能。项目页面见：https://fenghora.github.io/SegviGen-Page/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing 3D part segmentation methods—either relying on 2D priors with cross-view inconsistencies or requiring large annotated 3D datasets—SegviGen repurposes pretrained 3D generative models to induce segmentation via part-indicative colorization on geometry-aligned reconstructions. It predicts distinctive colors on active voxels to segment parts interactively, fully, or with 2D guidance, all within a unified framework. Experiments show it outperforms prior state-of-the-art by 40% on interactive segmentation and 15% on full segmentation, using only 0.32% of labeled training data, demonstrating effective transfer of generative priors to segmentation tasks with minimal supervision.
为解决现有3D部件分割方法存在的跨视图不一致或需大量标注数据的问题，SegviGen利用预训练3D生成模型中编码的结构先验，通过几何对齐重建体素上的部件指示性着色实现分割。该框架支持交互式、全部件及2D引导分割，统一实现。实验表明，其在交互式分割上比现有最佳方法提升40%，全分割提升15%，仅需0.32%的标注训练数据，证明了生成先验可有效迁移到分割任务中，且在弱监督下仍能取得强性能。
VisPCO: Visual Token Pruning Configuration Optimization via Budget-Aware Pareto-Frontier Learning for Vision-Language Models
Authors: Huawei Ji, Yuanhao Sun, Yuan Jin, Cheng Deng, Jiaxin Ding, Luoyi Fu, Xinbing Wang
First: 2026-04-16T16:21:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T16:21:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Visual token pruning methods effectively mitigate the quadratic computational growth caused by processing high-resolution images and video frames in vision-language models (VLMs). However, existing approaches rely on predefined pruning configurations without determining whether they achieve computation-performance optimality. In this work, we introduce , a novel framework that formulates visual token pruning as a Pareto configuration optimization problem to automatically identify optimal configurations. Our approach employs continuous relaxation and straight-through estimators to enable gradient-based search, solved via the Augmented Lagrangian method. Extensive experiments across 8 visual benchmarks demonstrate that effectively approximates the empirical Pareto frontier obtained through grid search and generalizes well across various pruning methods and VLM architectures. Furthermore, through learnable kernel functions, we investigate layer-wise pruning patterns and reveal that multi-step progressive pruning captures VLMs' hierarchical compression structure, achieving superior accuracy-efficiency trade-offs compared to single-layer approaches.
中文标题/摘要
标题：VisPCO：通过预算感知的帕累托前沿学习优化视觉令牌剪枝配置
视觉令牌剪枝方法有效缓解了视觉-语言模型（VLMs）处理高分辨率图像和视频帧时引发的二次计算增长。然而，现有方法依赖预定义剪枝配置，未验证其是否达到计算-性能最优。本文提出VisPCO框架，将视觉令牌剪枝建模为帕累托配置优化问题，自动识别最优配置。该方法采用连续松弛与直通估计器实现梯度搜索，通过增广拉格朗日法求解。在8个视觉基准上的大量实验表明，VisPCO有效逼近网格搜索获得的经验帕累托前沿，并在多种剪枝方法和VLM架构中具有良好泛化能力。此外，通过可学习核函数，我们研究层间剪枝模式，发现多步渐进剪枝能捕捉VLMs的层次压缩结构，在准确率-效率权衡上优于单层方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to optimize computational efficiency in vision-language models without sacrificing performance, this work introduces VisPCO, a framework that treats visual token pruning as a Pareto configuration optimization problem. It employs continuous relaxation and straight-through estimators to enable gradient-based search via the Augmented Lagrangian method, allowing automatic discovery of optimal pruning configurations. Experiments across eight visual benchmarks show VisPCO closely approximates the empirical Pareto frontier from grid search and generalizes across pruning methods and model architectures. Additionally, using learnable kernel functions, it reveals that multi-step progressive pruning aligns with VLMs' hierarchical structure, yielding better accuracy-efficiency trade-offs than single-layer pruning.
为优化视觉语言模型在处理高分辨率图像时的计算效率与性能平衡，本文提出VisPCO框架，将视觉标记剪枝建模为帕累托配置优化问题。通过连续松弛与直通估计器结合增广拉格朗日方法，实现基于梯度的自动配置搜索。在八个视觉基准测试中，VisPCO能逼近网格搜索所得的帕累托前沿，并在不同剪枝方法与模型架构间具有良好泛化能力。进一步通过可学习核函数发现，分层渐进式剪枝更契合视觉语言模型的层次压缩结构，相较单层剪枝方法，实现更优的精度-效率权衡。
XRZero-G0: Pushing the Frontier of Dexterous Robotic Manipulation with Interfaces, Quality and Ratios
Authors: James Wang, Primo Pu, Zephyr Fung, Alex Wang, Sam Wang, Bender Deng, Kevin Wang, Zivid Liu, Chris Pan, Panda Yang, Andy Zhai, Lucy Liang, Shalfun Li, Johnny Sun, Jacky Xu, Will Tian, Kai Yan, Kohler Ye, Scott Li, Qian Wang, Roy Gan, Hao Wang
First: 2026-04-14T17:34:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T16:19:28+00:00
Comments: Technical Report
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
The acquisition of high-quality, action-aligned demonstration data remains a fundamental bottleneck in scaling foundation models for dexterous robot manipulation. Although robot-free human demonstrations (e.g., the UMI paradigm) offer a scalable alternative to traditional teleoperation, current systems are constrained by sub-optimal hardware ergonomics, open-loop workflows, and a lack of systematic data-mixing strategies. To address these limitations, we present XRZero-G0, a hardware-software co-designed system for embodied data collection and policy learning. The system features an ergonomic, virtual reality interface equipped with a top-view camera and dual specialized grippers to directly improve collection efficiency. To ensure dataset reliability, we propose a closed-loop collection, inspection, training, and evaluation pipeline for non-proprioceptive data. This workflow achieves an 85% data validity rate and establishes a transparent mechanism for quality control. Furthermore, we investigate the empirical scaling behaviors and optimal mixing ratios of robot-free data. Extensive experiments indicate that combining a minimal volume of real-robot data with large-scale robot-free data (e.g., a 10:1 ratio) achieves performance comparable to exclusively real-robot datasets, while reducing acquisition costs by a factor of twenty. Utilizing XRZero-G0, we construct a 2,000-hour robot-free dataset that enables zero-shot cross-embodiment transfer to a target physical robot, demonstrating a highly scalable methodology for generalized real-world manipulation.Our project repository: https://github.com/X-Square-Robot/XRZero-G0
中文标题/摘要
标题：XRZero-G0：通过接口、质量和比例推动灵巧机器人操作的前沿
获取高质量、动作对齐的演示数据仍是扩展灵巧机器人操作基础模型的根本瓶颈。尽管机器人免操作的人类演示（如UMI范式）为传统遥操作提供了可扩展的替代方案，但当前系统受限于次优的硬件人体工学、开环工作流程及缺乏系统性的数据混合策略。为解决这些局限，我们提出了XRZero-G0，一个软硬件协同设计的具身数据采集与策略学习系统。该系统配备人体工学优化的虚拟现实界面，搭载俯视摄像头和双专用夹爪，直接提升数据采集效率。为确保数据可靠性，我们提出了一套闭环的数据采集、检查、训练与评估流程，适用于非本体感觉数据。该流程实现85%的数据有效性，并建立透明的质量控制机制。此外，我们研究了机器人免操作数据的经验扩展行为与最优混合比例。大量实验表明，将少量真实机器人数据与大规模机器人免操作数据（如10:1比例）结合，可实现与纯真实机器人数据集相当的性能，同时将采集成本降低20倍。利用XRZero-G0，我们构建了一个2000小时的机器人免操作数据集，实现了零样本跨具身迁移至目标物理机器人，展示了一种高度可扩展的通用现实世界操作方法。项目仓库：https://github.com/X-Square-Robot/XRZero-G0
Summary / 总结
To overcome the bottleneck of acquiring high-quality, action-aligned demonstration data for dexterous robot manipulation, the authors introduce XRZero-G0, a hardware-software co-designed system that combines an ergonomic VR interface with specialized grippers and a closed-loop data pipeline. This system enables efficient, real-time data collection while ensuring data validity through automated inspection and training feedback, achieving an 85% reliability rate. Experimental results show that mixing a small amount of real-robot data with large-scale robot-free data—such as a 10:1 ratio—yields performance comparable to pure real-robot datasets while reducing acquisition costs by 20x. Using XRZero-G0, the team built a 2,000-hour robot-free dataset that enabled zero-shot cross-embodiment transfer to a physical robot, demonstrating a scalable approach to generalized manipulation.
为解决高质动作对齐演示数据获取困难的问题，作者提出了XRZero-G0系统，该系统软硬件协同设计，配备人体工学VR界面、双专用夹爪和俯视摄像头，以提升数据采集效率。系统采用闭环流程对非本体感觉数据进行采集、检查、训练与评估，实现85%的数据有效性并支持透明的质量控制。实验表明，将少量真实机器人数据与大规模机器人免操作数据（如10:1比例）混合，可获得与纯真实数据相当的性能，同时采集成本降低20倍。利用该系统构建的2000小时免操作数据集，实现了对目标物理机器人的零样本跨具身迁移，验证了该方法在通用现实操作中的可扩展性。
Scepsy: Serving Agentic Workflows Using Aggregate LLM Pipelines
Authors: Marcel Wagenländer, Otto White, Britannio Jarrett, Pedro Silvestre, Yanda Tao, Guo Li, Huanzhou Zhu, Llúis Vilanova, Peter Pietzuch
First: 2026-04-16T16:15:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T16:15:29+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Agentic workflows carry out complex tasks by orchestrating multiple large language models (LLMs) and tools. Serving such workflows at a target throughput with low latency is challenging because they can be defined using arbitrary agentic frameworks and exhibit unpredictable execution times: execution may branch, fan-out, or recur in data-dependent ways. Since LLMs in workflows often outnumber available GPUs, their execution also leads to GPU oversubscription. We describe Scepsy, a new agentic serving system that efficiently schedules arbitrary multi-LLM agentic workflows onto a GPU cluster. Scepsy exploits the insight that, while agentic workflows have unpredictable end-to-end latencies, the shares of each LLM's total execution times are comparatively stable across executions. Scepsy decides on GPU allocations based on these aggregate shares: first, it profiles the LLMs under different parallelism degrees. It then uses these statistics to construct an Aggregate LLM Pipeline, which is a lightweight latency/throughput predictor for allocations. To find a GPU allocation that minimizes latency while achieving a target throughput, Scepsy uses the Aggregate LLM Pipeline to explore a search space over fractional GPU shares, tensor parallelism degrees, and replica counts. It uses a hierarchical heuristic to place the best allocation onto the GPU cluster, minimizing fragmentation, while respecting network topology constraints. Our evaluation on realistic agentic workflows shows that Scepsy achieves up to 2.4x higher throughput and 27x lower latency compared to systems that optimize LLMs independently or rely on user-specified allocations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Scepsy：使用聚合大语言模型流水线服务代理工作流
代理工作流通过编排多个大语言模型（LLM）和工具执行复杂任务。在目标吞吐量下以低延迟服务此类工作流具有挑战性，因为它们可使用任意代理框架定义，且执行时间不可预测：执行可能因数据依赖而分支、扇出或递归。由于工作流中的LLM数量常多于可用GPU，其执行还会导致GPU过载。 我们介绍了Scepsy，一种新的代理服务系统，可高效地将任意多LLM代理工作流调度到GPU集群上。Scepsy利用了这样一个洞察：虽然代理工作流的端到端延迟不可预测，但每个LLM总执行时间的占比在不同执行间相对稳定。Scepsy基于这些聚合占比决定GPU分配：首先，它在不同并行度下对LLM进行性能剖析；然后，利用这些统计数据构建一个聚合LLM流水线，作为轻量级的延迟/吞吐量预测器用于分配决策。为找到在满足目标吞吐量的同时最小化延迟的GPU分配，Scepsy使用聚合LLM流水线在分数GPU份额、张量并行度和副本数的搜索空间中进行探索。它采用分层启发式方法将最优分配部署到GPU集群，最小化碎片化，同时尊重网络拓扑约束。我们在真实代理工作流上的评估表明，与独立优化LLM或依赖用户指定分配的系统相比，Scepsy可实现最高2.4倍的吞吐量和27倍的延迟降低。
Summary / 总结
Scepsy addresses the challenge of efficiently serving complex agentic workflows that involve multiple LLMs and tools with unpredictable execution patterns and GPU oversubscription. It leverages the observation that while individual workflow latencies vary, the relative execution time shares of each LLM remain stable across runs. Scepsy first profiles LLMs under varying parallelism levels to build an Aggregate LLM Pipeline—a lightweight predictor for latency and throughput. It then searches over fractional GPU shares, tensor parallelism, and replica counts to find optimal allocations that meet target throughput while minimizing latency, using a hierarchical heuristic to avoid GPU fragmentation and respect network topology. Experimental results on real agentic workflows show Scepsy delivers up to 2.4x higher throughput and 27x lower latency than systems that allocate GPUs independently or rely on user-defined settings.
Scepsy 解决了复杂代理工作流（协调多个大语言模型和工具）在 GPU 集群上高效服务的挑战，这类工作流执行时间不可预测且易导致 GPU 过载。其核心洞察是：尽管工作流端到端延迟波动大，但各 LLM 的总执行时间占比在不同运行中相对稳定。Scepsy 首先在不同并行度下对 LLM 进行采样，构建轻量级的聚合 LLM 管道（用于预测延迟与吞吐量），再在 GPU 分配比例、张量并行度和副本数空间中搜索最优配置，以在满足吞吐量目标的同时最小化延迟。采用分层启发式算法部署最优分配，同时尊重网络拓扑约束。在真实代理工作流上的实验表明，Scepsy 相比独立优化 LLM 或依赖用户手动分配的系统，吞吐量最高提升 2.4 倍，延迟降低 27 倍。
Agent-Aided Design for Dynamic CAD Models
Authors: Mitch Adler, Matthew Russo, Michael Cafarella
First: 2026-04-16T16:15:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T16:15:23+00:00
Comments: 6 pages, 3 figures, to be published in CAIS'26
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In the past year, researchers have started to create agentic systems that can design real-world CAD-style objects in a training-free setting, a new variety of system that we call Agent-Aided Design. Generally speaking, these systems place an agent in a feedback loop in which it can write code, compile that code to an assembly of CAD model(s), visualize the model, and then iteratively refine its code based on visual and other feedback. Despite rapid progress, a key problem remains: none of these systems can build complex 3D assemblies with moving parts. For example, no existing system can build a piston, a pendulum, or even a pair of scissors. In order for Agent-Aided Design to make a real impact in industrial manufacturing, we need a system that is capable of generating such 3D assemblies. In this paper we present a prototype of AADvark, an agentic system designed for this task. Unlike previous state-of-the-art systems, AADvark captures the dynamic part interactions with one or more degrees-of-freedom. This design decision allows AADvark to reason directly about assemblies with moving parts and can thereby achieve cross-cutting goals, including but not limited to mechanical movements. Unfortunately, current LLMs are imperfect spatial reasoners, a problem that AADvark addresses by incorporating external constraint solver tools with a specialized visual feedback mechanism. We demonstrate that, by modifying the agent's tools (FreeCAD and the assembly solver), we are able to create a strong verification signal which enables our system to build 3D assemblies with movable parts.
中文标题/摘要
标题：代理辅助的动态CAD模型设计
过去一年中，研究人员开始构建能够在无训练环境下设计真实世界CAD风格物体的代理系统，我们称之为代理辅助设计。这类系统通常将代理置于一个反馈循环中，使其能够编写代码、编译为CAD模型、可视化模型，并根据视觉及其他反馈迭代优化代码。尽管进展迅速，关键问题依然存在：现有系统均无法构建具有活动部件的复杂3D装配体，例如活塞、摆锤或剪刀。为使代理辅助设计在工业制造中产生实际影响，我们需要能够生成此类3D装配体的系统。本文提出AADvark原型系统，专为此任务设计。与先前最先进的系统不同，AADvark通过一个或多个自由度捕捉动态部件间的相互作用，使其能够直接推理具有活动部件的装配体，并实现包括但不限于机械运动在内的跨领域目标。不幸的是，当前大语言模型在空间推理方面存在缺陷，AADvark通过集成外部约束求解器工具及专用视觉反馈机制加以解决。我们证明，通过修改代理工具（FreeCAD和装配求解器），我们能够生成强验证信号，使系统成功构建具有活动部件的3D装配体。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for agentic systems to generate complex 3D assemblies with moving parts—such as pistons or scissors—this paper introduces AADvark, a new agent-aided design system that explicitly models dynamic interactions through one or more degrees-of-freedom. Unlike prior systems, AADvark integrates external constraint solvers and a specialized visual feedback mechanism to compensate for the spatial reasoning limitations of current LLMs. Experimental results show that by modifying the agent’s tools (FreeCAD and an assembly solver), AADvark successfully generates functional 3D assemblies with movable components, demonstrating its capability to achieve cross-cutting mechanical goals.
为解决现有代理系统无法设计含活动部件的复杂3D装配体（如活塞或剪刀）的问题，本文提出AADvark系统，该系统通过建模一个或多个自由度的动态部件交互，首次实现对运动装配体的直接推理。为弥补当前大语言模型在空间推理上的不足，AADvark结合外部约束求解器与专用视觉反馈机制。实验表明，通过调整代理工具（FreeCAD与装配求解器），系统可成功生成具备可动部件的3D装配体，验证其在实现机械运动等跨领域目标上的能力。
Exact Structural Abstraction and Tractability Limits
Authors: Tristan Simas
First: 2026-04-08T17:59:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T16:15:00+00:00
Comments: 50 pages. 7 tables. Lean 4 formalization available at https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.19457896
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Any rigorously specified problem determines an admissible-output relation $R$, and exact correctness depends only on the induced classes $s \sim_R s' \iff \mathrm{Adm}_R(s)=\mathrm{Adm}_R(s')$. Exact relevance certification asks which coordinates recover those classes. Decision, search, approximation, statistical, randomized, horizon, and distributional guarantees all reduce to this same quotient-recovery problem. Tractable cases still admit a finite primitive basis, but optimizer-quotient realizability is maximal, so quotient shape alone cannot mark the frontier. For frontier theorems, orbit gaps are the exact obstruction. Exact classification by closure-law-invariant predicates succeeds exactly when the target is constant on closure orbits; on a closure-closed domain, this is equivalent to disjointness of the positive and negative orbit hulls, and when it holds there is a least exact closure-invariant classifier. Across four natural candidate tractability predicates, a uniform pair-targeted affine witness produces same-orbit disagreements and rules out exact structural classification on the full binary pairwise domain. Because the canonical optimizer-set exact specifications of that witness class are already rigorously specified problems, no universal exact-certification characterization over formal problems escapes the same obstruction; this is by internal witness class, not by asserting that every problem is binary pairwise. Restricting the domain helps only by removing orbit gaps. Approximation also has a strict limit: without explicit gap control, arbitrarily small perturbations can flip relevance and sufficiency.
中文标题/摘要
标题：精确结构抽象与可处理性界限
任何严格定义的问题都会确定一个可接受输出关系 $R$，精确正确性仅取决于由此诱导的类 $s \sim_R s' \iff \mathrm{Adm}_R(s)=\mathrm{Adm}_R(s')$。精确相关性认证询问哪些坐标能恢复这些类。决策、搜索、近似、统计、随机化、时间范围和分布保证都归结为这一相同的商空间恢复问题。可处理情形仍允许有限的原始基，但优化器-商的可实现性是最大的，因此仅凭商的形状无法标定前沿。 对于前沿定理，轨道间隙是精确的障碍。由闭包律不变谓词进行的精确分类仅在目标在闭包轨道上恒定的情况下成功；在闭包封闭域上，这等价于正负轨道包的不相交性，且当其成立时存在最小的精确闭包不变分类器。在四个自然候选的可处理性谓词中，统一的成对目标仿射见证会产生同轨道分歧，并排除在完整二元成对域上进行精确结构分类的可能性。由于该见证类的规范优化器集精确规范本身已是严格定义的问题，因此不存在超越相同障碍的通用精确认证特征化；这是由内部见证类决定的，而非断言每个问题都是二元成对的。限制域仅能通过消除轨道间隙来帮助。近似也有严格限制：没有显式间隙控制时，任意微小扰动都可能翻转相关性和充分性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to characterize when exact structural abstraction is tractable, the paper investigates the quotient-recovery problem underlying various computational guarantees. It introduces orbit gaps as the precise obstruction to exact classification, showing that closure-law-invariant predicates succeed only when positive and negative orbit hulls are disjoint, and then constructs a least exact classifier under such conditions. Experimental analysis across four natural tractability predicates reveals that a uniform affine witness induces orbit disagreements, ruling out exact structural classification on the full binary pairwise domain. The paper further demonstrates that even with canonical optimizer-set specifications, no universal exact-certification characterization escapes this obstruction, and domain restriction or approximation without explicit gap control cannot overcome the inherent limits.
该论文旨在解决形式化问题中精确结构抽象的核心挑战，动机在于在精确正确性标准下验证坐标的相关性。作者提出了一种商结构恢复框架，其中可计算性取决于恢复由可接受输出关系诱导的等价类。研究指出，尽管可计算情形存在有限原始基，但精确分类器的实现能力已达极限，因此商结构本身不足以标识计算前沿。轨道间隙被确定为精确分类的精确障碍，且闭包律不变谓词仅在正负轨道包不相交时才能成功实现分类。在四个自然候选可计算性谓词上，统一的仿射见证者诱导轨道分歧，排除了在完整二元成对域上的精确结构分类。即使使用标准优化器集合精确规范，也不存在能超越此障碍的通用精确认证刻画，且域限制或无间隙控制的近似方法均无法克服这一固有局限。
One-shot learning for the complex dynamical behaviors of weakly nonlinear forced oscillators
Authors: Teng Ma, Luca Rosafalco, Wei Cui, Lin Zhao, Attilio Frangi
First: 2026-04-16T16:10:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T16:10:44+00:00
Comments: 48 pages, 16 figures, graphical abstract, highlights
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Extrapolative prediction of complex nonlinear dynamics remains a central challenge in engineering. This study proposes a one-shot learning method to identify global frequency-response curves from a single excitation time history by learning governing equations. We introduce MEv-SINDy (Multi-frequency Evolutionary Sparse Identification of Nonlinear Dynamics) to infer the governing equations of non-autonomous and multi-frequency systems. The methodology leverages the Generalized Harmonic Balance (GHB) method to decompose complex forced responses into a set of slow-varying evolution equations. We validated the capabilities of MEv-SINDy on two critical Micro-Electro-Mechanical Systems (MEMS). These applications include a nonlinear beam resonator and a MEMS micromirror. Our results show that the model trained on a single point accurately predicts softening/hardening effects and jump phenomena across a wide range of excitation levels. This approach significantly reduces the data acquisition burden for the characterization and design of nonlinear microsystems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：弱非线性受迫振子复杂动力学行为的一次学习方法
复杂非线性动力学的外推预测仍是工程领域的核心挑战。本研究提出了一种一次学习方法，通过学习控制方程，从单个激励时程中识别全局频率响应曲线。我们引入了MEv-SINDy（多频演化稀疏非线性动力学识别）方法，用于推断非自治和多频系统的控制方程。该方法利用广义谐波平衡（GHB）方法，将复杂的受迫响应分解为一组慢变演化方程。我们在两个关键的微机电系统（MEMS）中验证了MEv-SINDy的能力，包括非线性梁谐振器和MEMS微镜。结果表明，仅用单个点训练的模型能准确预测软化/硬化效应和跳跃现象，适用于广泛的激励水平。该方法显著降低了非线性微系统的表征与设计所需的数据采集负担。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of predicting complex nonlinear dynamics with minimal data, this study introduces MEv-SINDy, a one-shot learning method that infers governing equations from a single excitation time history. The method combines Generalized Harmonic Balance to decompose forced responses into slow-evolving equations, then applies sparse identification to extract the underlying dynamics. Tested on two MEMS systems—a nonlinear beam resonator and a MEMS micromirror—MEv-SINDy accurately captured softening/hardening behaviors and jump phenomena across varying excitation levels using only one data point, drastically reducing the need for extensive experimental data.
为应对复杂非线性动力学预测中数据需求高的挑战，该研究提出MEv-SINDy方法，通过单一激励时程数据推断系统控制方程。该方法结合广义谐波平衡分解强迫响应为慢变演化方程，并采用稀疏识别提取动力学规律。在非线性梁谐振器和MEMS微镜两个系统上验证，仅用一个数据点即可准确捕捉软化/硬化效应与跳跃现象，覆盖多激励水平，显著降低实验数据采集负担。
Quantitative Approximation Rates for Group Equivariant Learning
Authors: Jonathan W. Siegel, Snir Hordan, Hannah Lawrence, Ali Syed, Nadav Dym
First: 2026-02-23T21:17:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T16:10:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The universal approximation theorem establishes that neural networks can approximate any continuous function on a compact set. Later works in approximation theory provide quantitative approximation rates for ReLU networks on the class of $α$-Hölder functions $f: [0,1]^N \to \mathbb{R}$. The goal of this paper is to provide similar quantitative approximation results in the context of group equivariant learning, where the learned $α$-Hölder function is known to obey certain group symmetries. While there has been much interest in the literature in understanding the universal approximation properties of equivariant models, very few quantitative approximation results are known for equivariant models. In this paper, we bridge this gap by deriving quantitative approximation rates for several prominent group-equivariant and invariant architectures. The architectures that we consider include: the permutation-invariant Deep Sets architecture; the permutation-equivariant Sumformer and Transformer architectures; joint invariance to permutations and rigid motions using invariant networks based on frame averaging; and general bi-Lipschitz invariant models. Overall, we show that equally-sized ReLU MLPs and equivariant architectures are equally expressive over equivariant functions. Thus, hard-coding equivariance does not result in a loss of expressivity or approximation power in these models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：群等变学习的定量逼近速率
通用逼近定理确立了神经网络可逼近紧集上任意连续函数。后续逼近理论工作为ReLU网络在α-Holder函数类f: [0,1]^N → R上提供了定量逼近速率。本文旨在为群等变学习背景下提供类似定量逼近结果，其中所学习的α-Holder函数已知满足特定群对称性。尽管文献中对等变模型的通用逼近性质已有大量研究，但对等变模型的定量逼近结果却鲜有报道。 本文通过推导若干主流群等变与不变架构的定量逼近速率，弥合这一差距。所考虑的架构包括：置换不变的Deep Sets架构；置换等变的Sumformer与Transformer架构；基于框架平均的不变网络，实现置换与刚体运动的联合不变性；以及一般的双Lipschitz不变模型。总体而言，我们证明了同等规模的ReLU MLP与等变架构在等变函数上具有同等表达能力。因此，在这些模型中硬编码等变性不会导致表达能力或逼近能力的损失。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to quantify approximation capabilities of group-equivariant neural networks, this paper derives explicit approximation rates for several equivariant architectures under the assumption that target functions are α-Hölder continuous and obey group symmetries. The method involves analyzing the expressive power of models such as Deep Sets, Sumformer, Transformer, frame-averaged invariant networks, and bi-Lipschitz invariant models, comparing their approximation performance against standard ReLU MLPs. Experimental results show that for equivariant functions, models with hard-coded symmetries achieve the same approximation rates as non-equivariant MLPs of equal size, indicating that enforcing equivariance does not reduce expressivity or approximation power.
为量化群等变神经网络的逼近能力，本文在目标函数满足α-Holder连续性且具有群对称性前提下，推导了多种等变架构（如Deep Sets、Sumformer、Transformer、帧平均不变网络及双Lipschitz不变模型）的显式逼近速率。方法通过分析这些架构与同等规模标准ReLU MLP在等变函数上的表达能力对比。实验结果表明，对等变函数而言，硬编码对称性的模型与同等规模的非等变MLP具有相同的逼近能力，说明强制等变性不会降低表达能力或逼近性能。
Reference-Free Sampling-Based Model Predictive Control
Authors: Fabian Schramm, Pierre Fabre, Nicolas Perrin-Gilbert, Justin Carpentier
Venue: ICRA
First: 2025-11-24T15:15:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T16:03:40+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the 2026 IEEE International Conference on Robotics and Automation (ICRA), Vienna, Austria
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present a sampling-based model predictive control (MPC) framework that enables emergent locomotion without relying on handcrafted gait patterns or predefined contact sequences. Our method discovers diverse motion patterns, ranging from trotting to galloping, robust standing policies, jumping, and handstand balancing, purely through the optimization of high-level objectives. Building on model predictive path integral (MPPI), we propose a cubic Hermite spline parameterization that operates on position and velocity control points. Our approach enables contact-making and contact-breaking strategies that adapt automatically to task requirements, requiring only a limited number of sampled trajectories. This sample efficiency enables real-time control on standard CPU hardware, eliminating the GPU acceleration typically required by other state-of-the-art MPPI methods. We validate our approach on the Go2 quadrupedal robot, demonstrating a range of emergent gaits and basic jumping capabilities. In simulation, we further showcase more complex behaviors, such as backflips, dynamic handstand balancing and locomotion on a Humanoid, all without requiring reference tracking or offline pre-training.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无需参考的采样基模型预测控制
我们提出了一种基于采样的模型预测控制（MPC）框架，能够在不依赖手工设计的步态模式或预定义接触序列的情况下实现涌现式运动。该方法通过优化高层目标，发现多样化的运动模式，包括小跑、快跑、稳健站立策略、跳跃和倒立平衡。基于模型预测路径积分（MPPI），我们提出了一种三次Hermite样条参数化方法，作用于位置和速度控制点。该方法能够自动适应任务需求，实现接触建立与接触解除策略，仅需少量采样轨迹即可完成。这种采样效率使得该方法可在标准CPU硬件上实现实时控制，无需其他先进MPPI方法通常所需的GPU加速。我们在Go2四足机器人上验证了该方法，展示了多种涌现步态和基础跳跃能力。在仿真中，我们进一步展示了更复杂的动作，如后空翻、动态倒立平衡和人形机器人的运动，且无需参考轨迹跟踪或离线预训练。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for autonomous locomotion without predefined gaits, this paper introduces a reference-free sampling-based MPC framework that discovers diverse motion patterns—such as trotting, galloping, jumping, and handstand balancing—through high-level optimization. Building on MPPI, it employs a cubic Hermite spline parameterization for position and velocity control points, enabling adaptive contact strategies with minimal trajectory sampling. Experiments on the Go2 quadruped show real-time performance on CPU hardware and demonstrate emergent gaits; simulation further reveals complex behaviors like backflips and humanoid handstand balancing without reference tracking or pre-training.
为实现无需预设步态的自主运动，本文提出一种无参考的采样基模型预测控制框架，通过优化高层目标自动发现包括小跑、快跑、跳跃、倒立平衡等多样运动模式。基于MPPI方法，采用三次Hermite样条参数化位置与速度控制点，使接触策略能自适应任务需求，且仅需少量采样轨迹即可实现高效控制。该方法可在标准CPU上实时运行，无需GPU加速；在Go2四足机器人及仿真环境中验证了其能力，展现出包括后空翻、人形倒立平衡等复杂行为，无需参考轨迹或离线训练。
AdaSplash-2: Faster Differentiable Sparse Attention
Authors: Nuno Gonçalves, Hugo Pitorro, Vlad Niculae, Edoardo Ponti, Lei Li, Andre Martins, Marcos Treviso
First: 2026-04-16T16:03:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T16:03:13+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Sparse attention has been proposed as a way to alleviate the quadratic cost of transformers, a central bottleneck in long-context training. A promising line of work is $α$-entmax attention, a differentiable sparse alternative to softmax that enables input-dependent sparsity yet has lagged behind softmax due to the computational overhead necessary to compute the normalizer $τ$. In this paper, we introduce AdaSplash-2, which addresses this limitation through a novel histogram-based initialization that reduces the number of iterations needed to compute $τ$ to typically 1--2. The key idea is to compute a coarse histogram of attention scores on the fly and store it in on-chip SRAM, yielding a more accurate initialization that enables fast forward and backward computation. Combined with a sparsity-aware GPU implementation that skips zero blocks with low overhead, AdaSplash-2 matches or improves per-step training time relative to FlashAttention-2 when block sparsity is moderate-to-high (e.g., $>$60\%), which often occurs at long-context lengths. On downstream tasks, models trained with our efficient $α$-entmax attention match softmax baselines at short-context lengths and achieve substantial gains in long-context settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AdaSplash-2：更快的可微稀疏注意力
稀疏注意力被提出以缓解Transformer中二次成本的核心瓶颈，特别是在长上下文训练中。α-entmax注意力是一种可微稀疏替代softmax的方法，能够实现输入依赖的稀疏性，但由于计算归一化因子τ所需的计算开销，其性能一直落后于softmax。本文提出了AdaSplash-2，通过一种新颖的基于直方图的初始化方法，将计算τ所需的迭代次数通常降至1-2次。其核心思想是在运行时计算注意力分数的粗略直方图并存储在片上SRAM中，从而获得更精确的初始化，实现快速的前向和反向计算。结合一种感知稀疏性的GPU实现（低开销跳过零块），当块稀疏度中等至较高（例如>60%）时，AdaSplash-2在训练每步时间上可匹配或优于FlashAttention-2，而这种情况在长上下文长度下常出现。在下游任务中，使用我们高效α-entmax注意力训练的模型在短上下文长度下可匹配softmax基线，在长上下文设置下则能实现显著性能提升。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational bottleneck of quadratic attention cost in transformers, especially at long contexts, this paper introduces AdaSplash-2, a differentiable sparse attention mechanism based on α-entmax that reduces the overhead of computing the normalizer τ. It achieves this by using a histogram-based initialization stored in on-chip SRAM to approximate τ with just 1–2 iterations, enabling fast forward and backward passes. When combined with a GPU implementation that skips zero blocks, AdaSplash-2 matches or outperforms FlashAttention-2 in training speed for moderate-to-high block sparsity (e.g., >60%), which is common in long-context scenarios. On downstream tasks, models using AdaSplash-2 match softmax performance at short contexts and show significant gains at long contexts.
为缓解长上下文Transformer中注意力计算的二次成本瓶颈，本文提出AdaSplash-2，一种基于α-entmax的可微稀疏注意力机制，通过新颖的直方图初始化方法（存储于片上SRAM）将归一化项τ的计算迭代次数降至通常1–2次。结合GPU上跳过零块的低开销实现，AdaSplash-2在中高块稀疏度（如>60%）下训练速度可匹配或超越FlashAttention-2，此类稀疏度常见于长上下文场景。在下游任务中，使用该机制训练的模型在短上下文下性能与softmax基线相当，在长上下文下表现显著提升。
OccuBench: Evaluating AI Agents on Real-World Professional Tasks via Language Environment Simulation
Authors: Xiaomeng Hu, Yinger Zhang, Fei Huang, Jianhong Tu, Yang Su, Lianghao Deng, Yuxuan Liu, Yantao Liu, Dayiheng Liu, Tsung-Yi Ho
First: 2026-04-13T00:27:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T16:00:33+00:00
Comments: 23 pages, 8 figures, 2 tables. Project page: https://gregxmhu.github.io/OccuBench-website/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
AI agents are expected to perform professional work across hundreds of occupational domains (from emergency department triage to nuclear reactor safety monitoring to customs import processing), yet existing benchmarks can only evaluate agents in the few domains where public environments exist. We introduce OccuBench, a benchmark covering 100 real-world professional task scenarios across 10 industry categories and 65 specialized domains, enabled by Language Environment Simulators (LESs) that simulate domain-specific environments through LLM-driven tool response generation. Our multi-agent synthesis pipeline automatically produces evaluation instances with guaranteed solvability, calibrated difficulty, and document-grounded diversity. OccuBench evaluates agents along two complementary dimensions: task completion across professional domains and environmental robustness under controlled fault injection (explicit errors, implicit data degradation, and mixed faults). We evaluate 15 frontier models across 8 model families and find that: (1) no single model dominates all industries, as each has a distinct occupational capability profile; (2) implicit faults (truncated data, missing fields) are harder than both explicit errors (timeouts, 500s) and mixed faults, because they lack overt error signals and require the agent to independently detect data degradation; (3) larger models, newer generations, and higher reasoning effort consistently improve performance. GPT-5.2 improves by 27.5 points from minimal to maximum reasoning effort; and (4) strong agents are not necessarily strong environment simulators. Simulator quality is critical for LES-based evaluation reliability. OccuBench provides the first systematic cross-industry evaluation of AI agents on professional occupational tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：OccuBench：通过语言环境模拟评估AI代理在现实专业任务中的表现
AI代理预期能在数百个职业领域（从急诊分诊到核反应堆安全监控再到海关进口处理）执行专业工作，但现有基准仅能评估那些拥有公开环境的少数领域。我们引入OccuBench，涵盖100个真实世界专业任务场景，覆盖10个行业类别和65个专业领域，该基准通过语言环境模拟器（LES）实现，利用大语言模型驱动工具响应生成以模拟特定领域环境。我们的多代理合成管道可自动产生具有可解性保障、难度校准和文档基础多样性的评估实例。OccuBench从两个互补维度评估代理：跨专业领域的任务完成度，以及在受控故障注入（显式错误、隐式数据退化和混合故障）下的环境鲁棒性。我们在8个模型家族中评估了15个前沿模型，发现：（1）无单一模型主导所有行业，因各行业具有独特的职业能力分布；（2）隐式故障（截断数据、缺失字段）比显式错误（超时、500错误）和混合故障更难，因其缺乏明显错误信号，需代理自主检测数据退化；（3）更大模型、更新代际和更高推理投入持续提升性能，GPT-5.2在最小至最大推理投入下性能提升27.5分；（4）强代理未必是强环境模拟器，模拟器质量对基于LES的评估可靠性至关重要。OccuBench首次系统性地对AI代理在专业职业任务中的跨行业表现进行评估。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate AI agents across diverse real-world professional domains beyond those with public environments, the authors introduce OccuBench, a benchmark covering 100 occupational scenarios across 10 industries and 65 domains, simulated via Language Environment Simulators (LESs) that generate tool responses using LLMs. Their multi-agent synthesis pipeline ensures solvable, calibrated, and diverse evaluation instances. Experimental results show no single model dominates all industries, implicit data faults (like truncation or missing fields) are hardest to handle due to lack of error signals, larger and newer models with higher reasoning effort perform better, and GPT-5.2 improves by 27.5 points with increased reasoning effort—yet strong agents do not necessarily simulate environments well, underscoring simulator quality as critical for reliable evaluation.
为评估AI代理在缺乏公开环境的多样化真实职业任务中的表现，作者构建了OccuBench基准，涵盖100个跨10行业、65个细分领域的任务场景，通过语言环境模拟器（LES）利用大语言模型生成工具响应。其多代理合成流程确保任务可解、难度校准和多样性。实验表明：无单一模型在所有行业表现最优，隐式数据退化故障最难处理（因缺乏明确错误信号），模型规模越大、更新越新、推理投入越高，性能越优，且模拟器质量对评估可靠性至关重要。
MambaSL: Exploring Single-Layer Mamba for Time Series Classification
Authors: Yoo-Min Jung, Leekyung Kim
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2026-04-16T15:51:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T15:51:16+00:00
Comments: accepted at ICLR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Despite recent advances in state space models (SSMs) such as Mamba across various sequence domains, research on their standalone capacity for time series classification (TSC) has remained limited. We propose MambaSL, a framework that minimally redesigns the selective SSM and projection layers of a single-layer Mamba, guided by four TSC-specific hypotheses. To address benchmarking limitations -- restricted configurations, partial University of East Anglia (UEA) dataset coverage, and insufficiently reproducible setups -- we re-evaluate 20 strong baselines across all 30 UEA datasets under a unified protocol. As a result, MambaSL achieves state-of-the-art performance with statistically significant average improvements, while ensuring reproducibility via public checkpoints for all evaluated models. Together with visualizations, these results demonstrate the potential of Mamba-based architectures as a TSC backbone.
中文标题/摘要

To ensure fair and reproducible benchmarking, they re-evaluate 20 strong baselines across all 30 UEA datasets under a unified protocol, addressing prior limitations in configuration scope and dataset coverage. MambaSL achieves state-of-the-art performance with statistically significant average improvements over baselines and is accompanied by public checkpoints to ensure reproducibility, demonstrating Mamba’s potential as a powerful TSC backbone.

Efficient Vector Symbolic Architectures from Histogram Recovery
Authors: Zirui Deng, Netanel Raviv
First: 2025-11-03T18:45:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T15:50:45+00:00
Comments: To appear at ISIT 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vector symbolic architectures (VSAs) are a family of information representation techniques which enable composition, i.e., creating complex information structures from atomic vectors via binding and superposition, and have recently found wide ranging applications in various neurosymbolic artificial intelligence (AI) systems and hardware systems. Recently, Raviv proposed the use of random linear codes in VSAs, suggesting that their subcode structure enables efficient unbinding, while preserving the quasi-orthogonality that is necessary for neural processing. Yet, random linear codes are difficult to decode under noise, which severely limits the resulting VSA's ability to support recovery, i.e., the retrieval of information objects and their attributes from a noisy compositional representation. In this work we bridge this gap by utilizing coding theoretic tools. First, we argue that the concatenation of Reed-Solomon and Hadamard codes is suitable for VSA, due to the mutual quasi-orthogonality of the resulting codewords (a folklore result). Second, we show that recovery of the resulting compositional representations can be done by solving a problem we call histogram recovery. In histogram recovery, a collection of $N$ histograms over a finite field is given as input, and one must find a collection of Reed-Solomon codewords of length $N$ whose entry-wise symbol frequencies obey those histograms. We present an optimal solution to the histogram recovery problem by using algorithms related to list-decoding, and analyze the resulting noise resilience. Our results give rise to a noise-resilient VSA with formal guarantees regarding efficient encoding, quasi-orthogonality, and recovery, without relying on any heuristics or training, and while operating at improved parameters relative to similar solutions such as the Hadamard code.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从直方图恢复中获得高效向量符号架构
向量符号架构（VSAs）是一类信息表示技术，支持组合操作，即通过绑定和叠加原子向量创建复杂信息结构，近年来在各类神经符号人工智能（AI）系统和硬件系统中得到广泛应用。最近，Raviv 提出在 VSAs 中使用随机线性码，认为其子码结构可实现高效解绑，同时保持神经处理所需的准正交性。然而，随机线性码在噪声下难以解码，严重限制了由此产生的 VSA 支持恢复能力，即从噪声组合表示中检索信息对象及其属性。本文通过编码理论工具弥合这一差距：首先，我们论证 Reed-Solomon 与 Hadamard 码的级联结构适合 VSA，因其生成码字间具有相互准正交性（一个常识性结果）；其次，我们证明可通过对直方图恢复问题求解来恢复所得组合表示。在直方图恢复中，输入为有限域上 N 个直方图，需找到长度为 N 的 Reed-Solomon 码字集合，使其逐元素符号频率符合这些直方图。我们通过与列表解码相关的算法给出直方图恢复问题的最优解，并分析其噪声鲁棒性。结果产生一种具有形式化保证的噪声鲁棒 VSA，确保高效编码、准正交性与恢复能力，无需任何启发式或训练，且参数优于类似方案（如 Hadamard 码）。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for noise-resilient vector symbolic architectures (VSAs) that maintain quasi-orthogonality and efficient unbinding, this work proposes a VSA based on concatenating Reed-Solomon and Hadamard codes, leveraging their mutual quasi-orthogonality. The method frames recovery as a 'histogram recovery' problem: given symbol frequency histograms over a finite field, it finds Reed-Solomon codewords whose symbol counts match the histograms, solved via list-decoding algorithms. Experimental analysis demonstrates that this approach yields a VSA with formal guarantees for efficient encoding, quasi-orthogonality, and robust recovery under noise, outperforming prior methods like Hadamard codes in parameter efficiency without requiring training or heuristics.
为解决向量符号架构（VSA）在噪声环境下难以恢复信息的问题，本文提出使用Reed-Solomon码与Hadamard码级联构造鲁棒VSA。该方法利用码字间准正交性，并将恢复问题建模为“直方图恢复”：给定有限域上符号频率的直方图集合，需找到匹配频率的Reed-Solomon码字。通过基于列表解码的最优算法求解该问题，所构建的VSA在噪声下仍能高效编码、保持准正交性并可靠恢复信息，无需启发式方法或训练，且参数优于Hadamard码等现有方案。
Boundary-Centric Active Learning for Temporal Action Segmentation
Authors: Halil Ismail Helvaci, Sen-ching Samson Cheung
First: 2026-04-16T15:50:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T15:50:21+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Temporal action segmentation (TAS) demands dense temporal supervision, yet most of the annotation cost in untrimmed videos is spent identifying and refining action transitions, where segmentation errors concentrate and small temporal shifts disproportionately degrade segmental metrics. We introduce B-ACT, a clip-budgeted active learning framework that explicitly allocates supervision to these high-leverage boundary regions. B-ACT operates in a hierarchical two-stage loop: (i) it ranks and queries unlabeled videos using predictive uncertainty, and (ii) within each selected video, it detects candidate transitions from the current model predictions and selects the top-$K$ boundaries via a novel boundary score that fuses neighborhood uncertainty, class ambiguity, and temporal predictive dynamics. Importantly, our annotation protocol requests labels for only the boundary frames while still training on boundary-centered clips to exploit temporal context through the model's receptive field. Extensive experiments on GTEA, 50Salads, and Breakfast demonstrate that boundary-centric supervision delivers strong label efficiency and consistently surpasses representative TAS active learning baselines and prior state of the art under sparse budgets, with the largest gains on datasets where boundary placement dominates edit and overlap-based F1 scores.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向时序动作分割的边界为中心的主动学习
时序动作分割（TAS）需要密集的时序监督，但未剪辑视频中大部分标注成本用于识别和优化动作转换点，而分割错误主要集中于此，微小的时序偏移会不成比例地降低分段指标。我们提出了B-ACT，一种基于片段预算的主动学习框架，明确将监督分配给这些高价值的边界区域。B-ACT采用分层两阶段循环：(i) 利用预测不确定性对未标注视频进行排序和查询；(ii) 在每个选定视频中，根据当前模型预测检测候选转换点，并通过融合邻域不确定性、类别模糊性和时序预测动态的新型边界得分选择前K个边界。重要的是，我们的标注协议仅要求标注边界帧，同时以边界为中心的片段进行训练，以利用模型感受野内的时序上下文。在GTEA、50Salads和Breakfast数据集上的大量实验表明，边界为中心的监督显著提升标注效率，并在稀疏预算下持续超越代表性TAS主动学习基线和先前最先进方法，尤其在边界定位主导编辑和重叠F1分数的数据集上收益最大。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high annotation cost and error concentration around action boundaries in temporal action segmentation, the paper proposes B-ACT, a clip-budgeted active learning framework that prioritizes labeling only boundary frames while training on boundary-centered clips to leverage temporal context. The method operates in two stages: first, it selects unlabeled videos based on predictive uncertainty; second, within each video, it identifies candidate transitions and selects top-K boundaries using a novel score combining neighborhood uncertainty, class ambiguity, and temporal dynamics. Experiments on GTEA, 50Salads, and Breakfast show B-ACT achieves superior label efficiency and outperforms existing active learning baselines and prior state-of-the-art under sparse budgets, especially on datasets where boundary accuracy dominates F1 scores.
为降低时序动作分割中边界区域标注成本并提升标注效率，本文提出B-ACT框架，该框架仅对边界帧进行标注，同时以边界为中心的片段进行训练以利用时序上下文。方法分两阶段：首先根据预测不确定性筛选未标注视频；其次在每个视频内检测候选边界，通过融合邻域不确定性、类别模糊性和时序动态的新型评分机制选择Top-K边界。在GTEA、50Salads和Breakfast三个数据集上的实验表明，B-ACT在标注预算稀疏条件下显著优于现有主动学习基线和最先进方法，尤其在边界定位主导F1分数的数据集上收益最大。
An Analysis of Regularization and Fokker-Planck Residuals in Diffusion Models for Image Generation
Authors: Onno Niemann, Gonzalo Martínez Muñoz, Alberto Suárez Gonzalez
First: 2026-04-16T15:48:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T15:48:40+00:00
Comments: Accepted at IJCNN 2026 IEEE. Personal use of this material is permitted. Permission from IEEE must be obtained for all other uses, in any current or future media, including reprinting/republishing this material for advertising or promotional purposes, creating new collective works, for resale or redistribution to servers or lists, or reuse of any copyrighted component of this work in other works
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Recent work has shown that diffusion models trained with the denoising score matching (DSM) objective often violate the Fokker--Planck (FP) equation that governs the evolution of the true data density. Directly penalizing these deviations in the objective function reduces their magnitude but introduces a significant computational overhead. It is also observed that enforcing strict adherence to the FP equation does not necessarily lead to improvements in the quality of the generated samples, as often the best results are obtained with weaker FP regularization. In this paper, we investigate whether simpler penalty terms can provide similar benefits. We empirically analyze several lightweight regularizers, study their effect on FP residuals and generation quality, and show that the benefits of FP regularization are available at substantially lower computational cost. Our code is available at https://github.com/OnnoNiemann/fp_diffusion_analysis.
中文标题/摘要
标题：扩散模型图像生成中正则化与福克-普朗克残差的分析
近期研究表明，使用去噪得分匹配（DSM）目标训练的扩散模型常违反支配真实数据密度演化的福克-普朗克（FP）方程。直接在目标函数中惩罚这些偏差可降低其幅度，但会引入显著的计算开销。同时观察到，强制严格遵守FP方程并不必然提升生成样本质量，因为通常较弱的FP正则化反而能获得最佳结果。本文探讨是否更简单的惩罚项可提供类似收益。我们实证分析了若干轻量级正则器，研究其对FP残差和生成质量的影响，并表明FP正则化的益处可在大幅降低计算成本下实现。代码见 https://github.com/OnnoNiemann/fp_diffusion_analysis。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that diffusion models trained with denoising score matching often deviate from the Fokker-Planck equation governing true data density, this paper explores whether lightweight regularization can reduce these deviations without the high computational cost of strict enforcement. The authors empirically evaluate several simple penalty terms, measuring their impact on Fokker-Planck residuals and generated image quality. Results show that even weak regularization can yield significant improvements in sample quality while substantially lowering computational overhead, suggesting that strong FP adherence is not necessary for high performance.
鉴于使用去噪得分匹配训练的扩散模型常偏离描述真实数据密度演化的Fokker-Planck方程，本文研究是否能通过更轻量级的正则化项实现类似效果，同时避免严格约束带来的高计算开销。作者实证评估了多种轻量正则化方法，测量其对FP残差和生成图像质量的影响。结果表明，即使采用较弱的FP正则化，也能显著提升样本质量，且计算成本远低于严格FP约束方案。
One Token per Highly Selective Frame: Towards Extreme Compression for Long Video Understanding
Authors: Zheyu Zhang, Ziqi Pang, Shixing Chen, Xiang Hao, Vimal Bhat, Yu-Xiong Wang
Venue: NeurIPS 2025
First: 2026-04-15T17:59:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T15:48:38+00:00
Comments: Appear in the proceedings of NeurIPS 2025
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Long video understanding is inherently challenging for vision-language models (VLMs) because of the extensive number of frames. With each video frame typically expanding into tens or hundreds of tokens, the limited context length of large language models (LLMs) forces the VLMs to perceive the frames sparsely and lose temporal information. To address this, we explore extreme video token compression towards one token per frame at the final LLM layer. Our key insight is that heuristic-based compression, widely adopted by previous methods, is prone to information loss, and this necessitates supervising LLM layers into learnable and progressive modules for token-level compression (LP-Comp). Such compression enables our VLM to digest 2x-4x more frames with improved performance. To further increase the token efficiency, we investigate frame-level compression, which selects the frames most relevant to the queries via the internal attention scores of the LLM layers, named question-conditioned compression (QC-Comp). As a notable distinction from previous studies, we mitigate the position bias of LLM attention in long contexts, i.e., the over-concentration on the beginning and end of a sequence, by splitting long videos into short segments and employing local attention. Collectively, our combined token-level and frame-level leads to an extreme compression model for long video understanding, named XComp, achieving a significantly larger compression ratio and enabling denser frame sampling. Our XComp is finetuned from VideoChat-Flash with a data-efficient supervised compression tuning stage that only requires 2.5% of the supervised fine-tuning data, yet boosts the accuracy from 42.9% to 46.2% on LVBench and enhances multiple other long video benchmarks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：每个高选择性帧一个标记：面向长视频理解的极压缩方法
长视频理解对视觉-语言模型（VLMs）而言固有挑战，因视频帧数量庞大。每个视频帧通常扩展为数十至数百个标记，而大语言模型（LLMs）有限的上下文长度迫使VLMs稀疏感知帧并丢失时序信息。为此，我们探索在最终LLM层实现每帧一个标记的极端视频标记压缩。核心洞察是：此前广泛采用的启发式压缩易导致信息丢失，因此需将LLM层监督为可学习且渐进的标记级压缩模块（LP-Comp）。该压缩使VLM能处理2至4倍更多帧且性能提升。为进一步提升标记效率，我们研究帧级压缩，通过LLM层内部注意力分数选择与查询最相关的帧，称为问题条件压缩（QC-Comp）。与以往研究不同，我们通过将长视频分割为短片段并采用局部注意力，缓解LLM在长上下文中的位置偏差（即对序列首尾过度集中）。综合标记级与帧级压缩，我们提出名为XComp的极端压缩模型，实现显著更高的压缩比并支持更密集的帧采样。XComp基于VideoChat-Flash微调，仅需监督微调数据的2.5%，即可将LVBench准确率从42.9%提升至46.2%，并在多个其他长视频基准测试中表现更优。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of long video understanding in vision-language models where excessive frame tokens overwhelm LLM context limits, the paper proposes XComp, a method that compresses video representation to one token per frame via learnable token-level compression (LP-Comp) and question-conditioned frame-level compression (QC-Comp). LP-Comp replaces heuristic compression with trainable modules to preserve temporal information, while QC-Comp selects frames based on LLM attention scores to align with query relevance. To counter position bias in long sequences, the method splits videos into segments and uses local attention. Experiments show XComp achieves 2x–4x more frames processed per token with improved performance, boosting accuracy from 42.9% to 46.2% on LVBench using only 2.5% of the supervised fine-tuning data.
为解决视觉语言模型在长视频理解中因帧数过多导致的计算开销大和时序信息丢失问题，本文提出XComp模型，通过可学习的逐帧压缩（LP-Comp）和基于问题的帧选择压缩（QC-Comp）实现极端压缩，每帧仅保留一个token。LP-Comp训练LLM层逐步压缩token以保留语义，使模型处理帧数提升2–4倍且性能更优；QC-Comp依据LLM注意力分数选择与问题最相关的帧；局部注意力机制缓解长序列中注意力位置偏差。在仅使用2.5%监督微调数据的情况下，XComp从VideoChat-Flash微调后，在LVBench上准确率从42.9%提升至46.2%，并在多个长视频基准测试中表现更优。
OmniLight: One Model to Rule All Lighting Conditions
Authors: Youngjin Oh, Junyoung Park, Junhyeong Kwon, Nam Ik Cho
First: 2026-04-16T15:47:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T15:47:49+00:00
Comments: CVPRW 2026; NTIRE 2026 Image Shadow Removal & Ambient Lighting Normalization Challenges (1st Perceptual Rank for White Lighting, 2nd Fidelity Rank & 4th Perceptual Rank for Color Lighting)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Adverse lighting conditions, such as cast shadows and irregular illumination, pose significant challenges to computer vision systems by degrading visibility and color fidelity. Consequently, effective shadow removal and ALN are critical for restoring underlying image content, improving perceptual quality, and facilitating robust performance in downstream tasks. However, while achieving state-of-the-art results on specific benchmarks is a primary goal in image restoration challenges, real-world applications often demand robust models capable of handling diverse domains. To address this, we present a comprehensive study on lighting-related image restoration by exploring two contrasting strategies. We leverage a robust framework for ALN, DINOLight, as a specialized baseline to exploit the characteristics of each individual dataset, and extend it to OmniLight, a generalized alternative incorporating our proposed Wavelet Domain Mixture-of-Experts (WD-MoE) that is trained across all provided datasets. Through a comparative analysis of these two methods, we discuss the impact of data distribution on the performance of specialized and unified architectures in lighting-related image restoration. Notably, both approaches secured top-tier rankings across all three lighting-related tracks in the NTIRE 2026 Challenge, demonstrating their outstanding perceptual quality and generalization capabilities. Our codes are available at https://github.com/OBAKSA/Lighting-Restoration.
中文标题/摘要
标题：OmniLight：一个统一模型应对所有光照条件

Motivated by the challenge of degraded visibility and color fidelity under adverse lighting conditions, this work introduces OmniLight, a unified model designed to handle diverse lighting scenarios. It builds upon DINOLight, a specialized ALN baseline, and extends it with a Wavelet Domain Mixture-of-Experts (WD-MoE) architecture trained across multiple datasets to enhance generalization. Experimental results from the NTIRE 2026 Challenge show that both the specialized and unified approaches achieved top rankings across all three lighting tracks, with OmniLight demonstrating strong perceptual quality and robust performance across varied domains.
为应对不良光照下图像可见性与色彩保真度下降的问题，本文提出两种图像光照修复策略：一种是针对单个数据集优化的专用基线模型DINOLight，另一种是跨数据集训练的通用模型OmniLight，其引入了小波域混合专家模块以增强泛化能力。实验表明，两种方法在NTIRE 2026三项光照相关任务中均取得顶尖排名，OmniLight尤其在多光照场景下展现出卓越的感知质量与鲁棒性，验证了统一架构在实际应用中的有效性。
Assessing the Potential of Masked Autoencoder Foundation Models in Predicting Downhole Metrics from Surface Drilling Data
Authors: Aleksander Berezowski, Hassan Hassanzadeh, Gouri Ginde
First: 2026-04-16T15:47:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-16T15:47:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Oil and gas drilling operations generate extensive time-series data from surface sensors, yet accurate real-time prediction of critical downhole metrics remains challenging due to the scarcity of labelled downhole measurements. This systematic mapping study reviews thirteen papers published between 2015 and 2025 to assess the potential of Masked Autoencoder Foundation Models (MAEFMs) for predicting downhole metrics from surface drilling data. The review identifies eight commonly collected surface metrics and seven target downhole metrics. Current approaches predominantly employ neural network architectures such as artificial neural networks (ANNs) and long short-term memory (LSTM) networks, yet no studies have explored MAEFMs despite their demonstrated effectiveness in time-series modeling. MAEFMs offer distinct advantages through self-supervised pre-training on abundant unlabeled data, enabling multi-task prediction and improved generalization across wells. This research establishes that MAEFMs represent a technically feasible but unexplored opportunity for drilling analytics, recommending future empirical validation of their performance against existing models and exploration of their broader applicability in oil and gas operations.
